- `zoom-viewer/` (plan v3 2.23d, Jakub 2026-10-31): `<app-zoomable [content]>` wraps a photo or a QR code in a button named "Zvětšit fotku: <description>" ("Zvětšit fotku" without one) or "Zvětšit: <QR label>" (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `cursor: zoom-in`, no other visible change) that opens `<app-zoom-viewer [content] (closed)>`: an `<app-dialog presentation="viewer">` (full screen at every width on `--color-surface-inverse`, the × and the tools in `--color-on-inverse`, safe areas kept) holding the content as `ZoomContent` (`zoom-content.ts`: `{ kind: 'photo', src, alt, width?, height? }`, the largest stored width, uncropped, or `{ kind: 'qr', matrix, label }`, drawn again by `app-qr-code`) fitted to the stage (a size container, `touch-action: none`). The viewer zooms itself, because the page cannot (Accessibility, `frontend.md`): two fingers pinch around their middle and pan, a double tap or double click zooms to 250 % at the point and back, the wheel (and a trackpad pinch, which Chrome sends as a wheel with Ctrl) zooms under the pointer without scrolling the page, `+`/`=`, `-`, `0` and the arrows act on the focused stage (`role="group"`, named by the hidden title "Zvětšená fotka" or "Zvětšený QR kód" and described by a hidden hint), and the buttons Oddálit, the level ("150 %", which returns to 100 %) and Přiblížit, each step announced through `DialogAnnouncer` ("Přiblíženo na 150 %"). At 100 % one finger swipes it away as in Photos (#382): the content follows the finger (sideways too), the dark background fades through the dialog's `[pulledAway]`, and a release that `SheetDrag` reads as closing closes the viewer as × does, while a short or upward swipe eases back; zoomed in, the finger pans instead, a second finger turns the swipe into a pinch, and a mouse never swipes. From 100 % to 400 %; dragging stops at the content's edge (`zoom-geometry.ts`: `zoomAround`, `panBy`, `clampView`, `zoomStep`, the content's own layout size, so a transform never feeds back). Today it wraps the photo of a news or project page (`content-article`), the About photo (`about`) and the QR code of Přispět (`payment-panel`); the QR codes inside dialogs (the 1 Kč add-account payment, the second-factor recovery) are not wrapped, since a dialog over a dialog waits on `DialogHistory`'s stack (2.18 / 2.24).

## Layout

`chunk-load-notice/`: rendered by `app.ts` after the `router-outlet` while `ChunkLoadRecovery.failure()` is set, with the text "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít. Web byl mezitím možná aktualizován nebo vypadlo připojení. Zkuste ji načíst znovu." When the first page failed (`initial`; the router has activated nothing, so no shell is on screen), a page of its own: `main#obsah.ui-panel-page` with a `.ui-panel`, the `h1` "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít" (focused; the tab title becomes "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít | Nová naděje – nadační fond" until the router sets the next page's title), the rest of the text as the lead, the button "Načíst znovu" and the link "Přejít na úvod" (`href="/"`, a full load). After a later navigation, a bar over the page (`.chunk-load-bar.ui-notice--alert.ui-notice--floating`, fixed at the bottom, at most `--content-max` wide) whose button "Načíst znovu" takes the focus and is `aria-describedby` the message, so the message is announced once, with the focus move; the bar is no `role="alert"`, because an alert would announce the same text again and a screen reader that cuts speech short on the focus move could drop it; when the focus cannot reach the button (an open modal `dialog` makes the page behind it inert and leaves it out of the accessibility tree, so no region of the bar would be announced either), the bar hands the message, read from its own rendered text so the one i18n id `layout.chunk-load.message` serves both, to `DialogAnnouncer`, whose region inside the open dialog announces it, and withdraws it when the bar goes; the notice itself renders no status region, so a page's own status region stays the only one; while it shows, `:root` carries `has-floating-notice` and `--floating-notice-height` (measured, kept by a `ResizeObserver`; the themes set it to `0rem`), which add that room below the page (the public footer's own `padding-bottom`, so its colour reaches the bar, or `body`'s on a page without it) and to `scroll-padding-bottom`, and a `focusin` listener scrolls a control the bar would hide above it in the next animation frame, after a left field has added its error line (Chromium does not scroll an element that is already in the viewport behind a fixed bar), leaving controls in an open `dialog` alone, so the bar never covers the end of the page or the focus (WCAG 2.4.11). That room-keeping lives in `shared/ui/floating-notice/floating-notice-room.ts` (`keepRoomForFloatingNotice`), shared with `signed-out-notice/`: rendered by `public-shell/` after the footer (where signing out lands; outside the donor and administration shells, whose pages keep their own status regions), an always rendered `role="status"` region that, while `core/auth/signed-out-notice.ts` (`SignedOutNotice`, root; `show(reason)`, `signed-out` or `device-data-deleted`) is shown, holds a `.ui-notice--status.ui-notice--floating` bar "Odhlásili jste se." with "Zavřít" (focus to `main#obsah`); the account sheet's "Odhlásit se" shows it once the navigation to `/` has finished, and the next navigation, a new sign-in or "Zavřít" takes it away.

`offline-status/` (plan v3 2.7): rendered by `app.ts` after the update notice. An always rendered polite region (`aria-live="polite"`, no `role="status"`, through `DialogAnnouncer` while a dialog is open) says "Jste offline. Zobrazujeme údaje z 12:30.", "Server fondu teď neodpovídá." and "Připojení je zpět."; while `Connectivity.offline()` a pill button fixed at the bottom centre ("Offline · údaje z 12:30", or "Offline", in the warning colours, above the floating notice through `.has-floating-notice`) keeps room for itself (`offline-pill-room.ts`: `html.is-offline` and its measured height in `--offline-pill-height`, `0rem` in the theme, so `scroll-padding-bottom`, the bottom padding of the page or the footer and the sticky `.ui-action-bar`, raised above it, keep content and the focused control clear of it) and opens a sheet (`app-dialog presentation="sheet"`): "Jste offline" with "Bez připojení můžete jen prohlížet. Zkontrolujte Wi-Fi nebo mobilní data." or "Server neodpovídá" with "Připojení k internetu máte, ale server fondu teď neodpovídá. Zkoušíme to znovu sami.", the age of the data ("Zobrazujeme údaje z …, nemusí být aktuální.") with what waits ("Odeslat zprávu, přihlásit se nebo cokoli změnit půjde, až se připojíte.", or "… až server zase odpoví.") and "Zkusit znovu" (`Connectivity.tryAgain()`; while it tries `aria-disabled`, so the focus stays, "Zkoušíme se připojit…" or "Zkoušíme spojení se serverem…", then in its status line "Pořád se nedaří připojit. Za chvíli to zkusíme znovu sami." or "Server pořád neodpovídá. …" in the warning colour, or "Připojení je zpět." with a "Zavřít" button in its place that takes the focus); while the sheet is open only its own status line speaks, while another dialog is open the announcement goes through `DialogAnnouncer`; texts under `layout.offline.*`. A dialog whose opener is gone when it closes (here the pill) returns the focus to `main#obsah`.

`update-notice/` (plan v3 2.3): rendered by `app.ts` after the chunk-load notice; an always rendered polite live region (`aria-live="polite"`, `aria-atomic="true"`, deliberately no `role="status"`, so a page's own status region stays the only one, as with the chunk-load notice, and the specs and journeys that read the page's status keep finding exactly one) that, while `ServerVersion.mustUpdate()` or `AppUpdates.mustReload()` and neither the chunk-load notice (`ChunkLoadRecovery.failure()`) nor the signed-out notice is shown, holds a bar `.ui-notice--warning.ui-notice--floating` with "Web má novou verzi. Než budete cokoli odesílat, načtěte ji." (`layout.update.message`) and the button "Načíst novou verzi" (`layout.update.load`, `aria-describedby` the message), which loads the current page in the newest build (`AppUpdates.loadNewVersion(router.url)`). It never takes the focus; when a modal `dialog` is open as it appears it hands its text to `DialogAnnouncer`. It keeps room below the page with `keepRoomForFloatingNotice`, as the other floating bars do, and waits while one of them is shown, so two bars never lie on each other. `shared/ui/floating-notice/floating-notice-room.ts` keeps a list of the bars on screen per document and keeps room for the latest, so when one bar hands over to another (this one to the chunk-load bar, the signed-out bar to this one) the page keeps its room until the last bar goes.

App shell of plan v3 2.23 (the decisions of 2.1, variant E V1): every public page and the donor area share one header and, at every width, one floating tab bar, and the donor area keeps its side panel on wide screens. `app-header/` (`app-header`): a `header` (the `banner`, `tabindex="-1"` without an outline) laid over the top of the page (`position: absolute`, the safe area added at the top, and its row aligned with `.ui-container`: `--space-8` at the sides, `--space-5` up to `tablet`, or the side safe area when larger), so it scrolls away with the page and has no bar of its own behind it: on the left the logo to `/` (`/images/logo-on-dark.svg` over a photo or a dark head, `/images/logo.svg` over a light one, chosen by the route's `head`), on the right, signed out, "Přihlásit se" (opens the sign-in; after it the focus moves to the button "Účet" that took its place; the logo may shrink and "Přihlásit se" keeps one line in `--text-header-action`, `--text-sm` capped by the viewport, so at 200 % text on a 320 px phone and at the largest iPhone text the row still fits and the header keeps its height) and, signed in, a round button named "Účet" (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) that shows the portrait of an administrator linked to a person of the foundation (`account-sheet/account-portrait.ts`, `AccountPortrait`: `PeopleApi.mine()` asked once per signed-in administrator, kept by the donor's id, so the portrait of one account never shows for the next one signed in on the same tab, `?w=480`, the smallest variant; an error keeps the initials), otherwise the initials of the name (`core/auth/initials.ts`: the first letters of the first and the last word, `aria-hidden`, while a visually hidden "Účet" names the button) or a person icon without a name, and opens the account sheet; a count the account holds for the donor (`core/layout/account-badges.ts`, `AccountBadges`: the donor area puts there the bank accounts with a question about an older symbol, the count the side panel shows, and clears it when the area closes) stands at the button's corner, `aria-hidden`, and its description ("1 otázka čeká na odpověď") joins the button's name, except on the page that holds it, and the sheet's row of that section shows the same count. A page deeper than a tab root shows the fixed round back button "Zpět" (`aria-label`, the glass of the tab bar) before the logo, at the left edge of the content column; on wide screens the donor area leaves it out (`backOnWide: false`), because its side panel leads around. In the donor area and the administration the back button stands alone, without the logo (`logoBesideBack: false`, Jakub 2026-11-05: the two clashed side by side), and the account button keeps to the right; a public page keeps the logo beside it, and a wide screen, without the back button, keeps the logo. Once the page has scrolled, an edge at the top covers what scrolls under the back button, as the scroll edge effect of iOS does, so no line of text runs under it (`app-header/scroll-edge.ts`, `followScrollEdge`, started by the header: the header's `::before` on a page with the back button, `--header-height` and the top safe area opaque, then fading out over `--scroll-edge-fade`, in the background colour of the content just under its lower edge (`--scroll-edge-tone`, read with `elementsFromPoint` in the next animation frame after each scroll or resize, so the dark head and the white body each run on under it), pointer-transparent, a pseudo-element outside the accessibility tree, solid with a hairline under `prefers-contrast: more`, and hidden at the top of the page, so an unscrolled page looks as before); the global `scroll-padding-top` adds the top safe area, so a focused control lands below the edge (#352). `account-sheet/` (`app-account-sheet`, inside `<app-dialog>`, `presentation` `sheet` while `CompactLayout.isCompact()`, a centred dialog otherwise): the head with the portrait or the initials, the `h2` with the donor's name ("Účet" without one) and "Variabilní symbol …", a `nav` of `ui-list` rows (Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, Bankovní účty, Údaje a zabezpečení, and for an administrator "Správa" with the badge "jen správci"), "Přidat na plochu" while `AppInstall.way()` offers a way (Jakub 2026-10-31 13:38: it stays in the sheet whatever "Teď ne" on the install card decided; with the browser's prompt the row closes the sheet and asks the browser, on an iPhone or in Safari on a Mac it unfolds the install card's instruction, `aria-expanded`) and "Odhlásit se" (`ui-button--danger-soft`, "Odhlašujeme…" while it runs; then `/` and the signed-out notice), and under it, because the phone footer stands only on O nás (Jakub 2026-10-31 14:59), `info@nfnn.cz`, "Kontakt" (`/o-nas#kontakt`), the registry line with the IČO and "Zásady ochrany osobních údajů" (the footer's i18n ids). The row of the section open carries `aria-current="page"`. A row or link hands the sheet's history entry over to the page it opens (`DialogHistory.handOver`, which answers whether the sheet owned an entry) and navigates with `replaceUrl` and the next app depth, so Back from that page returns where the sheet was opened; without an owned entry it navigates as a new entry, and to the address already open it only closes. The header closes the sheet when the session ends. `tab-bar/` (`app-tab-bar`): the `nav` "Hlavní sekce" with four links in a grid, O nás `/o-nas`, Aktuality `/aktuality`, Pomáháme `/projekty`, Přispět `/prispet` (`core/navigation/app-tabs.ts`, `APP_TABS`, `tabOf(url)` matches a tab's path and everything under it), each an inline SVG icon (O nás the outline of the logo's star) over its label; the current tab carries `aria-current="page"` at its root and `"true"` deeper in it, the pill (`--color-tab-pill`, its rim `--color-tab-pill-rim`: none in `nova-nadeje`, so the pill fills the bar up to `--tab-pill-inset` as an iOS tab bar does, and a tap just inside the bar's edge still lands on a tab), a bold label and a thicker stroke, so it is told apart by shape and weight, not by colour alone; hidden (`visibility: hidden`, out of the accessibility tree, its room kept) while the on-screen keyboard is open (`SoftKeyboard.isOpen()`, #359), as the keyboard covers a native tab bar; fixed at the bottom, centred, at most `--tab-bar-max-width` wide, `--tab-bar-gap-browser` above the bottom in a browser and, in an installed app (`display-mode: standalone`), the larger of `--tab-bar-gap` and the safe area less `--tab-bar-home-indicator-overlap`; its columns are as wide as their labels need (a label breaks only between words), and when the bar is narrower than four labels need at the root text size (`bp.container-down(tab-bar, tabs)`, 13 rem) it lays the tabs out two by two, so at 200 % text it grows instead of breaking a word; `tab-bar-room.ts` (`keepRoomForTabBar`) writes the bar's measured height into `--tab-bar-rendered-height` on the root (a `ResizeObserver`; the latest bar shown wins, the theme's `--tab-bar-height` when none), which `--tab-bar-clearance` is computed from; the page keeps `--tab-bar-clearance` free under its content (`.with-tab-bar` except on the landing page, the hero, the donor area's column; a `.ui-panel-page` takes it into its own dark padding and gives it back with a negative margin, so no white band shows under it) and in `scroll-padding-bottom` (`styles.scss`, `html:has(app-tab-bar)`), and a floating notice sits above the bar (`_notice.scss`). A tab's link is the address it was last left at (`core/navigation/tab-memory.ts`, `TabMemory`: the last URL seen in each tab and the current tab, followed on every `NavigationEnd`), so a tab opens where the visitor left it, and the current tab pressed on a page deeper in it returns to its root, as an iOS tab bar pops its stack (`BackTarget.toTabRoot`: one step back in the history when the entry behind is that root, otherwise the root replaces the page, so Back never returns to the page left, #394); a plain click on the tab at its root scrolls to the top (smoothly, unless `prefers-reduced-motion`), modifier clicks open the link as a browser does. `core/navigation/back-target.ts` (`BackTarget`, started at app start with `TabMemory` by `navigationAtStart` in `app.config.ts`): counts the app's own history depth in the history state (`nfnnAppDepth`, through `Location`, so it survives a reload: the first navigation reads it back; popstate takes the restored entry's depth, `replaceUrl` and `skipLocationChange` keep it, every other navigation adds one); each entry also keeps the path of the entry behind it (`nfnnAppBehind`); `back(parent)` goes back in the history while the app has an entry of its own behind and that entry is in the same tab (or either page is outside the tabs, as the donor area), and otherwise opens the route's `parent` with `replaceUrl`, so a page opened from a link outside never leaves the site on Zpět and a deep page a tab reopened never returns into another tab; `returningByButton` tells the shell that the history step came from Zpět, so the public shell focuses the new page's `h1` as after any app navigation (a browser Back keeps the browser's focus). `core/navigation/edge-swipe-back.ts` (`EdgeSwipeBack`, started by `edgeSwipeBackAtStart` in `app.config.ts`): on an iPhone or iPad (a touch screen whose browser has WebKit's `GestureEvent`: Safari in a tab, the app opened from the home screen and every iOS browser, all WebKit; not Android, whose back gesture is the system's, nor a Mac), it takes Safari's back swipe from the left edge, which slid the page under the finger, and turns it into one step back as in a native app (Jakub 2026-11-06, in the home-screen app and, the same day, on nfnn.cz in Safari too). A non-passive `touchstart` on the document cancels a touch that starts within `EDGE_ZONE_PX` (20 px, the strip where the system's edge pan starts; with `viewport-fit=cover` it lies inside the safe area in landscape) of the left edge, which stops WebKit's own back and forward navigation gesture (Safari since 13.4); passive `touchmove` and `touchcancel` and a non-passive `touchend` follow that one touch by its identifier. It counts as a swipe back when, with one finger and mostly sideways, it is released at least a third of the screen's width from where it began, or after at least 40 px with the last move at 0.3 px/ms or faster within the last 100 ms (as `SheetDrag` measures a flick), and not more than 10 px back from its furthest point (pulling back towards the edge calls it off). A step back is then what Back does: with a `dialog` open, a step back in the history (`DialogHistory` closes the top dialog or its last step), except while a navigation waits (`Router.currentNavigation()`: the unsaved-changes dialog of a guard), when the last open dialog gets a `cancel` and closes unless it refuses, as with Esc; in the home-screen app (`navigator.standalone`) without a dialog, exactly what Zpět does, `BackTarget.back(parent)` with the route's `parent` (so it never leaves the tab's stack and the shell focuses the new page's `h1`), and nothing on a tab root; in a browser tab, the browser's back, which may leave the site as the swipe did. A `popstate` or `pagehide` between the touch's start and end means the browser already went back by itself (a swipe that reached Safari's own gesture, as one made while the page still glides after a scroll can), so no second step follows. A tap within the edge (moved at most 10 px, shorter than 500 ms) has its native `click` cancelled at `touchend` and gets one synthetic `click` on its target with the touch's coordinates, so a link, button or label still works; a longer press is no tap. A touch that starts on a field (`input`, `select`, `textarea`, `contenteditable`) is never cancelled, so the caret and the keyboard work, and one on an element with `touch-action: none` (the zoom viewer's stage, the sheet's grabber) is cancelled for Safari but never taken as a swipe back, so panning a zoomed photo from the edge stays a pan (pointer events still reach it). A vertical scroll started within those 20 px does not scroll. Safari's forward swipe from the right edge is left alone, so scrolling with the right thumb keeps working. Android and the desktop keep their own gestures. Playwright's WebKit reports `maxTouchPoints` 0, so no e2e project turns it on, and it cannot perform the system gesture anyway, so it is a device check (a Safari tab and the home-screen app, a swipe right after a flick scroll, landscape, iPad Split View, Chrome on iOS). `core/navigation/route-chrome.ts` (`RouteChrome.current`) reads the deepest route's data: `parent` (inherited; the article and project pages name their list, the privacy policy `/o-nas`, `/darce` `/`), `head` (`photo`, `dark` by default, `light` for `/o-nas`, `/prispet` and `/darce`), `tabBar` (false for `/pripojeni` and `/prvni-nastaveni`), `landing` (`/`, whose route runs its guard on every navigation, so an in-app link to an old `/#…` address redirects too), `phoneFooter` (`/o-nas`), `backOnWide` and `logoBesideBack` (false for `/darce` and `/sprava`). `index.html` sets `viewport-fit=cover`, so the safe areas are the page's to keep (the header, the tab bar and `.ui-container`'s side padding take `env(safe-area-inset-*)`). `site-footer/`: on the dark footer the logo with `info@nfnn.cz` under it and a `nav` "Odkazy v patičce": on wide screens O nás, Aktuality, Pomáháme and Přispět (each with `aria-current="page"` and `.is-current` on its own tab), at every width "Kontakt" (`/o-nas#kontakt`), then the bottom bar with the copyright, the registry line and the privacy link. `public-shell/` renders the footer on wide screens on every page but the landing page, and on phones only on the About page, where the tabs end (`phoneFooter`). `public-shell/` asks `SetupRedirect.checkOnce()` (`features/operations/setup/setup-redirect.ts`) once per page load: while `GET /api/operations/setup` says `required`, it navigates to `/prvni-nastaveni`; a failure is ignored, so a running site never depends on it. Both shells start with the skip link "Přeskočit na obsah" (global `.skip-link`, fixed top-left, shown on keyboard focus) to `main#obsah`: in `public-shell/` the `main` around the pages, in `donor-shell/` (`/darce`, `/sprava`) the content column: in the donor area that of `side-nav` at every width (one `side-nav` whose `panel` input drops the panel on phones, so the routed page stays the same component when the window crosses `nav`), in the administration likewise (the panel only above `nav`, 2.24). `public-shell/` also follows navigation for keyboard and screen-reader users: after a route change (not the first load, not back/forward, not while a dialog is open) it focuses the page `h1` unless the page already moved the focus into `main`, and after a link to a section (`/o-nas#kontakt`, also when clicked again) it focuses that section; both get `tabindex="-1"` and no ring.

List rows of plan v3 2.25: `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` (`core/layout/compact-layout.ts`) is true below `nav` and also wherever the window is narrower than `nav` measured in the root text size (`innerWidth <= 899 × root font size / 16`, so an iPad on its side at 200 % text, 1194 px wide with a 32 px root, counts as narrow), following the media query, `resize` and a `ResizeObserver` on a hidden `1rem` probe, so enlarging the text switches at once; at the default text size on a wide screen it is false and every table stays as it was, pixel for pixel. Where it is true the lists render as `ui-list` rows instead of tables (one layout chosen by `@if`): the administration's payments (`/sprava/platby`) as rows grouped by month under a `h3.ui-list__group-title` ("Říjen 2026") that sticks under the header's back button (`--list-group-title-top`, the header's height in the administration), each row a `button.payment-row` (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) with the sender (or "Bez jména odesílatele"), the date, "VS …" or "bez VS" and, when paired, "dárce …" (or "dárce bez záznamu"), the amount and at most one state badge (vráceno, jen ručně, Ověření účtu); a waiting payment's row opens the pairing dialog, a paired one's opens the sheet "Přiřazená platba" (`presentation="sheet"`) with the payment, the sender, the symbol and message, the donor with a link to the card, the method and tags, and "Zrušit přiřazení" with its inline confirmation (or the line that an issued receipt blocks it); the sheet closes after an unpairing, a refused one keeps it open with the reason inside it, its donor link hands the sheet's history entry over to the card (`DialogHistory.handOver`), so one Back returns to the payments, and a live change waits while it is open; the filter stacks wherever the list is rows; the donors (`/sprava/darci`) as link rows to the card with the name, "VS …" · e-mail (or "vymazán") · IČO and only the tags that say something (no "běžný účet"); the history (`/sprava/historie`) as rows with the action, the time, the administrator (or server, or "správce bez účtu") and the application, then the server's sentence, the donor card link and the collapsed technical details (with the administrator's e-mail); the donor card (`/sprava/darci/:id`) as the summary over an inset list of its parts (Údaje, Přihlášení, Bankovní účty "1 účet / n účty / n účtů", Dary "n darů za … Kč", Potvrzení "1 vydané potvrzení / n vydaná / n vydaných potvrzení", Výmaz údajů; a part whose data failed says "Nepodařilo se načíst, otevřete a zkuste znovu", one still loading names no count), each part a page of its own at `/sprava/darci/:id/<part>` (`udaje`, `prihlaseni`, `bankovni-ucty`, `dary`, `potvrzeni`, `vymaz`; an unknown part opens the card) with only that card under the name, the header's back button leading to the card (the route's `parent` is `/sprava/darci/:id`, and `RouteChrome` fills in a parent's `:name` segments from the route's parameters) and, where the header shows no back button, the link "← <name>", its tables stacked as cards (`.ui-table--stacked`), and while its data loads or after it failed the part shows the wait or the failure with a retry under its own title; the Dary part shows the gifts as rows under a heading per year ("2026 · 2 dary · 800 Kč", refunds left out of the sum) with the date, the purpose and pairing, the sender account and symbol, the amount, the refund pill and the receipt number (or "Potvrzení zatím žádné"); on a wide screen the same address shows the whole card and focuses that part; and the donor's gifts (Moje dary and the overview) as rows per year with the date, the purpose and pairing tag, the amount, the refund pill and the receipt download under them. The `ui-list` row is a grid of named areas (`lead title value chevron` over `lead meta value chevron`, one line when a row has no meta), `ui-list__state` sits under the value, `ui-list__extra` spans the row under the title, and rows inside a `ui-card` take the card's padding.

## content

- The pages of the tabs (2.23) are built from `home/sections/`: `landing/` (`/`, `Landing`) shows only `hero` (full-bleed photo under the header, at least `100svh` tall and taller only when its text needs it, so the page fits the visible screen without scrolling in Safari, whose `100vh` is its larger viewport with the toolbar retracted, and in the installed app; copy, the CTA "Přispět" to `/prispet` and "Kde pomáháme" to `/projekty` only while `CompactLayout.isCompact()` and `ShortViewport.isShort()` are both false, because on phones and tablets, upright or on their side, the tab bar right under them offers both (Jakub 2026-11-05); the copy keeps the header's room above it, the safe-area insets beside it and room for the tab bar under it; up to the height `hero-lead` (680 px, an iPhone SE) the lead is only visually hidden and the screen reader still reads it, so the copy stays under the child's face (Jakub on #371), and up to `short` (500 px, a phone on its side) the headline takes `--text-hero-short`, capped by the width and the height, so it stays in the left part of the photo beside the face; no footer; the root and the body behind it are `--color-image-shade` (`styles.scss`, `html:has(app-landing), html:has(app-landing) body`, the same rule as the 404 page's dark canvas on phones), because Safari paints the strip under its floating toolbar with the body's background, so that strip continues the photo's dark bottom instead of white; the `html` rule also gives the root `overscroll-behavior: none`, so pulling past the top or bottom no longer drags the photo away from the page, as the rubber band of Safari and the installed app on an iPhone did), `about-page/` (`/o-nas`, "O nás | …", `AboutPage`) asks `GET /api/content/home` once (`HomeApi`) and shows `about` (with its `h1` "Vznik a poslání") with the photo and the people from that answer, `stats-bar` and the contact form, then the footer on every width, `donate-page/` (`/prispet`, "Přispět | …", `DonatePage`) shows `donate-cta` (its `h1` "Přispějte na pomůcky pro děti") with the signed-in donor's name and symbol from `AuthStore`; news are the Aktuality tab (`news-page/`) and projects the list of the Pomáháme tab (`list/`). The sections: `stats-bar` (years counted from the registry entry of 28 October 2005 on the visitor's day; the figures wait hidden at zero until 40 % of the bar is in view (`IntersectionObserver`), then fade in one after another and count up to their values in 1.6 s, the year 2005 only fades in; a screen reader reads the final figures all along from a visually hidden copy, and with `prefers-reduced-motion` or without `IntersectionObserver` they stand at their values at once), `about` (what the foundation does first, then the work with FOD, then its founding; beside the text the photo an administrator set at `/sprava/o-nas` (input `photo`, `about.photo` of the home content, Jakub 2026-10-30) as a `figure` with the whole photo (`?w=480` with a 480/960/1 440 px `srcset` never wider than the original, `sizes` the column, `loading="lazy"`, `width`/`height` from the API, `alt` from the API or empty; the figure narrows to keep an upright photo at most 640 px tall, never cropping it, and its shape is the `--about-photo-ratio` the template binds) with the FOD badge over its lower corner and the caption under it in a `figcaption`, where the badge never reaches; up to 768 px the photo goes under the text and the badge is hidden; without a photo, or when the home content fails, the stock photo with the badge as before; under both columns, after a hairline, the block "Kdo fond vede" (Jakub 30. 10. 2026, after a sponsor in the user tests could not find who runs the foundation today): the eyebrow `h3` "Kdo fond vede", then the two founders as a list (`.board-people`, two columns where each gets at least 28 rem, otherwise one under the other): each a round portrait (`app-avatar` with `/images/pavel-znamenacek-` and `/images/andrea-znamenackova-160.webp` and `-320.webp`, 160 px, 96 px up to 480 px, lazy) beside the name and the role ("Zakladatel a předseda správní rady", "Zakladatelka a členka správní rady"; Andrea added on Jakub's word of 30. 10. 2026, both approved their portraits), and under them the board as in the registry (not the auditor, Jakub 2026-10-30), the names held together by no-break spaces; that built-in block stays while the home content carries no `people` (input `people`, `PublicPeopleResponse` in `features/content/people-api.ts`, Jakub 2026-10-30). With people from `/sprava/lide-fondu` the same classes show the featured people as cards (`app-avatar`: the portrait `?w=480` at 160 px in a circle, or the initials without one, beside the name and the role), then the founders' note (`.founders-note`, at most 44 rem wide, one `p` per paragraph) with the web signatures of the featured people under it (`.signatures`, a list left-aligned and aligned to the bottom so the names under them line up: each an `img` of the uploaded image as it is, in its own ink colour, `?w=480`, about 44 px tall and 34 px up to 480 px, `alt=""` because the name is printed under it in small text), then the board line "Správní rada: …" built by `boardLine()` with no-break spaces inside each name and the note in parentheses, and the line under the board (`people.footnote`, `.board-footnote`, the same muted text close under it)), `donate-cta` (the eyebrow, title and lead, then `app-payment-panel` with the line before paying and the `h3` "Zaplaťte" projected between the amount and the bank details, then "Co bude dál"), in one order for every width (Jakub 2026-10-24, the audit's option B1; the decorative photo with its quote only above 768 px and taller than `short` (500 px), so a phone on its side keeps the phone's one column, at the top of its column and sticky under the header): (1) eyebrow "Přispět", heading "Přispějte na pomůcky pro děti" and one sentence why; (2) "Zvolte částku": preset or custom amount (500 Kč preselected; "Jiná částka" opens an empty field, so the QR says "Nejprve zadejte částku" until an amount is typed; whole crowns with spaces, hint after leaving the field); (3) `.before-pay`, the line before paying: signed out "Chcete potvrzení o daru pro daňové přiznání? Přihlaste se nebo si založte účet ještě před platbou: …", then "Potvrzení vystavíme i na firmu: při registraci zvolte Firma a vyplňte IČO." (`.company-hint`), with "Založit účet" and "Přihlásit se" (they open the dialog; after a sign-in from here the page stays and the focus moves to the "Bankovní spojení" label, now with the donor's symbol), signed in "Platíte jako <name>, variabilní symbol <VS>." (input `donorName` from `AuthStore.donor().name`, shown alone); (4) `h3` "Zaplaťte": the bank details (account, amount, the signed-in donor's variable symbol, IBAN, each with a copy button), for a signed-out visitor right under them an amber callout "Potvrzení o daru k platbě bez přihlášení vystavíme, až si založíte účet a ověříte v něm bankovní účet, ze kterého jste platili." whose "založíte účet" opens the registration like "Založit účet" (testers of 2026-10-30 paid by QR without reading the line before paying), then the QR that `spayd()` and `encodeText` (level H) draw in the browser from `PaymentDetailsStore`, by `app-qr-code` at 200 px (nothing is asked after the details: a preset redraws at once; typed digits and "Jiná částka" dim the QR at once (`.qr-image-stale`, `aria-busy`, "Uložit QR kód" disabled) and redraw it 400 ms after the last key, plan D30, so the morph runs once for the finished amount and a new one interrupts a running one; a visually hidden hint on the field says so; an amount that is not valid shows "Nejprve zadejte částku" only after those 400 ms; when the details fail or `spayd()` refuses, the QR's alert with "Zkusit znovu" loads the details again; after the visitor changes the amount a visually hidden `role="status"` line says "QR platba na 1 000 Kč je připravená." once, after 1.2 s without a change, and only when it names another amount than the last one) with its text and "Uložit QR kód" (kept in place and disabled while the QR waits to be redrawn; saves a 720 px PNG made in the browser, `qr-platba-nova-nadeje.png`, "Připravujeme obrázek…" while it runs, then the always rendered `role="status"` line `.qr-saved` names the file and says it is in the downloaded files, until the amount changes); up to 480 px the QR sits above its text; the DOM order is the visual order, so Tab reaches the copy buttons before the QR; (5) `h3` "Co bude dál": signed in, the gift shows in the overview within an hour of the bank booking it (another bank's transfer usually arrives by the next working day) and the yearly receipt is downloaded in the donor account after the year ends; signed out, how an earlier gift paid without signing in is paired once the donor sends one with the symbol from the same account, and that the yearly receipt comes with a donor account; both end with the sentence that the gift can be deducted from the tax base under the income tax act.
- `detail/` (`content-detail`, both `/aktuality/:slug` and `/projekty/:slug`): loads the article or project by its slug (`NewsApi.publishedBySlug`, `ProjectsApi.publishedBySlug`; when it answers with another slug, a former one, the page navigates to the current address with `replaceUrl`, so the canonical link names it); "Načítáme stránku…" (`role="status"`), a failure as `role="alert"` "Stránku se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu", a 404 (`content/news-not-found`, `content/project-not-found`) renders `not-found` at the same address with `[missing]` set to the kind ("Tato aktualita už na webu není." / "Tento projekt už na webu není.", "Všechny aktuality" / "Všechny projekty" and "Zpět na úvodní stránku") and the tab title "Stránka nenalezena | …"; when loaded, the dark intro with "← Všechny aktuality" / "← Všechny projekty" (`/aktuality`, `/projekty`), the eyebrow "<tag> · <date>" or "<tag> · <year>", the focused `h1`, the perex as lead, then the photo (`?w=960`, `srcset` 480/960/1440, `alt=""`) and the body rendered by `<app-markdown>` (plain text becomes paragraphs at empty lines, with single line breaks kept), the photo's `alt` from `coverImage.alt` (empty when absent), under a signed news article its signer (`.signer`: `<app-avatar>` of 64 px from the portrait's `?w=480` copy or the initials, the name and the role), then `<app-share-button>` with the title and the canonical address (`canonicalAddress()` of `core/seo/canonical-link.ts`, so `https://nfnn.cz/aktuality/<slug>` or `/projekty/<slug>` whichever host serves the page; not in the preview), and the line "Chcete pomoct dalším dětem? Přispějte na jejich pomůcky." over "Přispět" (`/prispet`) with "Zpět na aktuality" / "Zpět na projekty"; the header's "Zpět" leads to the list (`parent`). The loaded page is `detail/content-article` (`app-content-article [page] [kind] [preview]`, the `ContentPage` built by `newsPage()` / `projectPage()` in `detail/content-page.ts`, which also holds `ContentKind`), so the administration's preview shows the same page: with `preview` the title is an `h3` instead of the focused `h1` and the dark intro drops the room for the fixed header; `content-detail` focuses the heading through `focusHeading()`. Texts under `content.detail.*`; specs `content-detail.spec.ts` (Markdown and the alt included) and `content-article.spec.ts`.
- `news-page/` (`NewsPage`, `/aktuality`, v3 2.26 "Novinky", after Jakub's mockups of 2026-10-31): the light intro (`.ui-intro--light`) with the eyebrow "Aktuality" (hidden up to 480 px), the focused `h1` "Co je u nás nového" and a lead, then a page of `PAGE_SIZE` = 10 from `NewsApi.published` (`?strana=`, 1-based, anything else is page 1; a newer page change cancels the answer still on its way). Page 1 waits for the news and the stories together, so nothing moves under the visitor once it shows: the projects (`ProjectsApi.published`, pages of 50 one after another, at most `MAX_PROJECT_PAGES` = 20, each project once by id) load once per visit to the page and are kept for a return to page 1 and for a retry; their failure, or no answer within `STORIES_WAIT_MS` = 4 s, only leaves the stories out. On page 1 the newest news leads as `featured-news/` (`app-featured-news [item]`): its photo across the card (`?w=960` with the 480/960/1 440 px `srcset` the server keeps, `focalShape` `newsFeatured`, `fetchpriority="high"`) under a shade, the tag and date, the title as an `h2`, a perex of three lines (two up to 480 px), the signer (`app-avatar` of 40 px from `?w=480`, the name and, from 481 px, the role) and the actions, which wrap to the right under the signer when the text is large: `<app-share-button tone="dark" [subject]>` and the link "Číst celé" (its accessible name adds the title; it covers the whole card and shows only a round arrow up to 480 px). Then `year-stories/` (`app-year-stories [stories]`, `storiesOfNewestYear()`): "Příběhy z roku N" (each strip names its section by a heading id of its own) with the projects of the newest year in the site's order as a strip that scrolls sideways (each tile a 72 px photo `?w=480`, `focalShape` `storyTile`, or a heart, the tag and the title linking to the project; columns at least 16 rem wide, so at 200 % text a tile keeps its words, four in a row on a computer and 78 % tiles up to 768 px) and the link "Všechny N v Pomáháme" (ICU plural, "Zobrazit v Pomáháme" for one) to `/projekty?rok=N`, which the Pomáháme page of 2.23a reads. Then "Starší zprávy" (on a later page only the visually hidden "Zprávy na straně N", with no leading news and no strip) as `app-news-card`s in an auto-filling grid and the `nav` "Stránky aktualit" with "← Novější", "Strana N z M" and "Starší →". The always rendered `role="status"` line says "Načítáme aktuality…", "Zatím tu žádná aktualita není." with "Zpět na úvodní stránku" or "Tato strana neexistuje." with "Na první stranu"; a failure is the `role="alert"` "Aktuality se nepodařilo načíst." or, while `Connectivity.isOffline()`, "Bez připojení se aktuality nenačetly. Až budete online, zkuste to znovu.", in the danger colour, each with "Zkusit znovu", after which the focus goes to the `h1` or, when it fails again, back to the button. The news card (`news-card/`) shows under its perex the signer (`app-avatar` of 40 px from `?w=480`) and, when linked, Sdílet with the article as its `subject`; from a card 480 px wide (the container `news-card`) its photo stands beside the text (`newsCardSide`). The closing Přispět band of the mockups comes with 2.23a's `<app-donate-band>`. Texts under `content.news.*`; spec `news-page.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/content-pages.spec.ts` (the leading news, the stories, paging by ten, sharing and copying from the feed, the offline failure and the retry), the axe cases (with a signer and the failure) and the device step `news-feed`.
- `list/` (`content-list`, `/projekty`): the dark intro with the focused `h1` "Projekty" and a lead, then the page of `PAGE_SIZE` = 10 from `ProjectsApi.published`; the page is `?strana=` (1-based, anything else is page 1) and changes reload the list in place. A `role="status"` line that is always rendered says "Načítáme projekty…" and, when nothing is published, "Zatím tu žádný projekt není." with "Zpět na úvodní stránku", and a page past the last one says "Tato strana neexistuje." with "Na první stranu"; a failure is the `role="alert"` "Seznam se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu". The cards are `app-project-card` in an auto-filling grid (it and `news-card/`, a published article also in the administration's preview, take `[linked]`, true by default; false shows the title without its link, for the administration's preview; specs `news-card.spec.ts`, `project-card.spec.ts`), and when there is more than one page the `nav` "Stránky projektů" offers "← Novější", "Strana N z M" and "Starší →". Texts under `content.list.*`; spec `content-list.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/content-pages.spec.ts` (paging by ten with the address) and the axe case for a middle page and the empty projects list.
- `privacy-policy/` ("Zásady ochrany osobních údajů"; "Jaké e‑maily posíláme" lists the e-mails a donor gets (the templates in `backend/src/main/resources/templates/email/donors/`: confirmation and one reminder, password reset, e-mail change, security notices, an address used by another registration or account, erasure), says receipts, newsletters and per-gift e-mails are never sent and that these e-mails cannot be turned off; "Vaše práva" says what an erasure deletes and what stays: the issued receipts unchanged on the server with the name and address (and a company's identification number) on them, the payment records with the bank statement's account number and payer name, and the record of the administrators' actions (A6; nothing about devices until Phase 2 brings copies onto them); under the intro a `nav` "Obsah" links every part, whose `section`s carry the ids `spravce`, `udaje`, `emaily`, `uchovavani`, `prijemci`, `cookies`, `prava`, `kontakt`, `ucinnost`; "Evidence darů a potvrzení o daru" also says that `/overit` checks the signature in the browser and sends only the SHA-256 digest of the signed data for the status, and that sharing a verification link shares the receipt data, S-L7; "Hlášení chyb z prohlížeče" says what an error report holds and never holds, that it carries no account or IP address but could be matched by its time to other server records, which happens only when looking for a fault, that it is kept at most 14 days in the application log and in the container log until that is deleted by volume, and the limits name the 30 reports per 15 minutes and IPv6 counted by its /64), `not-found/` ("Tuhle stránku jsme nenašli", links home and to the contact form; with the input `missing` (`news` / `project`) it says the article or project is gone and leads to its list first; `NOT_FOUND_TITLE` is the tab title, `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` while shown, because nginx answers an unknown slug of a detail route with 200 and cannot know it is missing; below the `nav` breakpoint, where the page shows no footer, `styles.scss` gives the canvas and `body` the hero's `--color-surface-inverse` while `app-not-found` is shown, so the dark page continues under the tab bar and Safari's toolbar instead of ending in a white band).

## contact

`contact-api.ts` (`POST /api/contact/messages`); `contact-form/` with honeypot, 429 and generic errors (both end with a `mailto:` link to info@nfnn.cz), a request that reached no server (status 0) "Bez připojení se zprávu nepodařilo odeslat. Zpráva vám zůstala; až se připojíte, odešlete ji znovu." (2.7; the send button carries `appNeedsConnection`), "Odesíláme…" while sending, no example placeholders in the name and e-mail fields, the e-mail hint "Odpovíme vám na zadaný e-mail." (the site names no reply time, and the sender gets no confirmation e-mail on purpose), a client check of the backend's address shape (`pattern`, a dot in the domain); a signed-in donor finds the e-mail, and for a person also the name, filled in from `AuthStore.donor()` (only empty fields, also when the session arrives later and again after "Odeslat další zprávu"), editable; a 400 with `errors[]` puts each message at its field through `placeServerErrors` (the first refused field takes the focus) (the e-mail field shows its own "Zadejte e-mail ve tvaru jan@example.com." for any server refusal) instead of the general failure; the thank-you ("Zpráva odeslána!", "Odpovíme vám e-mailem na <address>.") fills an always rendered `role="status"` region and its heading takes the focus, "Odeslat další zprávu" brings back an empty form (prefilled again for a signed-in donor) with the focus in the name field; and a line under the button that the data serves only to handle the message, linking the policy (legitimate interest, no consent wording). Beside the heading only the e-mail stays; the seat ("Sídlo", no longer "Adresa") and the IČO moved into `accounting-details/` (`app-accounting-details`, Jakub 30. 10. 2026), a `ui-card` placed under the e-mail in the left column on wide screens and after the form below 768 px (grid areas, so the reading and Tab order is heading, form, details everywhere): "Údaje pro účetní" as a `dl.ui-data-list` with Název, IČO, Sídlo, Zápis (the registry entry), Číslo účtu and IBAN (from `PaymentDetailsStore` once it holds the payment details, the IBAN through `formatIban`; until the account is known the account row says "Najdete ho v sekci Přispět" linking `/#prispet` and the IBAN row is left out), a `copy-button` for the IČO, the seat, the account and the IBAN, and the line "Potvrzení o daru na firmu získáte, když si při registraci zvolíte Firma a vyplníte IČO."; each row puts its label beside the value while the card is wide enough and above it otherwise (flex-wrap, no breakpoint), and a copy button sits right of its value or, in a narrow card, under it. Texts under `home.contact.accounting.*`, spec `accounting-details.spec.ts`.

## donations (services and helpers)

- `payment-details-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/payment-details` without the access token (`PaymentDetails`: `account` `{ number, iban, recipientName }`, `currency`, `messages` `{ donation, accountVerification }`, `accountVerificationAmountMinor`; `recipientName` is the SPAYD text for `RN`, never shown). The answer passes a shape check and the account check (`paymentDetailsOf`: the IBAN must be the IBAN of the account number, `czech-account.ts`), so a malformed 200 or an account that disagrees with its IBAN becomes a failed load instead of breaking a panel or showing that account. `payment-details-store.ts` (`PaymentDetailsStore`, root): `load()` asks once per page load (again after a failure), `state` is `loading`, `ready` with the details or `failed`; every answer is kept with its `Date` in `localStorage` under `nfnn.payment-details` (`KEPT_PAYMENT_DETAILS`, `{ details, writtenAt }`), and a load that reached no server (`isUnreachable`: status 0, or a gateway status without a problem body) shows the kept details instead (read back through `paymentDetailsOf`, so a changed or broken entry is refused), while an answer of the server that fails the check or is a problem shows none, so a changed account never brings back the old one, with `age` (`{ writtenAt, kept }`) telling the panel how old they are (plan v3 2.7); the Přispět panel, the standing order guide and the add-account dialog share it, so a session asks once. Once the held details are five minutes old (the answer's `max-age`), the next `load()` or the page becoming visible again (`visibilitychange`) asks again with the request header `Cache-Control: no-cache` (the XHR backend ignores Angular's `cache` option, which is set too), keeping the details meanwhile and on a failure, and replaces them only when they changed (an unchanged answer is a 304 from the ETag); `reload()`, behind every "Zkusit znovu", shows the wait and asks the same way. `iban-format.ts` `formatIban` writes an IBAN in groups of four for the panel and the contact section, whose "Údaje pro účetní" (`contact/accounting-details`) shows the foundation's account from `PaymentDetailsStore` once the details are held, without asking for them itself.
- `spayd.ts`: `spayd(details, { amountMinor, variableSymbol?, purpose? })` → `{ spayd }` or `{ refused: { fields } }` (`amountMinor`, `variableSymbol`, `details`, in that order): the SPAYD text of a payment (`SPD*1.0*ACC*AM*CC[*X-VS][*RN]*MSG`, the message of the purpose), refused for an amount outside 1 heller to 9 999 999,99 Kč or not whole hellers, a symbol that is not 1 to 10 digits (or none for `ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION`) and details that are not SPAYD-ready (contract, "`GET /api/donations/payment-details`": among them an IBAN that is not the IBAN of the shown account number, checked by `czech-account.ts`, `ibanOfAccountNumber` and `accountMatchesIban`, on the cases of `shared-cases/payment/account.json`); the cases are `shared-cases/payment/spayd.json` (`spayd.spec.ts` runs each through `spayd()` and then `encodeText` at level H against its namesake in `qr/payment.json`).
- `payment-panel/` (`app-payment-panel`, inputs `variableSymbol` and `idPrefix`, `donate` by default, so the home page keeps `#donate-custom-amount`; a size container `payment-panel` that stacks its bank rows and sets the QR above its caption as on a phone also when the panel itself is at most 480 px of the root text size wide, as in the two-column home section on a tablet upright or with enlarged text): the amount choice, then its projected content, then the bank details, then the content projected with the attribute `afterBankDetails` (the signed-out receipt note of the home page, the standing order guide of the card "Poslat dar"), and the QR with "Uložit QR kód" of the home donate section (2) and (4); on phones and tablets (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, slice 2.23c, Q13) the QR's text block gives way, after the projected content, to the primary "Otevřít v aplikaci banky", which on a device whose `navigator.canShare` takes both the payment file and an image (iOS, `sharesPaymentFiles` and `sharesImages` in `bank-handoff.ts`) shares the PNG of the drawn QR (drawn ahead as below and waited for with the button disabled), because no iOS bank app takes the payment file from the share sheet (Jakub's iPhone test of 5 November 2026; Alza's `pay-via-bank-app` shares the image on iOS too), and when that share is dismissed or refused the notice says to choose "Uložit obrázek" and read the payment from Photos in the bank app; there the second button is "Poslat jako soubor platby", which shares `platba.spayd` (the SPAYD text of the drawn amount, `application/x-shortpaymentdescriptor`) for a bank app that may take it, with its own notice pointing back to the image; a drawing that failed ahead makes the primary share `platba.spayd` instead; elsewhere the primary hands `platba.spayd` to the share sheet where `navigator.canShare` takes the file and otherwise, when the service worker controls the page and reserves the payment (`local-payment-route.ts`, `LocalPaymentRoute.reserve`: a message `nfnn-payment-file` over a `MessageChannel`, answered within 2 s with a random id), opens `/platba.spayd?id=<id>`, which the worker answers on the device (Android, follow-up of 2.23c: `frontend/service-worker/payment-route.js`; the notice then says what to do if no bank opened), and otherwise downloads it (a browser without the worker, a worker of an older release or the safety worker, which do not answer the message); when another amount was picked while the worker was asked, neither the earlier payment opens nor its file downloads, then (where the primary does not share the image) "Poslat QR jako obrázek", which shares the PNG of the drawn QR (`QrCode.pngBlob`, 720 px, drawn ahead after each redraw and waited for with the button disabled, so `navigator.share` is called within the tap before anything is awaited and keeps its user activation on iOS; a drawing that failed ahead leaves the button to save the PNG instead) or, where images cannot be shared or the share is refused, downloads it and is then labelled "Uložit QR kód"; while a share is open both buttons stay focusable with `aria-disabled` and a second tap does nothing, and the answer to a share that comes after another amount was picked says nothing; one always-rendered `role="status"` line after the first button says what to do when the bank did not open (a dismissed or refused share, or the downloaded file), is cleared by another amount, and says nothing after a share that went through, since the page cannot know whether a bank app took it; the QR itself stays rendered (so its image can be made) in a `role="region"` folded with `hidden` under the disclosure button "Platíte z jiného zařízení?" (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`; the region is named by the button's constant first line), 260 px wide when unfolded with a caption naming the amount (read out too, since the image's label names no amount), and its failure callout stands outside the fold before the buttons, which wait disabled while no payment is drawn; there "Zkusit znovu" moves the focus to the block of the buttons (`tabindex="-1"`, `aria-busy`) while it waits and to "Otevřít v aplikaci banky" once the payment is drawn; on computers the panel is as before, shared since Jakub's decision of 29 October 2026 by the home page and the card "Poslat dar" of the donor overview; the chosen amount (a preset, or a valid "Jiná částka" with its typed value) is kept in memory by the root `ChosenAmountStore` (`chosen-amount.ts`, nothing stored in the browser, 500 Kč after a reload) and shared by both places, so moving from the home page to the overview or back keeps it (testers of 2026-10-30); it exposes `accountState` (`loading`, `ready` with the account, `failed`) and `amount` (the chosen whole crowns, `null` while none is typed) so the standing order guide in the card shows the account of the same answer and the chosen amount, `retry()` and `focusBankDetails()`. Under the bank rows a `role="note"` says "Údaje účtu z 3. 11. 2026 · nemusí být aktuální" ("Údaje účtu nemusí být aktuální" without a known day) while the details are the kept ones or the app is offline (2.7). The QR's failure is a callout of two kinds, inside an always-rendered `role="status"` region when it comes with the page and a `role="alert"` once the visitor's own retry fails again: `unavailable` (the details did not load: the error text) and `refused` (the details loaded but `spayd()` or `encodeText` refused them: "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Zaplaťte prosím podle bankovního spojení."); "Zkusit znovu" calls `PaymentDetailsStore.reload()`, moves the focus on computers to the QR box (`tabindex="-1"`) while it waits (on phones and tablets to the block of the buttons, above) and back to the button when it fails again. "Jiná částka" clears the ready line; an amount is announced once, not again when it is picked again, but again after the placeholder or an invalid amount came between. "Jiná částka" renders its field and focuses it within the tap itself (`ChangeDetectorRef.detectChanges()` before `focus()`), also when it is pressed again, because iOS opens the keyboard only for a focus inside the tap's handler (#381); the field is `inputmode="numeric"` with `enterkeyhint="done"`, and Enter lets go of it.
- `donations-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/me` and `/me/summary` (the API's `year` filter has no caller); `requestBankCheck()` (POST) and `bankCheck()` (GET) on `/me/bank-check`; `checkBankUntilDone()` posts and, while `checking`, reads the check again as soon as `LiveUpdates` hints `bank-import`, else after 2 s without a stream and 15 s with one, for at most 90 s, emitting every answer; `downloadedFile(response, fallbackFileName)` turns a blob answer into `DownloadedFile` (`blob`, `fileName` from `Content-Disposition`: `filename*=UTF-8''` first, then `filename=`, else the fallback) for the administration's downloads; the donor's receipts are drawn in the browser (`receipt-pdf/`, plan 1.9), so the donor area no longer calls the PDF endpoints.
- `bank-accounts-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/me/bank-accounts` (`ACTIVE`, `AWAITING_PAYMENT`, `DISCONNECTED`, `shared`, `pairingExcluded`, `verificationPaymentOn`, `symbolOffer` with `variableSymbol`, `paymentCount`, `firstMonth`/`lastMonth` as `YYYY-MM`, and `confirmedSymbols`), `POST …/{id}/disconnect|reconnect` (204), `confirmSymbol` / `declineSymbol` on `POST …/{id}/symbol-claim/confirm` (`{ pairedCount, changedYearlyReceipts }`) and `…/decline` (204) with `{ variableSymbol }`; `DonationItem.pairedBy` (`VARIABLE_SYMBOL` | `SENDER_ACCOUNT` | `CONFIRMED_SYMBOL` | null), `senderAccount`, `confirmedSymbol` and `receiptAvailable`.
- `receipt-format.ts`: what a receipt shows, one place for the verification page and the receipt drawn in the browser: `formatReceiptAmount(minor)` (`formatMinorUnits` + `\u00a0Kč`), `formatReceiptDate` (`5. 3. 2026`, as everywhere on the site, Jakub 2026-11-06), the English values of a bilingual receipt `formatReceiptAmountEn` (`CZK 1,000.50`) and `formatReceiptDateEn` (`15 March 2026`), `fillReceiptText(text, values)` (the frozen texts' `{name}` placeholders with MessageFormat's apostrophe quoting; a missing value throws), `formatReceiptDonor` (name, IČO for a company, `street, postal code city`), `receiptDisplay(receipt)` for a `SignedReceipt`; the donor and amount cases of `shared-cases/receipt/` run in its spec.
- `receipt-pdf/` (plan 1.9): the donor's receipt drawn in the browser from its frozen data, so the server renders no PDF (since 1.10 it has no drawing at all). `receipts-api.ts`, the feature service of both sides: `issue(subject)` → `POST /api/donations/me/receipts` (`{donationId}` or `{year}`, `IssuedReceipt` of `frozen-receipt.ts`), `image(sha256)` → `GET /api/donations/me/receipt-images/{sha256}` as bytes with their media type, and the two `ReceiptSource`s a drawing reads from: `donorSource(subject)` (that issue and those images) and `administrationSource(receiptId)` (1.19: `GET /api/admin/donations/receipts/{id}`, which issues nothing, and `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-images/{sha256}`). `receipt-document.ts`: `receiptDocument(receipt, issuedAt, captions)` puts every line together from the frozen texts and values (the English line beside the Czech one on a bilingual receipt, English table values in brackets, the footer from `receipt.footer`); the caption beside the verification QR and the images' alternative texts also come from the frozen texts (`receipt.verification.*`, `receipt.signature.alt`, `receipt.portrait.alt`, frozen since 1.10, the alternative texts in Czech), and `captions` (`receipt-captions.ts`, the same words) only fills in for a receipt frozen without them, which only the shared cases still hold and names the file `potvrzeni-o-daru-<number>.pdf` / `potvrzeni-o-darech-<year>.pdf`, on an English receipt `donation-receipt-<number>.pdf` / `donation-summary-<year>.pdf`. `verification-qr.ts`: `verificationSymbol(qrText)` encodes the proof address as a byte segment and the proof digits as a numeric one, level H at the smallest version that holds it, so the foundation's star fits in its middle, else L at the smallest version, without the star (above about 220 gifts; `smallestVersion` of `shared/ui/qr-code/qr-encoder.ts`); `qrPlacement(symbol)` puts a symbol up to version 15 beside the signature as a 46.2 mm square (modules of at least 0.6 mm, Jakub 2026-10-31) and a larger one in an area of its own with modules of 0.6–0.8 mm (100 mm at most). `receipt-art.ts`: the signature and portrait images, PNG, JPEG or the vector JSON of `vector-art.ts` (strict: exact keys, a positive view box, 1–2 000 paths of SVG path commands starting with `M`, fills `#rrggbb`, opacity 0–1, 256 KB at most), each refused unless its SHA-256 is the digest in the frozen data. `receipt-style.ts`: `resolveReceiptStyle(element)` reads the theme's `--receipt-*` tokens as text, with no canvas: colours only as literal `#rrggbb`, sizes in pt, margins in mm, leading as a number and the three font files by their content-named file name (`body.<16 hex>.ttf`, `body-bold.…`, `heading.…`); anything else is refused with `ReceiptStyleRefused`, and so is a text colour (`text`, `muted`, `english`) or the QR colour (`code`) below 4.5:1 on the white page (`checkedReceiptStyle`, which `drawReceipt` runs again in the worker). `receipt-layout.ts`, the only module that imports pdfkit (`eslint-confinement.js`), draws an A4 tagged PDF (PDF/UA subset, `lang`, Document, H1, H2, P, Table with TR/TH/TD, Figure with alt text, Link): the vector logo, the replaced-receipt notice, both parties, the gift line or a table whose header repeats as an artifact on every page, the totals, the closing kept on one page (declaration, then the signature block in the layout "3B dopis" of plan 1.16: place and date on the left, on the right a card with the round portrait, the signature, a rule, the name and role), the verification QR in the look of the site's payment QR (Jakub 2026-10-31: dots in `--receipt-color-code`, rounded finder and alignment marks in `--receipt-color-code-mark`, at level H a badge in the middle with the foundation's star in `--receipt-color-code-star` on `--receipt-color-code-star-fill`, 12 % of the symbol's width; the geometry is `brandedQrLayout` of `shared/ui/qr-code/qr-branded-layout.ts`, which the site's QR component draws from too) inside a link to its address with a caption (`receipt-captions.ts`, "Ověření potvrzení" and "Načtěte kód telefonem a uvidíte, co fond podepsal." (Jakub 2026-10-31), on a bilingual receipt with their English ids `…English`; beside the signature the caption stands under the card, and the signature row counts the code in the left column and the card with the caption in the right, so a long caption moves the closing to the next page instead of running into the footer, and a QR in its own area keeps its four-module quiet zone above the bottom margin), and the footer with the page numbers on every page; vector art is clipped to its box (a signature to 60 × 18 mm), and Title, Author and Subject are XML-escaped for the XMP packet only (pdfkit 0.20.2 writes them there raw; the document information dictionary keeps them as written); it is deterministic (the creation date is the issue time, the font subsets are named from the font id). Its specs run in the Node environment (`// @vitest-environment node`, so pdfkit's XMP is the bytes a worker writes) and draw every frozen case in both themes with each theme's own `--receipt-*` values and the captions the site prints: exact page counts (v2's, apart from the listed differences: the gift rows are 1.2 mm lower than v2's so a year of 12 or 13 gifts stays on one page with the larger code (Jakub 2026-10-31), which also brings the 366-gift year from 12 pages to 10, and a bilingual year of 2 or 3 gifts takes a second page for its closing in the default theme), the progress bar's estimate (`estimatedReceiptPages(gifts, bilingual)`, fitted to the default theme) equal to the drawn count, each caption once, no text below the bottom margin, the quiet zone clear, the XMP well-formed with `pdfuaid:part` 1, `dc:title` and `xmp:CreateDate`. `receipt-pdf.worker.ts` is a module worker (its own chunk `worker-<hash>.js`, compiled with `tsconfig.worker.json`) that fetches the fonts the style names from `/receipt-fonts/` (copied from `src/themes/<theme>/receipt/` by `angular.json`; the hash in the name makes them immutable, as the worker chunk is), says `ready`, draws the request it gets, posts `page` per page and `done` with the transferred bytes, or `failed` with a reason and the closed `name` of what it caught (`errorNameOf`, `core/errors/error-vocabulary.ts`): `tools-missing` when a font answers 404 (`receipt-fonts.ts`, `loadReceiptFonts`: a tab older than a deploy that changed the receipt fonts, which then takes the same one reload as a lost worker chunk), `fonts` for any other font failure, `drawing` for a throw in the layout. `receipt-pdf.ts`: `ReceiptPdf.draw(source)` emits the phases `data` (the receipt from its source and the images), `tools` (the worker loading) and `drawing` (with the page), then `done` with the `DownloadedFile`; it fails with `ReceiptPdfFailure` `tools-missing` (the worker failed before it said ready, as when a deploy removed its chunk, or could not be started), `fonts`, `image`, `unprintable` (the frozen data holds a character outside the printable set, found before any worker starts), `style` (the theme's receipt tokens were refused) or `drawing` (any other throw, on the main thread too), or after 120 s without an event (`drawing`, named `TimeoutError`); each failure carries the closed `errorName` of its cause, and `ReceiptPdf` reports it through `ErrorReporter` (place `global`): `pdf-load` for `tools-missing` and `fonts`, `pdf-render` for `drawing`, `image` and `style`, nothing for `unprintable` (the data, not the browser) or a refusal of the server; unsubscribing terminates the worker; specs replace the worker and the style through `RECEIPT_PDF_ENVIRONMENT` (`testing/fake-receipt-worker.ts`). `pdf-delivery.ts`: `PdfDelivery.needsSecondTap(file)` is true on an iPhone or iPad (iPadOS's desktop agent with touch too) whose `navigator.canShare` takes the file, because Safari drops the tap's user activation while the PDF is drawn and would refuse both the download and the share sheet; `save(file)` goes through `saveBlob`, `share(file)` answers `shared`, `dismissed` (the sheet closed) or `failed`. `receipt-restart.ts`: `ReceiptRestart.tryRestart(download)` reloads the page once per tab (`sessionStorage` `nfnn.receipt-restart`) with `?stahnout=rok-<year>` or `dar-<id>` and answers `reloading`, `reloaded-already` (this tab already reloaded for that download; the marker is cleared) or `unavailable` (no session storage, so no reload); `finished()` clears the marker, `abandoned(download)` only when it names that download.
  Bulk (slice 2.29): `receipt-layout.ts` splits into `ReceiptPdfFile` (the document, its fonts, the `Document` structure root and an image cache that opens each raster once with `doc.openImage`) and `ReceiptLayout` (one receipt in its own structure parent, footer pages numbered within that receipt), so `drawReceipt` draws exactly as before (the 14 frozen cases with a portrait give the same bytes) and `drawReceiptBundle(items, {info, duplex}, …)` puts each receipt in its own `Sect` on a new page, with a blank page before it when `duplex` needs it to start on an odd page, and flushes the pages after each receipt to keep the worker's memory flat. Its types sit in `receipt-bundle-options.ts`, outside the pdfkit confinement. The worker takes a `bundle` request beside the single one (`receipt-worker-messages.ts`) and reports `{kind: 'receipt', index}` after each receipt; `ReceiptPdf.drawBundle` fetches each image digest once (`ReceiptsApi.administrationImage`), sets the PDF's creation date to the latest `issuedAt` and emits `images`, `tools`, `drawing {receipt, receipts}` and `done`. `receipt-bundle.spec.ts` proves that each receipt's pages in a bundle print exactly what its single PDF prints, footer numbers included.
- `verification/` (the page `/overit`, public shell, plan 1.8, D32): `receipt-proof.ts` reads the proof of `shared-cases/verification/payload.json` strictly (digits back to bytes with `BigInt`, the body as `ProofBytesReader`/`ReceiptProofBody` read it on the backend, every rejection reason in the backend's order, only a key whose status is `active` verifies, `statusDigest` the SHA-256 of the signed message and the signature); `ed25519.ts` verifies with WebCrypto `Ed25519` and, when the browser lacks it, with `noble-ed25519.ts` (`@noble/curves` 2.4.0, `zip215: false`), the only file `eslint-confinement.js` lets import it and only by `import()`, so the library is a lazy chunk the WebCrypto path never loads; `receipt-keys.ts` (`trustedKeys(list, hostname)`) checks each entry of a key list and drops a bad one, counts a key id listed twice as revoked, and trusts the RFC 8032 test keys only on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` and `[::1]`; `receipt-keys-api.ts` (`GET /api/donations/receipt-keys` with the request header `Cache-Control: no-cache`, the app's HTTP backend being XHR) and `receipt-status-api.ts` (`POST /api/donations/receipts/status` with `{ digest }`) both send `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`; `receipt-key-store.ts` keeps the last trusted list in `localStorage` `nfnn.receipt-keys` (every access in try/catch, replaced whole); `receipt-verifier.ts` (`ReceiptVerifier.verify(fragment, progress)`) reads with the kept keys, fetches the list when the browser says it is online (5 s), keeps it, reads again, reports `asking-status` and asks the status (5 s) only for a valid signature; a key list failure is offline only when no response came at all (status 0) or the browser is offline, anything else (429, 5xx, timeout) is `no-answer`, and a list that is not a list is `keys-refused`; once the list came, any status failure or unknown answer is `unanswered`, never offline; outcomes `empty`, `broken`, `newer` (also a reserved flag), `no-connection`, `no-answer` (no keys), `keys-refused`, `failed` (an exception while verifying, reported as `verify-signature`/`global`; a code that is broken, invalid or revoked is no browser failure and reports nothing), `invalid`, `revoked`, `unknown-key` (`reach` `online`, `offline` or `no-answer`) and `signed` with the status `active`, `replaced`, `withdrawn`, `outdated`, `unknown`, `offline`, `no-answer` (key list failed, kept keys) or `unanswered` (status asked, no answer). `verification-page` drops the fragment with `Location.replaceState('/overit')`, sets `robots` `noindex` while shown (nginx also sends `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` for `/overit`, mirrored by `scripts/serve-dist.mjs`), says "Ověřujeme potvrzení…" with a `role="status"` line that changes to "Podpis sedí, ptáme se fondu na stav potvrzení…" once the signature has passed, and focuses the `h1` of the result, and on "Zkusit znovu" that of the checking state; `verification-result` shows the result: green (✓) only for a valid signature the foundation keeps as `active` or one checked offline (with the sentence that a replacement cannot be seen), a caution (!) for `replaced`, `outdated` (1.26, Jakub 2026-11-05: "Potvrzení platilo ke dni vystavení" and that the donor's gifts changed since, so only a newer receipt counts; without one, write to info@nfnn.cz with the receipt's number), `unknown` (Jakub 2026-10-30: never green), no usable answer from the foundation ("Podpis sedí, ale fond teď neodpověděl, zda potvrzení vede", with "Zkusit znovu"), an unknown key offline or without an answer, no keys, a refused key list ("Neověřeno: seznam klíčů fondu teď nejde použít", no retry, write to info@nfnn.cz), a broken or newer code (a broken one says to scan it again only when it was read by the camera or from a photo, `ProofInput`'s `found` carrying `{ fragment, scanned }`; from the address, a pasted link or a PDF it says to open the link from the PDF again or read its QR code) and a failure, red (×) for `withdrawn`, `invalid`, `revoked` (write to info@nfnn.cz with the receipt number; no new receipt is promised, since the web cannot issue one under a new key yet) and a key the foundation does not publish; the signed data as a `ui-data-list` (number, issue day, the receipt it replaces, donor, IČO, address, gift day and amount or year and total) and, for a yearly receipt, the table "Jednotlivé dary" with each day as a row header and amounts on one line; under every result that checked a signature a line saying what left the device (the digest after the foundation answered, the digest without an answer after an unanswered status call, nothing otherwise), and always the hint that the foundation verifies only at nfnn.cz/overit. Without a code the page offers `proof-input` (Jakub 2026-10-31, from the plan's admin scanner 2.30 brought to the public page): under three `h2`s "Načíst fotoaparátem" (only when `navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia` exists), "Nahrát soubor" and "Vložit odkaz". "Naskenovat QR kód" asks for the rear camera, shows a muted inline `<video>` named "Náhled z fotoaparátu" with "Zastavit skenování" (which takes the focus when the scan button it replaces had it; stopping turns off every track and focuses the scan button again), and reads a frame 250 ms after the previous read ended, until a timer of its own ends the scan after two minutes with what to try; a preview that cannot play, a frame that cannot be read or a camera that ends by itself stops the scan with "Fotoaparát se nepodařilo spustit…", a hidden tab turns the camera off (the scan button takes the focus if it was lost), and a code handed over by any way turns it off too; when the scan ends by itself the scan button takes the focus only if it was lost; "Nahrát PDF nebo fotku" (`app-file-button`, `application/pdf,image/*`, at most 20 MB) reads a PDF with `pdf-proof-link.ts` (the first `/URI` string of the file, literal or hex, a hex string in UTF-16 included, that is a proof link; the PDF is not rendered, so it finds the link the browser-drawn receipt carries on its QR code and nothing in a scanned or re-saved PDF without link annotations) and an image through `scan-device.ts` (the size from its PNG, JPEG or WebP header by `picture-size.ts`, more than 50 megapixels refused as "Obrázek je příliš velký…" and any other format, GIF included, as unreadable, then `createImageBitmap`, upright by its EXIF orientation, scaled to at most 2 400 px into a canvas); `pdf-proof-link.ts` looks at no more than 64 `/URI` strings and reads each over at most 64 KiB, so a crafted PDF cannot hold the page; "Odkaz z QR kódu" is a template-driven field with `appProofLink` (`proof-link-validator.ts`). Every way accepts only the exact prefix `https://nfnn.cz/overit#` followed by digits (`proof-link.ts`, at most 7 089 digits; a code of another sender says "Tento QR kód nepatří k potvrzení fondu Nová naděje…" and scanning goes on), never navigates anywhere and hands the digits to the page, which verifies them in place without touching the address or the history, so the code stays out of the URL; after any result "Ověřit další potvrzení" returns to the empty page and focuses its `h1`. `qr-reader.ts` (`QrReader`) asks the browser's `BarcodeDetector` for `qr_code` where it has one and otherwise, or when it finds nothing, the module worker `qr-decoder.worker.ts` (its own `worker-*` chunk, one per page, answers matched to their read by id, ended when the page is left or after a 10 s timeout, which gives every waiting read nothing; a worker that fails before its first answer, or cannot be started, is reported as `qr-encode`/`global` (the closest kind the closed list has for the QR code worker), and after three such starts in a row the reader starts no more and rejects every read with `QrReaderOutdated`, which `proof-input` shows as "Web se mezitím aktualizoval. Obnovte prosím stránku a načtěte kód znovu."), which alone loads `qr-decoding.ts`, the only importer of `jsqr` 1.4.0 (`eslint-confinement.js`), so jsQR is never in the page's bundles. Two `role="status"` lines, always rendered (an empty one is taken out of the layout with `position: absolute`, never `display: none`, so it stays in the accessibility tree), say under the camera "Spouštíme fotoaparát…" or "Namiřte fotoaparát na QR kód z potvrzení." and under the file "Čteme kód ze souboru…", and a `role="alert"` callout under the way that caused it names a refused or missing camera, a failed camera or a scan that gave up, a PDF without a verification link, an image without a code, an unreadable or too large file or picture. The gift table stays a two-column table on phones, against the rule that tables become cards: cards would triple its height and it fits at 320 px with WCAG text spacing even for 120 gifts. The `.ui-status__icon` modifiers `--success` (green), `--caution` (amber) and `--danger` (red) in `styles/ui/_panel.scss` colour its icon; the older `--warning` is red as well, so a new warning takes `--caution`.
- `donation-month.ts`: `formatMonth('2025-01')` → `leden 2025` (names `@@donor.month.january` … `december`), `formatMonthRange(first, last)` → `říjen 2026`, `březen–říjen 2026` or `leden 2025 – říjen 2026`, with no-break spaces and no locale data.
- `file-download.ts` `saveBlob(file)`: object URL on a hidden `<a download>` clicked inside the page, revoked in `setTimeout(…, 0)` (Safari and Firefox cancel a synchronously revoked download). `donation-date.ts`: `YYYY-MM-DD` → `15. 3. 2026` without locale data (no leading zeros in day and month, ČSN 01 6910, Jakub 2026-11-06); `formatBankTime` → Prague `5. 1. 2026 8:05` (hour without a leading zero, minutes two digits, as the backend's e-mails). `donation-amount.ts`: whole crowns without decimals, hellers always two (`1 000,50`). `testing/` fixtures.

## operations
