- `setup/setup-api.ts` (`SetupApi`, `providedIn: 'root'`): `status()` → `GET /api/operations/setup` (`SetupStatusResponse`, sent with `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`: the endpoint is public, and a session token the restarted backend no longer accepts would make every poll fail), `unlock(code)`, `sendTestEmail(code, request)` → `POST /api/operations/setup/test-email` (`SetupTestEmailRequest`: `MailServerRequest` plus `sender` and `recipient`; `TestEmailResponse` with `outcome: TestEmailOutcome`, `sent`, `recipient`), `complete(request)` (`SetupRequest`, `SettingValueRequest`, `SettingsSavedResponse`), and `watchRestart()`, which polls the status every `RESTART_POLL_MS` (3 s) and answers `'restarted'` after a failed poll followed by an answer (or after ten answers in a row, when the restart was faster than a poll) and `'slow'` after 100 polls; before answering `'restarted'` it refreshes an open session once (`AuthStore.refresh()`, a refusal ignored), because the access token's audience is `PUBLIC_URL` + `/api` and a changed site address makes the restarted backend refuse the old token with `auth/unauthenticated`, which the interceptor does not refresh; `SettingName` lists the setting names of the contract. `setting-labels.ts`: `SETTING_LABELS` (Czech label per setting, `@@operations.setting.<NAME>`), `settingLabel(name)` and `settingErrors(failure)`, which turns the `errors[]` of a 400 `operations/invalid-settings` into "Label: message" lines; shared with the administration's settings page. `test-email.ts`: `testEmailNotice(result, { host, sender })` turns a `TestEmailResponse` into the `Notice` both pages show (`@@operations.testEmail.<outcome>`): `SENT` "Zkušební e-mail odešel na {recipient}. Podívejte se prosím do schránky, i do nevyžádané pošty. Když do pár minut nedorazí, zkontrolujte adresu odesílatele a ověření její domény v Brevo." as a status, and as alerts: cannot connect to {host} (check the server name and port), {host} did not answer within 10 seconds (check the port, Brevo uses 587), no STARTTLS so the password was not sent, the sign-in refused (check the name and the SMTP key), the sender {sender} refused (verify the sender and its domain in Brevo), the recipient refused, or a general failure.
- `setup/first-setup` (`/prvni-nastaveni`, public shell): asks the status ("Zjišťujeme, jestli je web nastavený…"; a failure says to wait a minute with "Zkusit znovu"). Already set up: "Web už je nastavený" with a link to `/sprava/nastaveni`. With `codeRequired`: `h1` "První nastavení webu", a lead saying where the code is (ZimaOS → the app → terminal and logs → container `nfnn-backend`) and that the home network needs none, the field "Kód z logu" ("Opište prosím kód z logu." when empty) and "Pokračovat" ("Ověřujeme kód…"); a 403 says the code changes with every start. Otherwise, or once unlocked, the form in five numbered sections: 1. Web ("Adresa webu", filled with the page's origin when it is `https://`, else `https://nfnn.cz`; the field checks `https?://` without `?`/`#`), 2. Správce ("E-mail prvního správce", required; before the e-mails since the test e-mail goes there), 3. E-maily (server `smtp-relay.brevo.com`, port 587, name and password in `<app-password-reveal>`, both required while "Server vyžaduje přihlášení" is ticked, the checkboxes for sign-in and STARTTLS folded in `<details>` "Pokročilé nastavení serveru", sender and contact recipient `info@nfnn.cz`; at its end the hint "Zkušební e-mail přijde na e-mail prvního správce z kroku 2. …" and "Poslat zkušební e-mail" ("Posíláme zkušební e-mail…"; Jakub 2026-10-24), which first marks the involved fields that are invalid, says "Než zkušební e-mail pošleme, doplňte prosím označená pole." and focuses the first of them, and otherwise sends the typed server, sender and first administrator and shows `testEmailNotice` beside the button), 4. Banka ("Token Fio API (jen pro čtení, nepovinné)"), 5. Klíč k zálohám (Jakub 2026-10-28: the backups are encrypted and the setup does not go on until the key is saved): a lead saying the backups are encrypted with it and cannot be restored without it, the key from `SetupApi.backupKey(code)` (`GET /api/operations/setup/backup-key`, asked whenever the form opens and the key is not loaded; "Načítáme klíč k zálohám…", a failure with "Načíst znovu", 409 `backup-key-unavailable` a warning that the key appears later in Zálohy, and then the setup goes on without it) shown by `features/operations/backup-key/backup-key-panel` (`app-backup-key-panel`: "Klíč k zálohám je těchto 24 slov", an ordered list in a monospace grid (two columns on a phone) computed in the browser by `backup-key-words.ts` (with its own `bech32.ts` and synchronous `sha256.ts`, and the list `czech-wordlist.ts`); the `AGE-SECRET-KEY-1…` text itself is not shown (Jakub 2026-10-29: one form only); "Kopírovat", which copies the 24 words separated by spaces; "Stáhnout jako soubor", which saves `nfnn-klic-k-zaloham.txt`, an age identity file whose `#` lines explain how to restore and carry the 24 numbered words, so `age -d -i` takes it as it is and a printout holds both forms; a hint to keep the words in a password manager or on paper, four letters a word being enough, never only on the server), then "Mám uloženo, ověřit": the words disappear and three required fields "N. slovo klíče" ask for the positions the API drew, so they are typed from the saved copy (`backup-key-word-check.ts`: `appBackupKeyWord` compares the typed word read as the backend reads it, "Slovo nesedí s klíčem …" otherwise; autocomplete, autocorrect, autocapitalization and spellcheck off; focus on the first field), with "Ukázat slova znovu" to go back; "Uložit a spustit" before that step says at the button to save and check the words first and focuses it; the words are sent as `backupKeyWords`; a note that the web restarts, and "Uložit a spustit" ("Ukládáme…"); under the lead a "Budete potřebovat:" list (Brevo SMTP name and key, a verified sender domain, the administrator's e-mail, optionally the Fio token). Unless a test e-mail was sent with exactly the server and sender about to be saved, "Uložit a spustit" first shows the `role="alert"` warning "Zkušební e-mail s těmito údaji zatím neodešel. Bez funkčních e-mailů se správce nepřihlásí." with "Poslat zkušební e-mail" (focus to the test button, then the test) and "Uložit i tak". A 400 puts each refused value at its field (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and lists the rest under the form by label; success shows "Web se restartuje" with a spinner while `watchRestart()` runs, then "Web je nastavený" with the four next steps as a numbered list (register with the administrator's address, open the link, turn on the second factor in Údaje a zabezpečení, sign in again with the code) and "Zaregistrovat se" (opens the sign-in dialog in registration mode with that address filled in), or after 100 polls an alert pointing at the container log with "Zkusit znovu". Above the form a box offers "Obnovit ze zálohy" for a site moved or recovered from another server: the panel (a form) "Obnovit web ze zálohy" asks for the `.dump.age` file ("Soubor zálohy", `accept=".age,.dump"`; an older `.dump` is accepted too, another ending is named at once under the field, "Tohle není soubor zálohy …") and, for a `.dump.age`, the field "Klíč k zálohám" in `<app-password-reveal>` (required, the key or its 24 words; `appBackupKey` checks it with `parseBackupKey` and the canonical `AGE-SECRET-KEY-1…` form is sent); "Nahrát a obnovit" (disabled until a backup file is chosen) streams it with `SetupApi.uploadBackup(code, file, key)` (`PUT /api/operations/setup/restore`, the code in `X-Nfnn-Setup-Code`, the key in `X-Nfnn-Backup-Key`) showing "Nahráváme zálohu… N %", "Zpět k nastavení" returns. Then "Obnovujeme web ze zálohy" waits with `watchRestart(RESTORE_MAX_POLLS)` and asks the status: setup no longer required → "Web je obnovený ze zálohy" (sign in with the old administrator account; the backup brought the SMTP password and the Fio token with the backup key, and only a backup from an earlier version needs them entered again in Nastavení) with "Přihlásit se" (sign-in dialog returning to `/sprava/nastaveni`); still required and `lastRestore` of this request OK → back to the form with "Záloha je obnovená. Pochází z doby před prvním nastavením …"; failed → the restore panel with "Obnova se nepovedla: {error}. Web zůstal prázdný …"; not taken → "Kontejner záloh si obnovu do pěti minut nevzal …"; a code that changed with the restart returns to the code step first. `setup-redirect.ts` (`SetupRedirect.checkOnce()`, called by the public shell): asks the status once per load; while the setup is required it sends every other page back to `/prvni-nastaveni` and asks again after each navigation away from it, and stops once the status says it is done. Specs `first-setup.spec.ts`, `setup-api.spec.ts` and `setup-redirect.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/first-setup.spec.ts` (home network: the home page leads to the setup, saves everything and waits for the restart, the registration gets the administrator's address; the backup key shown, downloaded and three of its words sent; saving without a test e-mail warns first; the test e-mail goes with the typed values to the first administrator; leaving for the home page comes back to the setup; from elsewhere: the code unlocks the form; a backup uploaded and restored ending at the sign-in; the form at 390 px) and the axe case for the code step, the form with its errors and the backup key, and the restore panel with a refused file and an encrypted file without its key.

## donors

- `login-dialog/`: sign-in and registration through `AuthStore`; under the password a row `.session-choice` (plan v3 2.10, Jakub 2026-11-05 Q7, left out in the installed app): the checkbox "Zapamatovat si mě" (unticked, `REMEMBERED` when ticked, else `BROWSER`) and the small button "Cizí počítač?", which swaps the row for the ticked checkbox "Cizí počítač, nic neukládat" (`PUBLIC`) with the hint "Po zavření prohlížeče tu nic nezůstane." (`aria-describedby`) and "Zpět" (unticking it or "Zpět" brings the first row back); each swap moves the focus to the row's checkbox, and the chosen kind goes with the password, the passkey and the second-factor step (without a choice, such as a confirmation link's sign-in, `BROWSER`); `core/auth/public-session-idle.ts` (`PublicSessionIdle`, started at app start) signs a `PUBLIC` session out after `PUBLIC_IDLE_MS` (1 hour) without a pointer, key, touch or wheel input in any of its tabs (each tab tells the others over the session channel at most once a minute, `public-activity` in `session-tabs.ts`, and keeps the last input in `sessionStorage` `nfnn.public-activity`, so a reload continues the hour instead of starting it again), rechecked on every input and when the tab is shown again, then goes home with the signed-out notice, because the page's own refreshes keep the server's idle limit from ever running out; the problem's title and detail on failure, "Příliš mnoho pokusů z této adresy. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu." on 429; a sign-in answered `auth/second-factor-required` swaps the form for the code step. Every step after the two starting tabs has the same shape: "← Zpět na …" on top, a visible `h3`, then the content, and no tab switch; every change of step moves the focus to the step's `[autofocus]` element (the first field, or the heading of a sent panel). The code step: "← Zpět na heslo", `h3` "Ověření kódem z aplikace", "Přihlášení dokončíte kódem z ověřovací aplikace.", field "Kód z aplikace" with `inputmode="numeric"`, `autocomplete="one-time-code"` and the six-digit pattern, which posts the challenge and the code through `AuthStore.completeSecondFactor`; a wrong code keeps the step with the problem in the alert line, `auth/invalid-second-factor-challenge` returns to the password form with the problem's sentence, 429 the same sentence as the sign-in; under the code form "Nemáte telefon s aplikací? Obnovit přístup platbou" posts the challenge through `AuthApi.startSecondFactorRecovery` and replaces the step with the instructions ("← Zpět na kód" back to the focused code field, `h3` "Obnova přístupu platbou" focused, a lead asking for 1 Kč "z bankovního účtu, ze kterého jste nám už poslali dar (obvykle ten, ze kterého nám platíte)", never pointing to the overview a signed-out donor cannot open, a warning "Platbu pošlete nejdříve …" before the details while `usableFrom` is set, with a second line in the same box "Nemůžete tak dlouho čekat? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz a kód z aplikace vám vypneme." (mailto link), a warning "Tuto obnovu už jste zahájili … Pošlete platbu s tímto kódem." when the answer is `resumed` (Jakub 2026-10-24), account number and "OBNOVA …" message with copy buttons, "1 Kč", then `<app-qr-code>` with the answer's `spayd` encoded in the browser at level H (an alert "QR kód se nepodařilo vykreslit. Zaplaťte prosím podle uvedených údajů." if it cannot be) in a framed box below the details (so the details come first on a phone too), "Zpráva pro příjemce platí do …" in Prague time, and a note that the dialog may be closed because starting again while the message is valid shows the same details); 409 `donors/second-factor-recovery-unavailable` stays on the code step with the problem in the alert line; a registration answered 202 replaces the form with "Zkontrolujte si e-mail" (focused; the address in a `role="status"` line, a hint about the spam folder, "Poslat odkaz znovu" posting `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` with its own `role="status"` line (the registration, this request and the sign-in tab's "Poslat ověřovací e-mail znovu" send `returnTo: '/#prispet'` when the dialog was opened from the "Přispět" section, `LoginDialogOptions.confirmationReturnTo`, kept also after switching tabs), "Opravit e-mail" back to the kept form with the e-mail focused, "Zpět na přihlášení"); a 400 with field errors puts them at their fields (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and keeps only the title and detail in the alert line; a sign-in answered `auth/email-not-verified` keeps the problem in the alert line and adds "Poslat ověřovací e-mail znovu", which posts `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` and confirms in a `role="status"` line (its own sentence on 429); "Zapomněli jste heslo?" right under the password field opens the reset step ("← Zpět na přihlášení", `h3` "Obnovení hesla"), which posts the trimmed address through `AuthApi.requestPasswordReset` ("Zkontrolujte si e-mail" focused on 202 with the address, the hour of validity, the spam hint and "Poslat znovu"; its own sentence on 429, `describeProblem` otherwise). An address typed on one tab follows to the other and to the reset. When the browser supports passkeys, the login tab adds under the form a "nebo" divider (with `--space-6` above and `--space-5` below, so the passkey button is not hit instead of "Přihlásit se") and "Přihlásit se přístupovým klíčem" (`AuthStore.signInWithPasskey`, both buttons disabled while it runs, the form's fields made untouched so an empty e-mail shows no error; a dismissed device prompt says "Přihlášení přístupovým klíčem neproběhlo. Zkuste to prosím znovu, nebo se přihlaste heslem." and a refusal the problem, both in a `role="alert"` line under the passkey button). The registration opens with a lead saying the name (or company name, IČO and seat) goes on the receipt, no field carries an example placeholder (they read as filled-in values; labels and hints stay, as in the code field of the code step), the visible label "Registruji se jako" over "Fyzická osoba" / "Firma", PSČ before Město, name, street and city checked with `appPrintableOnReceipt` as in the profile card, IČO checked by its mod-11 checksum (`company-identifier.ts`, `appCompanyIdentifier`: "Toto IČO neexistuje, zkontrolujte prosím číslice."); a company types the IČO first (it takes the `[autofocus]`), and once it holds 8 digits with a valid check digit the dialog asks `CompanyLookupApi.lookUp` (`features/donors/company-lookup-api.ts`, `GET /api/donors/company-lookup`, 204 read as `null`; Jakub 2026-10-24) and fills the name, street, PSČ and city that are empty or still hold an earlier lookup's value, never what the donor typed, then says "Údaje jsme doplnili z obchodního rejstříku (ARES). Zkontrolujte je." in an always rendered `role="status"` region (`.company-lookup`); a 204, any error or a lookup still running when the registration is sent, the type switches to a person or the dialog closes changes nothing and shows nothing (no spinner, no alert, the registration never waits for it), the consent above "Vytvořit účet" ("Vytvářím účet…"). When the browser supports passkeys, it asks "Jak se budete přihlašovat" with an always visible hint explaining a passkey (radio group "Heslem" / "Přístupovým klíčem"); with the key the password field gives way to a hint and the request sends `password: null`. `donor-field-messages.ts` holds the name, street, city and postal code messages shared with the profile card.
- `email-verification/` (`/potvrzeni-emailu#token=…`): reads the token once and removes it with `Location.replaceState`; posts `AuthStore.confirmEmail` at once ("Potvrzujeme váš e-mail" with a spinner and a `role="status"` line); on 200 the donor is signed in, "E-mail je potvrzený" takes focus and "Přejít do přehledu" navigates to `/darce/prehled`; a link with `returnTo=%2F%23prispet` (checked by `core/auth/confirmation-return.ts`, anything else ignored) says the QR code now carries the symbol and offers "Pokračovat k příspěvku" to `/#prispet` instead, with a second, soft link "Přejít na můj přehled" (`a.overview-link` to `/darce/prehled`) under it, and its new-link form and "Přihlásit se" keep that destination; `donors/invalid-email-verification-token` and a missing token show a form asking for the address that posts `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` ("E-mail odeslán" on 202 with the spam hint and "Na úvodní stránku", the reset's 429 sentence, `describeProblem` otherwise); the invalid link adds "Už jste e-mail potvrdili? Pak se stačí přihlásit." with "Přihlásit se" (the dialog returning to `/darce/prehled`); another failure shows "E-mail se nepodařilo potvrdit" with the reason in `role="alert"` and "Zkusit znovu"; each panel's `<h1 id="email-verification-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch. For an account without a password the confirmed panel asks "Teď si vytvořte přístupový klíč…" with "Vytvořit přístupový klíč" (options with `{}`, the device, `addPasskey` named by `deviceName`; then "Přístupový klíč je uložený…" and "Přejít do přehledu"), a refusal in `role="alert"` saying it can be done later, and "Přejít do přehledu bez klíče". The link of an e-mail change carries `&change=email` (`changesEmail`): the page posts it with `AuthApi.confirmEmailChange` to `/api/donors/auth/email-change/confirm` (204, no session), signs out a session the browser still holds (the change ended it on the server), and its confirmed panel says "E-mail je změněný", "Nová adresa je potvrzená. Přihlaste se jí, heslem nebo přístupovým klíčem jako dosud. Kvůli bezpečnosti jsme vás odhlásili na všech zařízeních." and offers only "Přihlásit se" (the dialog returning to `/darce/prehled`), never the passkey step.
- `connect-app/` (`/pripojeni?client_id=…&redirect_uri=…&code_challenge=…&scope=…&resource=…&state=…`, the consent page of the MCP connector, public shell): reads the OAuth request from the query (`oauth-scopes.ts` names the known scopes "Čtení: …" and "Změny: …" for the connected applications of the account card and lists, in `SCOPE_ABILITIES`, what each lets the application do in plain words for the consent); without `client_id`, `redirect_uri` or `code_challenge` it says the request is incomplete; a visitor sees "Aplikace žádá o přístup ke správě" with "Přihlásit se", which opens the dialog (staying on the page) and the page reacts to `AuthStore.status()`; a signed-in donor without `ADMIN` sees "Připojit aplikaci může jen správce" with "Přihlásit se jiným účtem" (signs out, opens the dialog, and the page goes on to the consent after the sign-in) and "Odmítnout a vrátit se do aplikace"; an administrator sees "Ověřujeme aplikaci" while `AuthApi.describeClient` runs, then the consent ("Povolit přístup ke správě aplikaci claude.ai?", who the application says it is, the administrator's name, what reading allows as a list, the changes (`admin:write`, when asked for) as the checkbox "Povolit i změny" unticked by default with their list and the hint that the extent changes only by connecting again, a paragraph "Nikdy nebude smět …" naming the web-only actions, where to disconnect (Údaje a zabezpečení → Připojené aplikace); for a loopback redirect a note that the application runs on this computer) with a fresh confirmation: for an administrator with the code from the app the field "Kód z aplikace" (`#connect-app-code`, six digits, "Povolení potvrďte kódem z ověřovací aplikace.", the hint asks to wait for the next code) and "Povolit", plus under the field "Nebo bez kódu: Povolit přístupovým klíčem" (a link button) when the browser supports passkeys; an administrator with the code who also has a passkey (`AuthStore.hasPasskeys()`, asked before the consent shows) gets the passkey first instead, with "Povolit přístupovým klíčem" as the main button and "Nebo bez přístupového klíče: Zadat kód z aplikace" (Jakub 2026-10-29); for one without the code only "Povolit přístupovým klíčem" (disabled with an explanation in a browser without passkeys), which runs `AuthStore.confirmWith(null)`; and "Odmítnout", all posting `AuthApi.authorize` (an approval carries only the granted scopes and `secondFactorCode` or `passkey`; a refused code or key, or a cancelled device, shows a `role="alert"` line in the consent and focuses the code with its text selected, so the next code replaces it) and leaving through `ExternalNavigation.assign(redirectTo)` ("Vracíme vás do aplikace…"); a refused client or a failed request shows the problem with "Zkusit znovu"; each panel's `<h1 id="connect-app-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch.
- `password-reset/` (`/obnova-hesla#token=…`): reads the token once and removes it with `Location.replaceState`; asks for the new password once, in `<app-password-reveal>`; posts `AuthApi.confirmPasswordReset`; on 204 calls `AuthStore.logout()` if a session is held and offers the sign-in dialog; `donors/invalid-password-reset-token` and a missing token offer the dialog's reset tab (`LoginDialogService.open('reset')`); other failures show `describeProblem` in the form's alert line; each panel's `<h1 id="password-reset-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch. With passkey support the page is "Obnovte si přístup" with a radio group "Nové heslo" / "Nový přístupový klíč"; the key form has "Název klíče" (from `deviceName`) and, after `donors/second-factor-code-required`, "Kód z aplikace" (focused, the server's sentence in the alert line); "Vytvořit klíč" posts `AuthApi.passkeyRecoveryOptions`, lets the device create the key and posts `recoverWithPasskey`, then "Přístupový klíč je uložený" offers the sign-in dialog; an administrator's refusal shows the backend's title and detail.
- `dashboard/`: `symbol-card/` (variable symbol with a copy button, "Přispět" to `/#prispet`, where the home page puts the symbol into the QR; "Přispět znovu" once the input `hasDonated` is true). `profile-card/` ("Údaje na potvrzení o daru": name, IČO for a company, address, receipt language; "Upravit údaje" turns it into a form (street, then PSČ and Město in the order of the registration) saved with "Uložit údaje" sending name, address and `preferredLocale` through `AuthStore.updateProfile` (`PATCH /api/donors/me`); e-mail and IČO read-only; focus to the first field on edit and back to the button after save or cancel; the hint says a new language reaches only receipts downloaded for the first time; name, street and city carry `appPrintableOnReceipt` (`features/donors/printable-on-receipt.ts`, `printedText` of `receipt-characters.ts`, whose spec runs the shared cases of `receipts/printable.json`), so a character no receipt prints is refused at its field before anything is sent ("Obsahuje znak, který na potvrzení o daru nevytiskneme, například emoji nebo čínské písmo. Napište prosím tento údaj latinkou, cyrilicí nebo řeckým písmem."), and a 400 with field errors puts them at their fields (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and keeps only the title and detail, with any error no field owns, in the form's alert line; a `role="status"` line confirms the save; `sessionEnded` is answered like a failed load). `account-card/` ("Přihlášení a zabezpečení", Jakub 2026-10-24): one summary list (`dl.account-data`, one `.account-row` per item: label, state, one action; up to 768 px the state goes under the label and the action) with the rows E-mail (the address; while `pendingEmailChange` is set "Čeká na potvrzení: <adresa>" in amber, "Potvrzovací odkaz platí do …" and "Poslat znovu" (`AuthStore.resendEmailChange`, "Posíláme…") and the quiet "Zrušit" (`AuthStore.cancelEmailChange`, "Rušíme…", focus back to "Změnit e-mail"), their outcome in the card's status line and a refusal as an alert under them; action "Změnit e-mail"), Heslo ("Nastavené", "Bez hesla" or "Bez hesla, jen přístupové klíče"; "Změnit heslo" or "Nastavit heslo", plus the quiet "Odebrat heslo" for a donor with a password and a key), Dvoufaktorové ověření ("Zapnuté, kód z ověřovací aplikace" / "Vypnuté", with a passkey and the code off the note "Přihlášení přístupovým klíčem je chráněné i bez kódu.", for an administrator without it the amber hint "Správa se vám otevře, až se přihlásíte přístupovým klíčem nebo zapnete kód z aplikace a přihlásíte se s ním."; "Zapnout/Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření") and Přístupové klíče ("Žádný", "1 klíč", "2 klíče", "5 klíčů"; "Spravovat přístupové klíče"). Each action is a disclosure (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` to the row's `dd.account-panel`, which spans the row): opening one closes any other, the focus moves to the form's first field (to the passkeys panel for keys), and cancel or success returns it to the row's button; every visible action word carries the rest of its name as hidden text. The forms are the earlier ones: "Zapnout kód z aplikace" asks the current password (`AuthStore.setupSecondFactor`) after saying what a lost phone means, since there are no backup codes (Jakub 2026-10-29): "Když telefon s aplikací ztratíte, přístup obnovíte platbou 1 Kč z bankovního účtu, který u nás máte ověřený aspoň týden. Dřív to nejde; do té doby nám napište a kód z aplikace vám vypneme." (the 7-day rule of `SecondFactorRecoveryPolicy`, the administrator's reset otherwise), then shows the QR code (`app-qr-code`, plain, 200 px, encoded in the browser from the enrolment's `otpauthUri` at level M; a URI the encoder refuses, such as one with a character outside ISO-8859-1, shows instead the alert "QR kód se nepodařilo vykreslit. Opište prosím klíč níže do ověřovací aplikace." and the key and the code field stay), the link "Přidat do ověřovací aplikace v tomto telefonu nebo tabletu" to the enrolment's `otpauthUri` (under the QR, above it up to 480 px), the key in groups of four with a copy button and the code field posting `AuthStore.confirmSecondFactor`, which adopts the new session, and the status line says the factor is on and the other sign-ins ended; "Vypnout kód z aplikace" asks the password and a code (`AuthStore.disableSecondFactor`); the e-mail form sends the new address and the current password through `AuthStore.requestEmailChange`, after 202 the status line says the link went to the new address and the current one stays until it is opened, and the store reads the profile again so the pending change shows (a failed reload changes nothing); the password form posts `AuthStore.changePassword`, which adopts the new session; refusals are form-level alerts and `sessionEnded` is answered like the profile card. For an account without a password and without a passkey (`passkeyCount` 0) the rows offer no action but "Spravovat přístupové klíče", and the amber callout "Účet zatím nemá heslo ani přístupový klíč. Nejdřív si vytvořte přístupový klíč, …" with the primary "Vytvořit přístupový klíč" opens the passkeys row with its form (no button where the browser cannot create passkeys). With a key the password form replaces every password field with "Po odeslání vás zařízení požádá o potvrzení jedním z vašich přístupových klíčů." (`AuthStore.confirmWith`). `passkeys-panel/` (`app-passkeys-panel`, the Přístupové klíče row's panel, a region named by the row label through `labelledBy`, `focusTarget()` and `collapse()` for the card; it reports the count through `countChanged`): an intro opening with the benefit ("Přihlaste se otiskem prstu, obličejem nebo zámkem obrazovky místo hesla."), naming Apple, Google and Windows Hello and saying a passkey sign-in is protected without the code from the app; the keys from `AuthApi.passkeys` with "přidáno … · naposledy použito …" or "zatím nepoužito", "Přejmenovat" opening an inline "Nový název klíče" field (prefilled, focused) with "Uložit název" / "Zrušit" posting `AuthApi.renamePasskey`; "Odebrat" with the inline "Odebrat klíč <název>? Přihlásit se jím pak nepůjde." and "Ano, odebrat" / "Ponechat"; "Přidat přístupový klíč" opens a form with "Název klíče" (prefilled from the user agent) and the current password, "Vytvořit klíč" ("Čekáme na zařízení…") asks for the options, lets the device create the key and posts it; outcomes in a `role="status"` line, refusals and the device's own answers as a form alert; without browser support a sentence replaces the button; for an account without a password the form asks no password, the only key shows "Jediný způsob přihlášení…" instead of "Odebrat", one key brings the advice to add a second device and none an amber warning; the first key of a donor with a password also says the password can now be removed at the Heslo row. `connected-apps-card/` ("Připojené aplikace", its own card after the account card, only for an administrator): `AuthApi.connections`, one row per connection with the host, "připojeno … · naposledy použito …", the permissions as a list and "Odpojit" (`AuthApi.disconnect`, "Odpojujeme…", then a `role="status"` line that the application is disconnected and cannot act any more), "Žádná aplikace není připojená. …" without one, a failed load in `role="alert"` with "Načíst znovu"; `sessionEnded` like the other cards. `device-data-card/` ("Data v tomto zařízení", after the account card, plan v3 2.14): one state line ("Uloženo v zařízení · aktualizováno <datum> <čas>" in Prague time, the date and the time each kept whole but free to wrap apart, "Data se ukládají do zařízení…" during the first sync, "Kopie v tomto zařízení je zamčená. …" when `DeviceVault.locked()`, which also drops "Načíst data znovu", "V tomto zařízení zatím nic uloženo není.", or in memory-only mode "V tomto prohlížeči se nic neukládá. …", which also drops the sentence about the encrypted copy and the delete button), the sentence that the copy of gifts, receipts and bank accounts is encrypted with a key only this device knows, that the server does not change, and when sign-out keeps it (password: always; passkey: only when the key gives PRF), "Načíst data znovu" (`DonorCopySync.reload`, every chunk again, "Načítáme data…", disabled while running; `appNeedsConnection`, so offline it is held with the hint "Potřebuje připojení" and no request; the outcome in `app-live-notice`, `failed` blaming the connection, `mismatch` saying the server's data could not be verified ("Data ze serveru se nepodařilo ověřit. …") and `no-copy` naming info@nfnn.cz) and "Smazat data z tohoto zařízení" (danger outline), which opens `delete-device-data-dialog` ("Smazat data z tohoto zařízení?", the lead that the copy goes from this device, the donor is signed out here, the server keeps everything and the copy comes back at the next sign-in, "Smazat data a odhlásit" / "Mažeme data…" and "Zrušit", which takes the focus; `app-dialog [dismissible]="false"` while deleting, so Esc, × and the backdrop do nothing): it calls `DeviceVault.forget`, then `AuthStore.logout`, goes home and shows the signed-out notice with the reason `device-data-deleted` ("Data z tohoto zařízení jsme smazali a odhlásili jsme vás.", Q6); a failed deletion keeps the dialog open with an alert and signs nobody out. Every busy label speaks as "we" ("Ukládáme…", "Odebíráme…", "Ověřujeme kód…").
- `bank-accounts-card/` ("Moje bankovní účty", on the section Bankovní účty): a text button "Jak přiřazujeme platby?" with a round "?" icon (`aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) expands a `role="region"` panel (`pairing-help/`, inputs `variableSymbol` and `open`, `closeRequested` on Escape), collapsed by default, answering short questions, each in at most two sentences (how a gift arrives, a bank without a symbol field (write the symbol into the message for the recipient), a payment without the symbol, a standing order with an old symbol, the answer to the question, the shared account, how to stop pairing, the exceptions: another donor's symbol, symbol 0, collection accounts; then the e-mail) in true-to-backend Czech with the donor's symbol; Esc collapses and refocuses; the text is capped at 520 px. One row per account with status label, hint, "ověřeno …", "vypnuto od …" (the day after `disconnectedOn`) and the gifts without a symbol (`donationCount` counts only donations paired by account), plus a line per `confirmedSymbols` entry. A row with `symbolOffer` shows `symbol-claim/` (`app-symbol-claim`: inputs `accountId`, `offer`, `donorName`, `busy`; output `answer`): a `role="group"` named by the question "Z tohoto účtu k nám chodí i platby s variabilním symbolem … (14 plateb, leden 2025 – říjen 2026). Jsou to dary od vás (Jana Nováková)?" (never an amount) with "Ano, jsou moje" (described by the note that the answer cannot be changed) and "Ne, nejsou moje"; while saving both are `aria-disabled` and the pressed one `aria-busy`; the answered offer is dropped at once. "Ano" posts `confirmSymbol`, emits `donationsChanged` when `pairedCount` is above 0 (the area refetches summary and list) and announces "Přiřadili jsme vám … plateb se symbolem … z účtu …" plus "Potvrzení za rok … se změnilo, stáhněte si nové." for `changedYearlyReceipts`; "Ne" posts `declineSymbol`. While no account is verified (`ACTIVE`) and none carries a question, a short nudge under the list says a 1 Kč from the standing order's account brings the older gifts too; an empty card says the account appears by itself once a gift with the symbol comes from it, and that a gift sent without the symbol, with another one or before the account existed needs the account added through "Přidat účet" (earlier gifts from it usually follow), then, in two sentences, that a standing order is best switched to the donor's symbol, else a 1 Kč through "Přidat účet". The public `focusTitle()` focuses the card heading; the public `openAddDialog()` (used by the section when the address carries `?pridat=ucet`, in the `read` phase of `afterNextRender` so it runs after the page title took the focus) focuses "Přidat účet" first, so closing returns there, and opens the dialog unless the donor has no symbol. A shared account's hint is an amber warning box (`account-hint--warning`, `--color-warning*`, hidden "Upozornění:") that never names the other donor, in the list and in the add dialog alike. The buttons and chips avoid the word "párování": a `pairingExcluded` account keeps its chip ("Ověřený" / "Nepřiřazujeme"); its live row says only payments with the symbol pair now; an active one offers no "Nepřiřazovat platby z tohoto účtu", an `AWAITING_PAYMENT` one reads "Jen s variabilním symbolem" and keeps that button, a disconnected one offers no "Znovu přiřazovat platby z účtu" and says why on its newest row. "Nepřiřazovat platby z tohoto účtu" (set apart by `--space-5` above it up to 768 px while the row asks a question) asks an inline confirmation "Ano, nepřiřazovat" / "Ponechat" (focus on "Ponechat"; with confirmed symbols it says those stop pairing too); "Znovu přiřazovat platby z účtu" shows only on the newest row of its account number; both reload the list, announce in `role="status"` and focus the card heading; problems go through `describeProblem` in `role="alert"`, 404/409 reload. "Přidat účet" opens an `app-dialog` with one short paragraph (send 1 Kč by the details below; the account then appears and pays without the symbol), right under it the watch box, then while nothing new is verified one line naming the accounts already verified (`ACTIVE`, not shared, not symbol-only) that need no 1 Kč ("Účet … už máte ověřený, 1 Kč z něj posílat nemusíte." / "Účty … a … už máte ověřené, …"; the dialog cannot know which account the donor means, testers of 2026-10-30), then the branded QR (`app-qr-code`, 200 px) that `spayd()` writes from `PaymentDetailsStore` with `accountVerificationAmountMinor`, the donor's symbol and `ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION` and `encodeText` draws at level H (when the details did not load the error with "Zkusit znovu", when they loaded but are refused "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Zkuste to prosím znovu za chvíli.", both retrying through `PaymentDetailsStore.reload()`; when the donor's own variable symbol is what `spayd()` refuses, "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Napište nám prosím na info@nfnn.cz." with no retry, which could not help), and account, amount, message "Ověření účtu Nadační fond Nová naděje" and symbol, each but the amount with a copy button (up to 480 px the details come before the QR), under them the line that a bank without a field for the variable symbol takes it in the message ("Ověření účtu Nadační fond Nová naděje <symbol>", pairing rule 9), and a closed `<details>` "Kdy to funguje a co se stane s dřívějšími dary" (Czech accounts only, no collection account, earlier gifts for the first verifier, a standing order with an older symbol needs exactly 1 Kč with that message, the timing); it watches in the `role="status"` box ("Čekáme na vaši platbu…" with a spinner): 30 s after opening and 30 s after each check ended (the backend's global pause) it runs `checkBankUntilDone()` and reloads the list, emitting `bankChecked`; an account `ACTIVE` now that was not when the dialog opened turns the box into a drawn tick "Účet … je ověřený" (plus "Přiřadili jsme vám také N dřívější dary z tohoto účtu, celkem … Kč." from the row's `donationCount` and `totalAmountMinor` when it brought any) and ends the watch, and so does a changed `verificationPaymentOn` ("Platba z účtu … dorazila"); the result then replaces the intro, the QR, the payment details and the `<details>`, takes the focus (`tabindex="-1"`) and the close button reads "Hotovo"; with a `symbolOffer` the dialog asks the question itself (`app-symbol-claim` under the box, hidden from that account's row meanwhile, so it stands once) and announces the answer in its own `role="status"` line, which takes the focus (a refused answer shows its `role="alert"` in the dialog); a hidden tab pauses and checks at once when visible; while the dialog is open a `bank-accounts` hint reads the list at once, so a verification the hourly import or another check brought shows without waiting for the next 30 s, and a tick ends the watch; after 20 checks "Platba zatím nedorazila" with "Kontrolovat dál"; animations stop under `prefers-reduced-motion`. A payment paired by `SENDER_ACCOUNT` carries the tag "bez symbolu · účet …3333/2010" in its purpose cell and one paired by `CONFIRMED_SYMBOL` "symbol 6123456789 · účet …5399/0800" (the full sentence as hidden text, no `title`).
- Donor area (`donor-area`, 2.23): the app header and the tab bar of the public pages (Layout), the header's top padding as room above the content and `--tab-bar-clearance` below it; on wide screens the side panel "Můj účet" with the sections Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, Bankovní účty and Údaje a zabezpečení, and for an administrator the group "Pro správce" with Správa, around the routed section; on phones (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, the `side-nav` with `[panel]="false"`, so one `router-outlet` serves both widths and a page keeps its state when an iPad turns) no panel, the account sheet leads between the sections and the header's "Zpět" leads home; the routed section and the help line "Máte dotaz k platbě? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz" (Jakub 2026-10-30 kept it without a second link to the contact form, because two colour-only links in one line would need underlining). It provides `donor-donations.ts` (`DonorDonations`, one per visit of the area, so switching sections loads nothing again and a new sign-in starts empty; only opening Přehled or Moje dary more than 30 s after the last bank check ended (the backend's pause, measured from the device's clock) starts the same silent check as opening the area, through `checkBankIfStale()`, and nothing while one runs): the bank accounts from `BankAccountsApi.list()` once on open (a failure leaves none; the bank accounts card replaces them with every list it shows or answer it records, through its output `accountsChanged`), whose accounts with a `symbolOffer` are the overview's question callouts and the badge of "Bankovní účty" in the side panel and, through `AccountBadges`, on the account button and the sheet's row (Jakub 2026-10-24: the count, read as "1 otázka čeká na odpověď", "2 otázky čekají na odpověď", "5 otázek čeká na odpověď" through `pluralCategory`); summary and list from `DonationsApi` on open (`state` loading/ready/error with retry, shown by `donations-pending/`; a 401 `auth/unauthenticated` or a session lost by a failed refresh sends the donor home with the sign-in dialog, returning to the same section), the bank check (on open and on "Zkontrolovat nové platby" it posts `requestBankCheck`, reads it again while `checking` as `checkBankUntilDone()` does, refetches summary and list whenever a check ends, keeps `checkingBank` until they arrived, ignores an answer overtaken by a later fetch, and counts `bankDataArrived` when `bankDataAsOf` changed or the donor asked, so the bank accounts section reloads its list; after "Zkontrolovat nové platby" `bankCheckOutcome` says how many `RECEIVED` gifts are new, none, that the bank is still busy after the last poll, or that the check failed). `bank-check/` (`app-bank-check`, on the overview's bank line and in Moje dary) shows "Kontrolujeme nové platby v bance…" or the outcome ("Přibyl 1 nový dar." / "Přibyly 3 nové dary." / "Přibylo 5 nových darů.", "Žádný nový dar zatím nepřišel. Banky se ptáme nejvýš jednou za 30 sekund, zkuste to proto za chvíli znovu." (the backend's `on-demand-cooldown`), "Banka zatím neodpověděla. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu.") before "Údaje z banky k …" in one always rendered `role="status"` line, the button "Zkontrolovat nové platby" (disabled while checking) and, for a failed check, the `role="alert"` "Nové platby se nepodařilo zkontrolovat. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu."; leaving the section clears the outcome. From the device copy (2.15): `DonorDonations` reads summary and gifts only through `DonorDonationsRepository` (`features/donations/device-copy/donor-donations-repository.ts`, root): `held()` is the open copy's view while it agrees with its summary (a copy that disagrees counts as absent, so the page reads the API instead of a list that contradicts its totals), and `fresh('catch-up' | 'sync')` answers from the copy after that catch-up or sync stored it and from the API (`/api/donations/me/summary` and `/api/donations/me`) when there is no open copy, when it is still empty (its catch-up then runs beside) or when the sync did not store it, so a sync that fails, times out or finds the session gone never leaves the page without data. A copy that holds the gifts is shown at once and caught up, and what the catch-up brings waits behind the "Zobrazit N nový dar" button like a live hint; the reload that ends an automatic bank check (on opening the area) offers its gifts the same way while the page shows the copy, so a catch-up's button is not replaced by a list that moves; every other reload (the end of a bank check the donor asked for, a receipt whose row is gone) syncs and shows its answer directly; a copy that opens after the page asked the API is shown as soon as it opens (also after the API failed), unless the API's answer on the page is newer than the copy; a copy that opens for another donor than the one the page showed (a session that changed donor in place; a read from the API names the donor signed in when it was asked) replaces the list at once instead of offering its gifts as new ones; a failed reload keeps a list shown from the copy instead of the error. A receipt is drawn through `copyBackedReceiptSource`, so the copy's active receipt and its kept images are drawn again when the server cannot be reached (status 0, or a gateway status 502–504 or 520–524 without a problem body). The bank accounts still come from the API (2.15 kept the Bankovní účty page on it). `first-sync-notice/` (`app-first-sync-notice`, in the donor shell, an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region without `role="status"`, as `update-notice`): when the first sync of an empty copy takes longer than a second, a bar fixed at the bottom of the screen (`ui-notice--floating` in a neutral look, the page kept clear of it through `keepRoomForFloatingNotice`, so nothing on the page moves) says "Ukládáme přehled vašich darů do tohoto zařízení…", and when it stored the copy, in the status look, "Přehled vašich darů je uložený v tomto zařízení." for 4 s; nothing for a quick sync, a failed one, a copy that already held the gifts or a device that keeps nothing (memory only, "Cizí počítač"). It promises nothing offline: until the copy opens without a connection (after 2.7), the offline-unlock PR adds that sentence under a new id. Live hints (2.16): on `donations` `DonorDonations` reads summary and list (with a copy: syncs it) in the background and shows them at once unless they bring gifts the page does not show yet, which wait behind a button at the end of the same status line, "Zobrazit 1 nový dar" / "Zobrazit 3 nové dary" / "Zobrazit 5 nových darů" (`incomingCount`, `showIncoming()`, the focus then on "Zkontrolovat nové platby"), so the list never moves under the donor's finger; while a bank check runs the button is hidden (its end reloads everything anyway, and the focus would land on the disabled check button); a check the donor starts, or any full reload, shows everything at once and drops the button. On `bank-accounts` it reads the bank accounts again (the badge and the questions), on `bank-import` the time of the bank data (`GET` of the bank check, unless a check runs), and after a reopened stream all three. `missing-gift/` (`app-missing-gift`, in the empty states of Přehled (at the top of "Jak poslat první dar", above its steps, so a donor who already paid reads it first), Moje dary and Potvrzení o daru): the `h3` "Poslali jste dar a nevidíte ho?", two sentences (a payment without the symbol, with another one or from before the account is not paired yet; verifying the account it came from with 1 Kč usually brings the earlier gifts) and "Přidat účet, ze kterého jste platili" to `/darce/ucty?pridat=ucet`, which opens the add dialog and drops the query (`replaceUrl`). `standing-order/` (`app-standing-order`, input `variableSymbol`): the text button "Jak nastavit trvalý příkaz" (`aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) opens a `role="region"` named by it, scrolls the button with the opened guide into view (`scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` after render, so the opening never happens below the screen), with the account number (input `account` from the card's `app-payment-panel`, otherwise, as in Moje dary, from `PaymentDetailsStore`; "Načítáme číslo účtu…", on failure a `role="alert"` "Číslo účtu se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." with "Zkusit znovu", which loads again or, with `account`, emits `retryRequested` for the card), the donor's symbol, both with copy buttons, "<amount> Kč, nebo jiná, kterou chcete posílat" (input `amount` from the card's panel) or, without an amount, "Jakákoli, kterou chcete posílat", "Každý měsíc, nebo jak často chcete", that every payment then appears by itself and counts in the yearly receipt, that the amount and the day are changed only in the donor's bank and the site needs no setting, and for an older symbol the advice to switch it or verify the account under "Bankovní účty" (link); Esc closes and refocuses the button., the receipt is drawn in the browser by `ReceiptPdf` (plan 1.9; a running one marks its button `aria-disabled="true"` and `aria-busy` (the button keeps the keyboard focus, so a drawing of several seconds never drops it to the page, and a press meanwhile starts nothing); `yearlyProgress(year)` / `singleProgress(item)` hand its state to `<app-download-progress class="receipt-saved">` under its button in the table row, the year heading of Moje dary, the year row of Potvrzení o daru and the card of Přehled: "Připravujeme údaje potvrzení…", "Načítáme nástroje pro PDF…", "Vytváříme PDF…" (the status line changes only per phase, so a screen reader hears three announcements, not one per page; the bar moves by page against `estimatedReceiptPages(gifts)` of `receipt-document.ts`, which the layout spec holds to the real page count of every case, with "Strana N" beside it as its `aria-valuetext`), then `PdfDelivery.save` and "Potvrzení <file> je stažené. Najdete ho ve stažených souborech.", or on an iPhone or iPad that shares files "Potvrzení <file> je připravené. Otevřete ho tlačítkem Otevřít potvrzení." (under a payment's row "… tlačítkem Otevřít PDF.") while the same button now reads "Otevřít potvrzení za rok <year> (PDF)" (in the payments table "Otevřít PDF") and takes the focus unless the donor moved it elsewhere while the PDF was drawn (after a save, a failure, a cancel and on ready the button gets the focus back only when it fell to the page or still stands where it stood when the download started); a ready file is dropped, with the finished lines, as soon as the signed-in donor's name or address changes (`AuthStore.donor`), because the next issue replaces that receipt, while a change of the language keeps it; tapping it hands the file to the share sheet and issues nothing again ("Potvrzení <file> jste předali z nabídky sdílení."; "… je stažené …" when the browser saved it instead or sharing failed; a closed sheet keeps it ready); "Zrušit" from 8 s on ends it ("Přípravu potvrzení jste zrušili.", the `cancelled` state in the neutral colour) and returns the focus to the download button; every download keeps its own line (`receiptTasks`, one per year or gift), and a new download clears only the finished lines of others; `tools-missing` reloads the page once through `ReceiptRestart`, and after its first load the section resumes the download named by `?stahnout=` only when this tab's `sessionStorage` marker names that same download (`ReceiptRestart.resumes`), so a link with the parameter issues nothing; it removes the parameter either way; every other end of that download (saved, failed for another reason, cancelled) clears the marker (`ReceiptRestart.finished`, `abandoned(download)`), so a later link cannot issue it without a click; a miss after that one reload (`tryRestart` answers `reloaded-already`) says "Nástroje pro PDF se nepodařilo načíst ani po obnovení stránky. Zkontrolujte prosím připojení nebo blokování skriptů, zkuste jiný prohlížeč, nebo nám napište na info@nfnn.cz.", and a browser without session storage (`unavailable`, no reload) "Web se mezitím aktualizoval. Obnovte prosím stránku a stáhněte potvrzení znovu.", an image that fails its digest "Podpis na potvrzení se nepodařilo ověřit, a tak jsme PDF nevytvořili…", a drawing failure "PDF se v prohlížeči nepodařilo vytvořit…") and their failures (`receiptError` with its scope: the problem title for `donations/year-not-yet-completed`, `donations/yearly-receipt-not-available` and `donations/receipt-before-first-year`, the title of a 404 or `donations/receipt-before-first-year` on a single receipt after which summary and list are fetched again, the title and detail of `donations/receipt-unprintable`, of `donations/receipt-signing-paused` (slice 1.13), of `donations/receipt-signing-key-unpublished` (slice 1.22) and of `donations/receipt-signatory-missing`, `donations/receipt-issue-limit` and `donations/receipt-too-long` (slice 1.21: each says why a retry cannot help now and what to do instead), else "Potvrzení se nepodařilo stáhnout. Zkuste to prosím znovu."). Every section starts with `page-title/` (`<h1 tabindex="-1">`, focused on open). `overview/` (Přehled), the same order on phones and desktops (Jakub 2026-10-24): first the notices, a card "Potvrzení o darech za rok … je připravené" with "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" when the donor gave in `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` and that year is not before `receiptsAvailableFrom`, and an amber callout per bank account with a `symbolOffer` ("Máme na vás otázku k platbám z účtu …" with "Odpovědět" to `/darce/ucty`, from the area's bank accounts); then for a donor with gifts (an account verification alone is none, Jakub 2026-10-29) the card "Poslední dar" (the amount, date and state of the newest item that is not an account verification, then `app-bank-check` with the note on the hourly search and bank delays), the card "Poslat dar" (`send-gift/`, Jakub 2026-10-29: the `h2`, a lead that the symbol is already in the details and the QR, `app-payment-panel` with the donor's symbol and the standing order guide fed by it, projected right after the bank details and before the QR (testers of 2026-10-30 missed it under the QR); one instance outside the state branches, so loading the donations never asks for the QR again; it replaced the symbol card and every link to `/#prispet`), one row of stats as a `dl` without icons (Celkem darováno, Počet darů (the summary's count without the `RECEIVED` account verifications, which a smaller "a ověření účtu" names under it), "Darováno v roce …" for the year after `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough`, 0 Kč without a gift that year; three columns, up to 640 px three lines of label and value) and "Poslední dary" (the newest five through `donations-table/` without the receipt column, "Všechny dary" to `/darce/dary`); for a donor without a gift "Jak poslat první dar" takes the place of the last gift and the stats (three steps pointing to the card "Poslat dar" below and naming the donor's symbol, then the bank line), followed by "Poslat dar"; while loading or after a failure the loading line or its alert stands above "Poslat dar". Last comes `install-card/` (`app-install-card`, plan v3 2.4, only while `AppInstall.offered()`): the `region` "Nová naděje jako aplikace" with a lead, for `ios-share` and `mac-dock` the steps in Safari and that the app asks to sign in once more (an app added to the Home Screen keeps its own storage), for `prompt` "Nainstalovat aplikaci", which opens the browser's install question, and "Teď ne", which hides the offer for 90 days; it never takes the focus as it appears (it can come at any moment, when the browser fires `beforeinstallprompt`), and after either button the card is replaced by its closing line ("Aplikace se instaluje…" or "Dobře, nabídku aplikace teď skryjeme…"), which takes the focus, so it is not lost with the pressed button. It is offered only to a signed-in donor (plan 2.1), never on the public site. `gifts/` (Moje dary): "Všechny dary" with `app-bank-check` and, with gifts, the sentence that single receipts come at once, the yearly one from 1 January of the next year and only one kind goes into the tax return (the yearly one holds every gift of the year), then the gifts grouped by year, newest first (Jakub 2026-10-24): each year a `section` named by its `h3` (the year) with "3 dary · 1 500 Kč" from the summary's `byYear` (through `gift-count/` (`app-gift-count`, also in the year rows of Potvrzení o daru; `receivedVerifications(items, year)` counts the `RECEIVED` account verifications, which are named apart: "1 dar a ověření účtu · 2 001 Kč", "Ověření účtu · 1 Kč" alone, their amount staying in the sum) and, by `yearReceipt(year, summary)` (`dashboard/yearly-receipt.ts`, also used by Potvrzení o daru, from `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` and `receiptsAvailableFrom`, never the client clock), "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" for a year with its receipt, "Roční potvrzení za rok … stáhnete po skončení roku, od 1. ledna …." for a later year and nothing before the first receipt year; the year's gifts through `donations-table/` named by the year heading, with "Stáhnout PDF" where `receiptAvailable` (a `REFUNDED` row says "Vrácený dar potvrzení nemá", an account verification "Započítá se do ročního potvrzení", one before the first receipt year "Potvrzení vystavujeme od roku …", and a line under the list says from when receipts are issued, verifications aside), and in the footer the note on the hourly search and bank delays and the standing order guide; without gifts "Zatím tu není žádná platba." and the missing gift block; a failed download shows its alert where it was started, a yearly one under that year's heading and a single one in the gift's receipt cell (`app-donations-table` input `errorsAtRows`, through `DonorDonations.yearlyError` and `singleError`, the error carrying the download key; Jakub 2026-10-31), so the page does not move; only when that year or gift is gone after the payments load again does the alert stand above the years and scroll into view. `receipts/` (Potvrzení o daru): an intro under the heading (single receipts at once, the yearly one from 1 January of the next year, and for the tax return either the single ones or the yearly one, not both, since the yearly one holds every gift of the year; after a change of name or address a newly downloaded receipt gets a new number and replaces the earlier one), then "Roční potvrzení o darech" as one row per summary year (year, count through `app-gift-count`, total) with "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" (download icon `aria-hidden`) up to `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough`, "Ke stažení od 1. ledna …" (the next year) after it and "Za tento rok potvrzení na webu nevystavujeme. Potřebujete ho? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz." before `receiptsAvailableFrom` (plus the line from which year receipts are issued; without such a year the line "Potvrzení tu vystavujeme za dary od roku …. Za dřívější dary nám napište na info@nfnn.cz." instead, `.yearly-earlier`), no calendar in the frontend, and without gifts a line that a gift with the symbol appears here plus the missing gift block; the card "Potvrzení k jednotlivému daru" lists the gifts with `receiptAvailable` of every year whose yearly receipt is not out yet (`yearReceipt` is `after-year-end`, so the running year) through `donations-table/` named by its heading, with "Stáhnout PDF" and its saved line per row and a failed single download as its alert (Jakub 2026-10-30: testers looked for these receipts on this page first; Moje dary keeps them too), and says older gifts' receipts are under Moje dary; without such a gift it only says single receipts are under Moje dary. Last, always shown (also while the donations load), the card "Ověřit potvrzení" (Jakub 2026-10-31: one entry in the receipts section, not a link per receipt) says the donor can verify their own receipt or one someone showed them and links to `/overit` with a `ui-button`. `bank-accounts/` (Bankovní účty): an intro and the bank accounts card wired to the area's state. `details/` (Údaje a zabezpečení): the profile card, the account card (with the passkeys panel inside), the devices card (`devices-card/`, "Přihlášená zařízení", 2.9: `AuthApi.devices()` once on open, each row named by `deviceName()` of `device-labels.ts` from the closed platform and browser lists ("iPhone · Safari", "Neznámé zařízení" when both are other), the sign-in date and the last use, "Toto zařízení" as a badge on the current one and "Odhlásit" (aria-label "Odhlásit {name}") on every other, "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" while there is another, "Jinde přihlášení nejste." when there is none; each sign-out's status line advises changing the password when the donor did not sign in there, and for an administrator the line after "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" says the connected applications stay connected (Q12); a device the server no longer finds (`donors/device-not-found`, signed out elsewhere) leaves the list like a signed-out one; focus moves to the next row's "Odhlásit", else to "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení", else to the card's heading (`tabindex="-1"`); a failure is an alert with "Načíst znovu" for the list, and a 401 `auth/session-ended` ends the session through its output), for an administrator the connected-apps card, and "Smazání účtu" (a donor cannot delete the account: an e-mail to info@nfnn.cz with a prefilled subject, what is erased and what stays for accounting, and to download the receipts first). `donations-table/`: input `labelledBy` names the table by its card heading; every payment cell carries `data-label`, so up to 640 px each row is a card (date and amount without their labels); amounts right-aligned with `--numeric-figures`, bold in the text colour (Jakub 2026-10-29: amounts in the donor overview, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, the administration's payments and the donor card are bold `--color-text`, the accent stays for links and buttons); a row with `accountVerification` (Jakub 2026-10-29) is secondary: muted text and a smaller, lighter amount, its purpose "Ověření účtu" as the API names it; each "Stáhnout PDF" carries the gift's date and amount as hidden text.

## admin

- `core/auth/admin-guard.ts` on `/sprava/**`: restores the session first; an administrator passes, a signed-in donor without the role lands on `/darce/prehled`, and the guard tells `core/auth/admin-access-refusal.ts` (`AdminAccessRefusal`, a root signal) why, so the overview opens with a warning callout in an always-rendered `role="status"`: "Tento účet nemá přístup do správy. Pokud ho potřebujete, požádejte jiného správce, aby vám ho přidělil.", or, for an administrator signed in without the second factor (`secondFactor.neededForAdministration`), "Správa se vám otevře, až se přihlásíte přístupovým klíčem nebo kódem z aplikace. Kód z aplikace si zapnete v části Údaje a zabezpečení." with a link to `/darce/udaje`; leaving the overview clears it; a visitor goes to `/` with the sign-in dialog open and returns to the asked page after signing in (like `donor-guard.ts`); the guard opens it with `forAdministration: true`, so the login tab's lead says "Pro vstup do správy se přihlaste." instead of "Přístup do vašeho přehledu příspěvků".
- `shell/admin-shell` is the parent route of every `/sprava` page (`admin.routes.ts`, plan v3 2.24, the approved mockup `ucet-a-sprava-mobil-po-revizi-2026-10-31`): the app header and the site's tab bar of the public pages and the donor area (one app, Jakub 2026-10-31; the route `sprava` has `head: 'light'`, `parent: '/'` and `backOnWide: false`), `DIALOG_PRESENTATION` `sheet`, so every `presentation="auto"` dialog of the administration is a sheet below `nav`, and between them `<app-side-nav navId="admin-nav" label="Správa" [panel]="!compact()">` around the `router-outlet`, the panel only above `nav`. `shell/admin-sections.ts` holds `ADMIN_SECTIONS`, the panel: "Přehled" (`/sprava`, exact), then groups by how often an administrator needs them (Jakub 2026-10-24): `ADMIN_DAILY` Každý den ("Platby z banky", "Dárci", "Aktuality", "Projekty"), `ADMIN_OCCASIONAL` Občas ("Hromadná potvrzení", once a year and so first there, slice 2.29, "Fotka v sekci O nás", "Lidé fondu", "Správci", "Měsíční e-mail", "Zálohy", "Upozornění na telefon", "Historie změn"), `ADMIN_TECHNICAL` Pro technika ("Nastavení", "Logy a diagnostika", "Odpovědi o starších symbolech"); a new section adds its entry there (texts `admin.nav.*`) and its route a `parent`. Every child route names its `parent` for the header's back button: the daily sections `/sprava`, a donor `/sprava/darci`, an editor its list, the occasional and technical pages `/sprava/vice`, so every section is two taps from the overview (from a daily section to an occasional one it is three: Back, the row, the section) and Back retraces them. `shell/administration-lock.ts`: the token `ADMINISTRATION_LOCK` (`{ locked, panel, keepsPage, firstDownload }`, unlocked with no panel by default; `admin.routes.ts` provides it on the shell route from `lock/administration-lock-service.ts`, slice 2.19): while `locked()` the shell renders `panel()` (a signal, `NgComponentOutlet`) instead of the page, with the header and the tabs kept; locking removes the `router-outlet`, except when `keepsPage()` (a form was half-typed, `DirtyForms.any()`, at the moment it locked): then the page stays rendered, `hidden` and `inert`, under the panel and is there again, text and all, after the unlock. The kept page sits in `[appSuspendsDialogs]` (`shared/ui/dialog/dialog-suspension.ts`, the token `DIALOG_SUSPENSION`, `false` by default): a dialog of the page closes its native `<dialog>` while locked, without being dismissed, and opens again at the unlock, so a hidden modal never blocks the panel; a navigation while locked asks `unsavedChangesGuard`, which lets a page that is not rendered go (`null`) and lets a kept editor's question wait behind the panel. The panel's heading takes the focus when it appears; at the unlock the shell gives the focus back to the field of the kept page that had it, else to the page's `h1`. While unlocked an always rendered `aria-live` region `.admin-first-download` shows "Ukládáme správu do zařízení" with a progress bar and "n z m" during the first download of the administration's copy (`firstDownload()`). `lock/`: `administration-lock-service.ts` (`locked` = an administrator and `AdministrationAccess` not unlocked, `passkeyUnlocks()`, `unlockWithPasskey()`, `unlockWithPassword(password, code)`: the server checks both, then the access unlocks and the vault opens the copy with the password, each only while the same administrator is still signed in; `signOut()` keeps `locked` through `logout()` and the navigation home, so a kept page never shows on the way out) and `administration-lock-panel` ("Správa je zamčená": "Odemknout přístupovým klíčem" first when the copy has a PRF wrap, never asked for before the press, "Použít heslo" beside it or as the main button, disabled offline with "Jste offline…", the password view "Odemknout heslem" with `#zamek-heslo`, the hidden user name and `#zamek-kod`, "Odhlásit se z tohoto zařízení", and only signing out when neither way can unlock). `device-copy/`: `administration-sync-api.ts` (`/api/admin/donations/sync`, `/api/admin/donors/sync` with their `/chunks`, `POST …/devices/current/administration-copy` and `/unlock`) and `administration-copy-download.ts` (`AdministrationCopyDownload`, loaded by `device-copies.ts` for an administrator: when the vault opens the administration copy it records a first copy stored on the device before downloading it, downloads both manifests and their chunks as `donations/<id>` and `donors/<id>`, checks every digest twice at most, reports `device-copy-mismatch`, clears the copy on `administrator: false`, keeps it on a 403, and shows `firstDownload` only for a copy that opened empty) and `administration-copy.ts` (`AdministrationCopy`, root: what the pages read from the open administration copy; `rows(part, kind)` gives one signal per part and kind, the same signal at every call, with the rows of every chunk `<part>/<kind>` or `<part>/<kind>:…` in the order of the chunk ids and each chunk's rows as the server listed them, `null` while no copy is open and loaded (locked, downloading for the first time, empty or damaged) and `[]` for a loaded copy without that kind, so a page can choose between the copy and the API; `summary` the donations summary `{ today, byYear }` and `storedAt` the time of the last download, both `null` while nothing is loaded). `shell/section-rows/` (`app-admin-section-rows`, inputs `label` and `groups` of `SectionRowGroup { id, label, rows: SectionRow { path, label, icon?, badge? } }`): a `nav` of titled groups (`section` named by its `h2`), each a `ui-list ui-list--inset` of row links with an optional inline icon (`payments`, `donors`, `news`, `projects`, `more`), the title, an amber count pill `.ui-list__count--warning` (`aria-hidden`, its description visually hidden, only above 0) and the chevron. `more/` (`/sprava/vice`, "Občas a pro technika", `h1` focused on open): the same rows, `nav` "Další sekce správy", for `ADMIN_OCCASIONAL` and `ADMIN_TECHNICAL`. The pages render no "← Správa" link; the crumbs of a donor and of the news and project editors show only above `nav`, where no back button stands; their `.container` is left-aligned beside the panel.
- `admin-api.ts`: `importState()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/import-state` (`ImportStateResponse`, `ImportFailure`, `ImportFailureKind` as in the contract); `payments(pairing, year)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/payments` (`AdminPaymentListResponse`, `AdminPaymentResponse`, `PairedDonationResponse`, `PairingMethod`), `searchDonors(query)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/donors` (`AdminDonorSearchResponse`, `AdminDonorResponse`), `pairPayment(paymentId, donorId)` and `unpairPayment(paymentId)` → the two `POST`s (`PairPaymentResponse`, `UnpairPaymentResponse`); `administrators()` → `GET /api/admin/donors/administrators` (`AdministratorListResponse`, `AdministratorResponse`), `grantAdministration(email)` → `POST` there, `revokeAdministration(donorId)` → `DELETE .../administrators/{id}`, `resetSecondFactor(donorId)` → `POST /api/admin/donors/{id}/second-factor/reset`; `symbolClaims()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/symbol-claims` (`SymbolClaimListResponse`, `SymbolClaimResponse`, `SymbolClaimAnswer`), `reverseSymbolClaim(id)` → `POST .../symbol-claims/{id}/reverse`, `deleteSymbolClaim(id)` → `DELETE .../symbol-claims/{id}`; `adminActions(before?, limit = 50)` → `GET /api/admin/donors/actions` (`AdminActionListResponse`, `AdminActionResponse`); `donors(query)` → `GET /api/admin/donors` (`DonorAccountListResponse`, `DonorAccountResponse`, `AddressResponse`), `donor(donorId)` → `GET /api/admin/donors/{id}`, `donorDonations(donorId)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/donors/{id}` (`AdminDonorDetailResponse`, `AdminDonorRecordResponse`, `AdminReceiptResponse`, `ReceiptKind`; the accounts and donations reuse `BankAccountItem` and `DonationListResponse` of the donor features), `disconnectDonorAccount`, `reconnectDonorAccount` and `removeDonorAccount(donorId, linkId)` → the two `POST`s and the `DELETE` under `.../bank-accounts/{linkId}`; `report()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/report` (`AdminReportResponse`, `ReportSettingsResponse`, `ReportSectionsResponse`, `SentReportResponse`, `NextReportResponse`), `changeReportSettings(request)` → `PUT .../report/settings` (`ReportSettingsRequest`), `sendReport(month)` → `POST .../report/send` (`SendReportResponse`), `overview(year)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/overview` (`AdminOverviewResponse`, `MonthOverviewResponse`, `TallyResponse`), `backups()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/backups` (`AdminBackupsResponse`, `BackupFileResponse`, `BackupRunResponse`, `RestoreFileResponse`, `BackupKind`, `RunOutcome`, `RestoreFileState`, `BackupProblem`), `requestBackup(kind)` → `POST .../backups/requests` (`BackupRequestResponse`), `restoreBackup(fileName)` → `POST .../backups/{fileName}/restore` (`RestoreRequestResponse`), `downloadBackup(fileName)` → `GET .../backups/{fileName}` as a blob turned into a `DownloadedFile` by `downloadedFile` from `donations-api.ts`; `logFiles()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/logs` (`LogFilesResponse`, `LogFileResponse`), `logWindow(name, from, bytes)` → `GET .../logs/{name}` (`LogWindowResponse`), `searchLog(name, pattern, before, limit)` → `GET .../logs/{name}/search` (`LogSearchResponse`, `LogMatchResponse`), `logLevels()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/loggers` (`LogLevelsResponse`, `LoggerResponse`, `LogOverrideResponse`, `LogLevel`), `setLogLevel(logger, level, minutes)` → `PUT .../loggers/{logger}`, `resetLogLevel(logger)` → `DELETE` there, `diagnostics()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/diagnostics` (`DiagnosticsResponse`, `JvmDiagnosticsResponse`, `DatabaseDiagnosticsResponse`, `DiskSpaceResponse`), `threadDump()` → `GET .../diagnostics/threads` as text, `push()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/push` (`AdminPushResponse`, `PushSubscriptionResponse`, `OpenAlertResponse`, `PushAlertSwitches`, `PushAlertArea`, `PushService`), `subscribePush(request)` → `POST .../push/subscriptions` (`PushSubscriptionRequest`), `changePushAlerts(id, alerts)` → `PUT .../push/subscriptions/{id}/alerts`, `removePushSubscription(id)` → `DELETE .../push/subscriptions/{id}`, `testPush()` → `POST .../push/test` (`PushTestResponse`), `settings()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/settings` (`AdminSettingsResponse`, `AdminSettingResponse`, `SettingGroup`), `changeSettings(changes)` → `PUT` there (`SettingsSavedResponse`), `sendTestEmail(request)` → `POST .../settings/test-email` (`AdminTestEmailRequest`: `MailServerRequest` plus `sender`; `TestEmailResponse`), `receiptSignatory()` and `chooseReceiptSignatory({personId, receiptRole})` → `GET` and `PUT /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory` (`ReceiptSignatoryResponse` with `ChosenReceiptSignatoryResponse` and `ReceiptSignatureSummaryResponse`s; `MAX_RECEIPT_ROLE_LENGTH` 120), `receiptSignature(personId)`, `uploadReceiptSignature(personId, file)` and `removeReceiptSignature(personId)` → `GET`, `POST` (multipart `file`) and `DELETE /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatures/{personId}` (`ReceiptSignatureResponse` with `kind` `ReceiptSignatureKind` and `vector` `SignatureVectorResponse` of `SignatureVectorPath`s); `receiptSigningKeys()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signing-keys` (`ReceiptSigningKeysResponse`, `ReceiptSigningKeyResponse`, `ReceiptSigningKeyStatus`); fixtures in `testing/admin-fixtures.ts` (`importState`, `unpairedPayment`, `pairedDonation`, `paymentList`, `foundDonor`, `administrator`, `symbolClaim`, `adminAction`, `donorAccount`, `adminBankAccount`, `adminDonation`, `adminReceipt`, `donorDetail`, `reportView`, `monthOverview`, `overview`, `backupFile`, `backupRun`, `backupsView`, `logFile`, `logWindow`, `logOverride`, `loggerEntry`, `diagnostics`, `setting`, `settingsView`, `receiptSignature`, `noReceiptSignature`).
- `payments/` (`/sprava/platby`, "Platby z banky"): `h1` focused on open, an intro, and a toolbar with the two `aria-pressed` buttons "Čekají na přiřazení" (default; "Čekají na přiřazení (N)" once the unpaired list has answered, N its `total`, one more after each unpairing) and "Přiřazené" (the count only from an unfiltered unpaired list), then a `role="search"` bar with one text field "Hledat" (hint "Jméno odesílatele nebo dárce, účet, variabilní symbol, zpráva nebo částka v korunách.", asked 300 ms after the typing pauses) and the native date fields "Od" and "Do" (asked on change, each bounding the other with `min`/`max`). The state lives in the address (Jakub 2026-10-24): `stav=prirazene` for the paired tab, `q`, `od`, `do` and `darce` (a donor id, from the donor card's link "Platby tohoto dárce"; switching to the unpaired tab drops it), written with `replaceUrl` so a filter change adds no history entry, while Back from a donor card and a shared link open the same view; the page follows `queryParamMap`, so the panel link resets it. Each view asks for 50 (`limit=50`, `switchMap` drops a stale answer); while fewer are shown than `total`, the line "Zobrazeno 50 z 312." and "Načíst další" ("Načítáme další…") under the table ask for the next 50 with `offset` and append them, the focus moves to the first new row (`tabindex="-1"`), a failure is a `role="alert"` "Další platby se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." and the button stays. With any filter the always-rendered `role="status"` line `.payments-count` says "Nalezena 1 platba." / "Nalezeny 3 platby." / "Nalezeno 12 plateb." / "Nenalezena žádná platba.", and when a live `payments` hint (2.16) brings payments the view does not show yet, the same line ends with the button "Zobrazit 1 novou platbu" / "Zobrazit 2 nové platby" / "Zobrazit 5 nových plateb", which shows the first page again and focuses the card's title, while the tab count follows at once (only payments that come before the last row shown count as new, so a payment that moves up from below when one leaves a longer list simply joins the rows; a hint that brings nothing new updates the shown rows in place; while the pairing dialog or an unpairing confirmation is open, or the next page loads, the page waits and reads after it closes or arrives), an empty result says "Žádná platba neodpovídá hledání." with "Zrušit filtr" (clears text, period and donor and focuses the search field), and a donor filter adds "Jen platby dárce <jméno>." (the name from the first listed donation; "Jen platby jednoho dárce." without one) with "Zobrazit platby všech dárců". The paired view lists every year newest first (no year choice any more). The card lists the payments as a table (cards with `data-label` up to 640 px): date (with the amber tag "jen ručně" and the visible note "Správce zrušil přiřazení, web ji proto sám nepřiřadí." when `unpairedByAdminAt` is set), amount (with the badge "Ověření účtu" when `accountVerification`), sender (name and account), symbol and message ("bez VS" without one), for paired payments the donor as a link to `/sprava/darci/{donorId}` ("dárce bez záznamu" when the record is gone) and the tag "vráceno" for a refunded donation, the method in words ("podle variabilního symbolu", "podle účtu", "podle potvrzeného staršího symbolu", "ručně správcem") and the tag "potvrzení vystaveno"; the last column holds "Přiřadit dárci" (unpaired) or "Zrušit přiřazení" (paired; replaced by "Nelze zrušit, k daru je vystavené potvrzení." when `receiptIssued`), which turns into the inline confirmation "Dar 500 Kč zmizí z přehledu dárce <jméno> a platba se vrátí mezi čekající na přiřazení; web ji sám znovu nepřiřadí. Zrušit přiřazení?" (a group named "Zrušit přiřazení platby <platba>?") with "Zrušit přiřazení" ("Rušíme…") / "Ponechat" before the `POST`; focus moves to "Ponechat" when it opens, back to the opener after "Ponechat" or a refusal, and to the page's notice after success. The card titles are "Platby čekající na přiřazení" and "Přiřazené platby". Empty lists say "Všechny platby z banky jsou přiřazené…" or "Zatím není přiřazena žádná platba."; loading is a `role="status"` line, a failed load a `role="alert"` line with "Zkusit znovu"; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog, 403 goes to `/darce/prehled`.
