"Přiřadit dárci" opens an `<app-dialog>` naming the payment (amount and day, sender, symbol and message; for an account verification the badge "Ověření účtu" and a warning callout that pairing it by hand counts 1 Kč for the donor but verifies no account, which a payment with the donor's symbol does) with a search field prefilled with the first word of the sender's name (two letters or more), searched at once and focused with its text selected; a typed query goes to `searchDonors` 300 ms after the typing pauses, `switchMap` drops stale answers and a failed search does not end the stream listing the donors as radio buttons ("Jana Nováková · VS 4821937560 · jana@example.cz · IČO …": the e-mail comes from `donors(query, 100)`, `GET /api/admin/donors` with the same query, asked beside the search and matched by `donorId`, so two donors of one name can be told apart; the results show at once and the e-mails follow, and a failed lookup only leaves them out; "Žádný dárce neodpovídá." as `role="status"`, a failed search as `role="alert"`); "Přiřadit" becomes "Přiřadit dárci <jméno>" once a donor is chosen and shows "Přiřazujeme…" while posting; a refusal stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` line (`describeProblem`), success closes it, reloads the list and puts a `role="status"` line on the page ("Platba 500 Kč z 10. 10. 2026 je přiřazena dárci Jana Nováková."), extended by "Dárce si už stáhl roční potvrzení za 2026; při dalším stažení dostane nové. Dejte mu prosím vědět." when `changedYearlyReceipts` is not empty; unpairing reports the same way ("… už není přiřazena. Web ji sám znovu nepřiřadí; najdete ji mezi platbami čekajícími na přiřazení."), extended, when it withdrew the yearly receipt that listed the gift (1.26), by "Roční potvrzení za 2026, na kterém dar byl, je teď zrušené; ověření ukáže, že platilo ke dni vystavení. Zbyly-li dárci za ten rok další dary, může si stáhnout nové. Dejte mu prosím vědět."; after either, the link "Otevřít kartu dárce <jméno>" sits under the notice. Specs in `admin-payments.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-payments.spec.ts` (pair through the dialog with the receipt note and the paired view, unpair with the confirmation and the held tag, a refused pairing staying in the dialog and Escape returning focus to the opener, a search with the next page and Back from a donor card restoring it) and the axe case for the page and the open dialog.
- `administrators/` (`/sprava/spravci`, "Správci"): `h1` focused on open (outline hidden, like the other script-focused headings), an intro saying who can become an administrator. The card "Kdo web spravuje" lists the administrators in the look of the people of the foundation (Jakub 2026-10-31: the shared block `.ui-person-list` / `.ui-person-row` of `styles/ui/_person-list.scss`): each row has a small round portrait (`app-avatar`, the initials without one), the name as a link to the donor card, the e-mail under it and the badges "To jste vy" (the signed-in one, found by `AuthStore.donor().id`) and "Dvoufaktorové ověření zapnuté" or "Dvoufaktorové ověření vypnuté" for `secondFactorEnabled` (the code only; the API does not say whether passkeys exist) with the line "Správa se mu otevře jen přístupovým klíčem." under the latter. The portrait is the one of the person of the foundation linked to the administrator: the page also loads `PeopleApi.load()` once and joins its members to the administrators by e-mail, taking the first member in the order of the site with `linkedToAccount` whose personal or fund e-mail equals the administrator's (the same person `GET /api/admin/content/people/me` gives that administrator for the account button; both e-mails come already normalised by the backend, so the join compares them as they are); when the people cannot be loaded the rows keep their initials and nothing else changes. The actions sit at the right of the row, and under it up to 640 px: for another administrator "Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření" (only with the factor on; inline confirmation across the row "Ověřili jste, že žádá majitel účtu (například odpověděl z registrovaného e-mailu)? Správce se pak přihlásí jen heslem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí." with "Ano, vypnout" ("Vypínáme…") / "Ponechat") and "Odebrat roli správce" (danger outline; "<jméno> přestane být správcem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí. Jeho dárcovský účet zůstane. Odebrat roli správce?" with "Ano, odebrat" ("Odebíráme…") / "Ponechat"); focus moves to "Ponechat" on open, back to the opener after it or a refusal, and to the notice after success; the signed-in administrator has no buttons but the line "Svůj účet spravujete v Údajích a zabezpečení." (a link to `/darce/udaje`). Outcomes are a `role="status"` line on the page ("Petr Svoboda už není správce. Všechna jeho přihlášení jsme ukončili.", "Dvoufaktorové ověření u Petr Svoboda je vypnuté … Poslali jsme mu e-mail; …") or a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail; the list reloads after each. The card "Přidat správce" is a form with the field "E-mail dárce" (`required`, `email`; "Vyplňte prosím e-mail registrovaného dárce." / "Zadejte prosím platnou e-mailovou adresu.") and "Přidat správce" ("Přidáváme…" while posting); success clears the field and says "<jméno> je teď správce. Správa se mu otevře, až se přihlásí s kódem z ověřovací aplikace nebo přístupovým klíčem." (or "… po dalším přihlášení." when the factor is already on); a refusal (404 not registered, 409 already) stays at the form as a `role="alert"` line and keeps the value. Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the payments page. Specs in `admin-administrators.spec.ts` (the portrait of the linked person, the first one when two share the e-mail, and the initials when the people cannot be loaded); Playwright `e2e/admin-administrators.spec.ts` (the portrait, add by e-mail, the refusal at the form, reset and removal with their confirmations) and the axe case for the page with an open confirmation and the empty-form error.
- `symbol-claims/` (`/sprava/starsi-symboly`, "Odpovědi o starších symbolech"): `h1` focused on open, an intro paragraph explaining the older-symbol question followed by a two-item list of what each correction does. The card "Odpovědi dárců" lists every answer as a table (cards up to 640 px): donor as a link to the donor card ("dárce bez záznamu" when the record is gone), account and symbol in the mono font, the answer as a tag ("Ano, jsou moje" green, "Ne, nejsou moje" grey) with the Prague time under it, the donations paired through it ("1 dar", "3 dary", "5 darů"), and the action: "Vrátit přiřazení darů" (danger outline) for a yes (an inline confirmation naming the donations that will be deleted, the payments going back to waiting and the answer turning to Ne, with "Vrátit přiřazení" / "Ponechat" before the `POST`) or "Zeptat se dárce znovu" for a no (with "Zeptat se znovu" / "Ponechat" before the `DELETE`); focus moves as on the administrators page. Outcomes are a `role="status"` line ("Přiřazení darů u Jana Nováková (VS 6123456789) jsme vrátili: 3 dary jsme smazali a platby s tímto symbolem zase čekají na přiřazení. Odpověď je teď Ne, dárce se už neptáme." / "Odpověď Ne u … jsme smazali. Pokud otázka pořád platí, dárce ji uvidí znovu ve svém přehledu.") or a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail (the receipt refusal); the list reloads after each. The empty list says "Zatím nikdo neodpověděl na otázku o starším variabilním symbolu."; loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts` (reverse and delete with their confirmations, the refusal by a receipt) and the axe case for the page with an open confirmation.
- `yearly-receipts/` (`/sprava/hromadna-potvrzeni`, "Hromadná potvrzení", slice 2.29; contract "Bulk yearly receipts"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what is kept, issued and replaced, and that a single receipt to send by e-mail comes from the donor card. The card "Vystavit a vytisknout" asks `yearlyReceiptCandidates(null)` first (the last completed year) and offers the years from `receiptsAvailableFrom` through `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` in a select, which keeps its place and focus while another year loads (an always rendered status line "Načítáme dárce za rok …" instead of the counts, the button and the table; "Zkusit znovu" after a failure asks for the year chosen); a 400 `donations/year-not-yet-completed` or `donations/receipt-before-first-year` there says that bulk receipts come after the first year the foundation issues them for. Four labelled counts (donors with gifts, receipts already issued, to be issued, erased donors skipped), the checkbox "Tisknu oboustranně" (on by default, because two donors' receipts on one sheet cost more than a blank page, UX review of 2.29; its label says it adds blank pages and to turn it off for one-sided printing; never disabled, so it keeps focus; changing it while drawing or after the PDF is ready draws it again without issuing), "Vystavit a připravit PDF k tisku" (`aria-disabled` while running, so focus stays on it; it says "Vystavujeme potvrzení…" and then "Připravujeme PDF…"; soft once a PDF is ready; after a failure it goes on as "Zkusit znovu" does) and "Nechte prosím stránku otevřenou…", which sends the year's donors in batches of 50 one after another (`issueYearlyReceipts`) with "Vystavujeme potvrzení N z M…", then draws every returned receipt into one PDF through `drawBundle` with "Kreslíme potvrzení…" (announced once; the counter "k z n" is the progress bar's visible detail and `aria-valuetext`). A failure shows a `role="alert"` line with "Zkusit znovu", which sends only the donors not yet answered, or after a drawing failure only draws again; the run can be cancelled, which moves focus to that line. While it runs `screen-wake-lock.ts` (`ScreenWakeLock` behind `SCREEN_WAKE_LOCKS`) keeps the screen on, takes the lock again when the tab comes back and lets it go on done, failure, cancel or leaving. When done: "PDF k tisku je připravené: obsahuje N potvrzení.", "Stáhnout PDF" ("Stáhnout znovu" from memory, offline too, for the life of the page) and "Otevřít PDF" through `PdfDelivery` (`potvrzeni-o-darech-<year>-hromadne.pdf`, titled "Roční potvrzení o darech za rok <year>"), "Tiskněte ve skutečné velikosti (100 %)…", the card "Výsledek" (in place of the counts from before the run) with the four outcomes and the replaced donors (with the sentence that the old receipts stop being valid, so the donors get the new ones) and the skipped donors, each linking to the donor card with a Czech reason per problem code, and the table "Dárci za rok <year>" (donor, gifts, total, receipt number or "zatím ne"; sorted with `Intl.Collator('cs')`; cards up to 640 px) updated to the new numbers. Tests: `admin-yearly-receipts.spec.ts`, `screen-wake-lock.spec.ts`, `receipt-pdf-bundle.spec.ts`, `e2e/admin-yearly-receipts.spec.ts` (a duplex run read back page by page and downloaded again with no request; a failed batch retried with 50, 10 and 10 donors), the axe, theme and device steps, and `e2e/bulk-receipts-load.spec.ts`, the nightly 300 receipts at 4× CPU (`BULK_RECEIPTS_LOAD=1`).
- `actions/` (`/sprava/historie`, "Historie změn"): `h1` focused on open, an intro. The card "Co správci udělali" lists the `admin_action` trail newest first as a table (cards up to 640 px): the Prague time, the administrator (name and e-mail; "správce bez účtu" when the account is gone, its id then inside the details; "přes aplikaci claude.ai" under them when the change came through a connected application, from the item's `client`), the action in Czech (`ACTION_NAMES` maps `PAIR_PAYMENT`, `UNPAIR_PAYMENT`, `GRANT_ADMINISTRATION`, `REVOKE_ADMINISTRATION`, `RESET_SECOND_FACTOR`, `REMOVE_PASSKEYS`, `REVERSE_SYMBOL_CLAIM`, `RESET_SYMBOL_CLAIM`, `DISCONNECT_SENDER_ACCOUNT`, `RECONNECT_SENDER_ACCOUNT`, `REMOVE_SENDER_ACCOUNT_LINK`, `ERASE_DONOR`, `CHANGE_REPORT_SETTINGS`, `SEND_MONTHLY_REPORT`, `CHANGE_RECEIPT_SIGNATURE`, `REMOVE_RECEIPT_SIGNATURE`, `ISSUE_RECEIPT` "Vystavení potvrzení o daru", `WITHDRAW_RECEIPT` "Zrušení potvrzení o daru" (1.26), `CREATE_NEWS`, `UPDATE_NEWS`, `PUBLISH_NEWS`, `UNPUBLISH_NEWS`, `DELETE_NEWS`, `CREATE_PROJECT`, `UPDATE_PROJECT`, `PUBLISH_PROJECT`, `UNPUBLISH_PROJECT`, `DELETE_PROJECT`, `CHANGE_PROJECT_ORDER`, `CHANGE_ABOUT_PHOTO`, `REMOVE_ABOUT_PHOTO`, `REQUEST_BACKUP`, `REQUEST_TEST_RESTORE`, `DOWNLOAD_BACKUP`, `RESTORE_BACKUP`, `BACKUP_RESTORED`, `BACKUP_RESTORE_FAILED`, `CHANGE_LOG_LEVEL`, `RESET_LOG_LEVEL`, `CHANGE_SETTINGS`, `SUBSCRIBE_PUSH` "Zapnutí upozornění přes push na zařízení", `CHANGE_PUSH_ALERTS` "Změna upozornění přes push na zařízení", `REMOVE_PUSH_SUBSCRIPTION` "Vypnutí upozornění přes push na zařízení" (2.27), `CHANGE_BACKUP_SCHEDULE`, `REGISTER_RECEIPT_SIGNING_KEY` "Registrace klíče k ověřovacím QR kódům", `ADOPT_RECEIPT_SIGNING_KEY` "Převzetí klíče k ověřovacím QR kódům ze zálohy"; an unknown code is shown as it is; a row by the server itself, `adminId` the nil UUID, names "server" as the administrator and no id in the details), the link "Otevřít kartu dárce" to `/sprava/darci/{donorId}` whenever the server names the donor the change concerned (`donorId`: donor actions, payment pairings, symbol answers, bank accounts), and in the details column the server's sentence `summary` (Jakub 2026-10-24: human Czech written by the backend, e.g. "Platba 500 Kč ze dne 10. 10. 2026 ručně přiřazena dárci Jana Nováková.") above the stored details inside a closed `<details>` "Technické podrobnosti" (with the ids; English for rows before 2026-10-24). The first page asks for 50; while `hasMore`, "Načíst starší" under the table asks for the next 50 before the oldest `performedAt` listed and appends them ("Načítáme starší…" while loading, a `role="alert"` "Starší záznamy se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." on failure, the button stays). The empty trail says "Zatím žádný správce nic nezměnil."; loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-actions.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-actions.spec.ts` (the first page and the appended older page, the empty trail) and the axe case for the page with its paging button.
- `donors/` (`/sprava/darci`, "Dárci"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what the card offers. The card "Hledat dárce" has the field "Jméno, e-mail nebo variabilní symbol" (`type="search"`, hint "Hledáme část jména nebo e-mailu a začátek variabilního symbolu; nabídneme nejvýše 20 dárců."); typing is debounced 300 ms, trimmed and deduplicated, each query goes to `donors(query)` and lands in the address as `?q=` (`replaceUrl`), so the page reopens with the same search and the browser's back button returns to the result list. States as `role="status"` or `role="alert"` lines: "Začněte psát a dárci se objeví tady." (empty query), "Hledáme dárce…", "Hledání se nepodařilo. Zkuste to prosím znovu." with the button "Zkusit znovu" (asks the same query again), "Žádný dárce neodpovídá.", a visible "Zobrazujeme prvních N dárců. Upřesněte hledání…" when `hasMore`, and a visually hidden "Nalezení dárci: N" above the table (cards up to 640 px): the name as a link to `/sprava/darci/{id}` with "Osoba" or "Firma · IČO …" under it, e-mail, variable symbol, registration (the Prague date only) and the tags "Správce", "Dvoufaktorové ověření", "E-mail neověřen" (or "běžný účet"); an erased donor (`erasedAt`) shows "vymazán" instead of the placeholder e-mail and only the tag "Údaje vymazány". 401 and 403 as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-donors.spec.ts`.
- `donor-detail/` (`/sprava/darci/:id`, "Dárce"): the link "← Dárci", `h1` with the donor's name (focused on open; "Dárce" until it loads or when there is none). Once `donor(id)` answers, a summary strip (`section.donor-summary`, "Shrnutí dárce") shows the variable symbol with `app-copy-button` ("Kopírovat variabilní symbol"), the e-mail with "ověřen" / "neověřen", the received gifts ("2 dary za 800 Kč", once `donorDonations(id)` answers) and the badges "Správce" and "2FA". Under it the `nav` "Části karty dárce" lists in-page links "Údaje · Přihlášení · Bankovní účty · Dary · Potvrzení" (only to the cards shown; `href` `/sprava/darci/{id}#udaje` and so on): a click keeps the address, and focuses the card (`id` without diacritics, `tabindex="-1"`, no ring), which scrolls it into view below the top safe area thanks to the global `scroll-padding-top`. The cards, the first two from `donor(id)`, the rest from `donorDonations(id)`, each loading on its own: "Údaje" is a `dl` (type, e-mail with the tag, address, variable symbol, language ("česky" / "anglicky"), registration, role "správce" (a link to `/sprava/spravci`) / "dárce"). "Přihlášení" (once the account is loaded) is a `dl` (two-factor authentication "zapnuté" / "vypnuté", passkeys with their count or "žádné") with "Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření" for a donor who has it on (inline confirmation "Ověřili jste, že žádá majitel účtu (například odpověděl z registrovaného e-mailu)? Dárce se pak přihlásí jen heslem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí." with "Ano, vypnout" / "Ponechat"; success "Dvoufaktorové ověření u <jméno> je vypnuté a všechna přihlášení jsme ukončili. Dárce dostal e-mail; …") and "Odebrat přístupové klíče" with the hint "Pro dárce, který přišel o zařízení s klíčem." for a donor with keys (confirmation "Dárce se jimi pak nepřihlásí a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí. Odebrat?", `removePasskeys(id)` is a DELETE, success "Přístupové klíče u <jméno> jsme odebrali a všechna přihlášení ukončili. …"); after either the account is fetched again without the loading state, so the card and its result stay. A 404 leaves a single card "Účet dárce" saying "Takový dárce není. Vraťte se na seznam dárců a vyhledejte ho znovu." with the link "Zpět na hledání dárců" and hides everything else. Every inline confirmation answers "Ponechat"; focus moves to "Ponechat" when it opens, back to the opener (ids `donor-reset-open`, `donor-passkeys-open`, `account-<kind>-<linkId>`) after it or a refusal, and to the result line after success. The result lines sit in the card the action belongs to: one `app-live-notice` each in "Přihlášení", "Bankovní účty" and the erasure card (`cardNotice` names the card). The last card, "Výmaz osobních údajů (GDPR)", offers "Vymazat osobní údaje" (danger outline, id `donor-erase-open`) with the hint "Pro dárce, který požádal o výmaz podle GDPR."; for an administrator the hint "Správci nejde vymazat osobní údaje. Nejdřív mu odeberte roli správce na stránce Správci." replaces the button, and an erased donor reads "Osobní údaje tohoto dárce jsou vymazané.". The button opens an `<app-dialog>` "Vymazat osobní údaje: <jméno>" saying the step cannot be undone, listing what is deleted (name, address, e-mail and password replaced by placeholders, the bank accounts, the older-symbol answers) and what stays (donations and receipts for the accounting, the receipts unchanged on the server with the donor's name and address; the bank payments as the statement gives them, with the account number and payer name, and the entries in Historie změn; A6), that the donor gets an e-mail, and a checkbox "Dárce o výmaz požádal a ověřil(a) jsem, že jde o majitele účtu"; "Zrušit" has the focus when it opens, the danger button "Vymazat osobní údaje" stays disabled until the box is ticked and shows "Mažeme…" while `eraseDonor(id)` (a DELETE) runs; a refusal stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` line, success closes it, puts "Osobní údaje dárce jsme vymazali a na jeho dosavadní e-mail poslali potvrzení. …" in the erasure card with the focus, and both halves reload. An erased donor (`erasedAt` on either half) gets a `role="note"` box under the `h1` "Osobní údaje tohoto dárce jsme vymazali <čas>. …", "vymazán" / "vymazána" instead of the placeholder e-mail and address, no actions, and "Bankovní účty" says "Bankovní účty jsme s osobními údaji dárce smazali; další platby se mu už nepřiřadí." "Bankovní účty" says that its column "Dary přiřazené podle účtu" (formerly "Dary přes účet", which Hana read as an error for a company giving with its symbol from a verified account, 30. 10. 2026) counts only payments without the symbol that were paired by the account, the gifts with the symbol being under Dary, explains when to switch pairing off and when to remove the account, and lists the links (account with the tag "sdílený", the status "Páruje platby" / "Čeká na platbu se symbolem" / "Párování vypnuté" with the verification, connection and disconnection dates, the donations paired by the account with their total and the last date) with "Vypnout párování" (live link), "Zapnout párování" (disconnected, not `pairingExcluded`) and "Odebrat účet" (danger outline; confirmation "Smažeme N darů přiřazené přes tento účet i odpovědi o starších symbolech; platby zůstanou nespárované. Odebrat?"), replaced by "Nejde odebrat: k darům z účtu je vystavené potvrzení." when the link is the only one of that account and one of its donations has a receipt number; the outcomes are `role="status"` lines naming the account, a refusal (409 by a receipt) a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail plus, for `donations/bank-account-has-receipt`, the next step (switch pairing off), and the three cards reload after each. "Dary" says "Celkem N darů za X Kč." (counting only received donations, plus "Vrácené dary (n) se nezapočítávají." when some were refunded) and lists every donation (received, amount, purpose, how it was paired with the account or symbol, the receipt number or "zatím žádné"); "Vydaná potvrzení" lists every receipt (number, "Dar z <datum>" or "Rok YYYY", issued at with CZ/EN, "platné" / "nahrazeno novějším" / "zrušeno, dary se změnily" (1.26, `withdrawalReason` `DONATIONS_CHANGED`), and at each valid receipt, also of an erased donor, "Stáhnout PDF" (1.19, A7; its name ends with the hidden "potvrzení <číslo>", so it holds the visible text in every state): `admin-receipt-downloads.ts` (`AdminReceiptDownloads`, provided by the card) draws it with `ReceiptPdf.draw` from `ReceiptsApi.administrationSource(id)`, so it issues nothing, and refuses a receipt the read no longer answers as `ACTIVE` (replaced since the card loaded: the card says so in its alert and lists the receipts again), shows the donor portal's steps through `app-download-progress` at the row (the step and saved texts of `receipt-pdf/receipt-progress.ts`, which both use under their `donor.dashboard.receipt.*` ids, so rewording them changes both pages; "Zrušit" returns focus to the row's button; a new download clears the finished lines of the others), saves it through `PdfDelivery` or, on an iPhone that shares files, turns the button into "Otevřít PDF" for the share sheet, and puts any failure as a `role="alert"` line at the row: the problem of the read, or for a drawing failure an administrator's sentence (tools not loaded: reload the page; drawing; signature or portrait not verified; unprintable character; theme refused); a lost session goes to the sign-in as the card's other calls do; under the button of a receipt whose `keyId` the published key list names `revoked` an amber note "QR na tomto potvrzení podepsal zneplatněný klíč. Stránka /overit ho ukáže jako zneplatněné." (Jakub 2026-11-05, Phase 1 handover card 6: the PDF is still offered, warned), the card reading `GET /api/donations/receipt-keys` without the token (`ReceiptKeysApi`) once it lists a valid receipt, and only once per visit, through `trustedKeys`, as `/overit` does, so it warns exactly when `/overit` says "Neověřeno: fond tento klíč zneplatnil"; the note describes the button (`aria-describedby`); a list that cannot be read, and a key the list does not name, warn of nothing; empty: "Dárci zatím nikdo žádné potvrzení nevystavil.") and, unless the donor is erased, offers "Vystavit potvrzení" (1.7): a select "Potvrzení k vystavení" (its hint says the same data return the receipt that already holds) with "Roční potvrzení za rok YYYY" for each year from `receiptsAvailableFrom` to `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` (both from the detail, so no client clock) in which a received donation lies, newest first, then "Dar z <datum>, X Kč" for each received donation with `receiptAvailable`; without any it says "Dárce nemá žádný dar, za který by šlo potvrzení vystavit.". The button posts `issueReceipt(id, {year} | {donationId})` (`POST /api/admin/donations/donors/{id}/receipts`), shows "Vystavujeme potvrzení…" with the button and the select disabled, then a `role="status"` line in the card's `app-live-notice` (focused) with the number, the Prague time and the status ("Vystavili jsme potvrzení 2027-000001 z <čas>. Je platné.", with ", které nahrazuje potvrzení …" for a replacement, or "Potvrzení … z <čas> dál platí: údaje se nezměnily, nové číslo jsme nevydali." when the answer is a receipt the card already listed) and reloads the donations part without hiding the cards; a refusal (the cap, an erased donor) is a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail, or "Potvrzení se nepodařilo vystavit. Zkuste to prosím znovu.", and the focus goes back to the button. The PDF is drawn in 1.9; under the donations the link "Platby tohoto dárce" opens `/sprava/platby?stav=prirazene&darce={id}`, the paired payments filtered to this donor. Without a `donor_record` (404 from the donations side) the three cards say "Dárce nemá záznam v evidenci darů, takže nemá žádné účty, dary ani potvrzení." Specs in `admin-donor-detail.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-donors.spec.ts` (the journey from `/sprava` through the search, the card, the reset, the disconnect and the removal, the refusal by a receipt, issuing a receipt of one donation, the unknown donor) and the axe case for the list and the card with an open confirmation; the journey (with the in-page link to Přihlášení) and the axe case cover the erasure dialog too.
- `home/` (`/sprava`, "Přehled" in the panel): `h1` "Správa" (`tabindex="-1"`, focused on open), then the card "Od posledního přihlášení" (Jakub 29. 10. 2026), then the card "Co potřebuje vaši pozornost", then, only while `CompactLayout.isCompact()` (2.24, where no side panel stands), the section rows `nav` "Sekce správy": the group "Každý den" with Platby z banky (its badge the unpaired count the attention card loads, "N platba čeká na přiřazení"), Dárci, Aktuality and Projekty, and the group "Občas a pro technika" with the one row "Lidé fondu, správci, zálohy, nastavení…" to `/sprava/vice`, below it two smaller status cards side by side (one column when narrow), "Stav stahování plateb z banky" and "Zálohy databáze", and at the bottom the card "Dary a dárci". The attention card gathers six sources, each loading and failing on its own: the import state, `payments('unpaired')` (every unpaired payment of every year: the count is `total`, the sum adds the listed `amountMinor`), `backups()` and `administrators()` (for each administrator without the code, `donor(id)` is asked and those with `passkeys === 0` count as without any second factor), `receiptSigningKeys()` (`GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signing-keys`) and `receiptSignatory()` (`GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory`, slice 1.16): with nobody chosen the red row, first in the list because it stops every receipt, "Potvrzení o daru nikdo nepodepisuje, a tak je web dárcům nevystaví." with the link "Vybrat podepisujícího" to `/sprava/lide-fondu`, with a signatory without a receipt signature the row "Kdo podepisuje potvrzení o daru, zatím nemá nahraný podpis; potvrzení mají jen čáru se jménem." with "Nahrát podpis", and on a failure a row with "Zkusit znovu". While any of the first five loads it says "Zjišťujeme, jestli na vás něco nečeká…" (`role="status"`); with nothing pending "Vše je v pořádku. Platby z banky naposledy stažené {čas}, poslední záloha {čas}."; otherwise a list with one row per item and its way forward: the import outage (`reportedOutage`: "Platby z banky se nestahují (N× za sebou) a fond o tom dostal e-mail. Důvod: <reason>." and "Co udělat:" with the next step per `ImportFailureKind`, `NEXT_STEPS`, adapted from the outage e-mail, with "Otevřít Nastavení" or "Otevřít logy" where it helps), an import that never ran (pointing to the Fio token in Nastavení), "N platby čekají na přiřazení dárci (X Kč)." with "Přiřadit platby" (`/sprava/platby`), the backup problems in plain words (`BACKUP_PROBLEM_HELP` in `backups/backup-texts.ts`, the same sentences the backups page shows, which keeps the technical `BACKUP_PROBLEM_TEXTS` under its "Technické podrobnosti") with "Otevřít zálohy" and, when the server administrator is needed, the same "Zkopírovat zprávu pro technika" as the backups page, "Klíč k zálohám si zatím nikdo neuložil. Bez něj žádnou zálohu neobnovíte. Uložit ho má někdo ze správní rady, ne technik." with "Uložit klíč" (`/sprava/zalohy#klic-k-zaloham`) while `backups().key` is available and unconfirmed, a red row "Klíč <id> není zveřejněný, proto se QR kódy jím podepsané neověří. Jeho záznam v databázi chybí nebo neprošel kontrolou pečeti (změna mimo web nebo záloha jiného serveru, kterou server nepřevzal); obraťte se na technika serveru." (plural "Klíče … nejsou zveřejněné …", `settings/receipt-signing-key/unpublished-keys.ts`, whose `unpublishedKeyIds` adds the server's own key when `currentKeyPublished` is false, also when its row is missing) with "Otevřít nastavení" (`/sprava/nastaveni#klic-k-overovacim-qr`) for every receipt signing key that is not published, a second red row with the same link for a withdrawn key of the server and every audit mismatch (`signingKeyWarnings`, `key-warnings.ts`) (`receiptSigningKeys()`, its own source of the card: a failure says "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům se nepodařilo zkontrolovat." with "Zkusit znovu"), "Správce <jméno> nemá druhý faktor (kód z ověřovací aplikace ani přístupový klíč), správa se mu neotevře." with "Otevřít Správce", and last "Potvrzení o daru zatím nemají nahraný podpis, jen čáru se jménem." with "Nahrát podpis" (`/sprava/nastaveni#settings-RECEIPTS`, the receipts section the signature card follows; Hana 30. 10. 2026 found the missing signature only in Nastavení); a failed source is its own row ("… se nepodařilo zjistit", with "Zkusit znovu" for the payments and the administrators, the import and backups retried in their cards). The import card: "Načítáme stav importu…" (`role="status"`) while loading; on failure a `role="alert"` line "Stav importu se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu"; when loaded and without a failure, "Stahování plateb je v pořádku, naposledy {čas}.", then a `dl`: "Stažené období" (`coveredFrom – coveredTo` as `formatDonationDate`, "zatím nic"), "Poslední úspěšné stažení" and "Poslední pokus" (`formatBankTime`, Prague; "zatím žádné" / "zatím žádný"), "Poslední chyba" (the Czech name of the `ImportFailureKind`, its time, the stored message in brackets, or "žádná"). The card "Dary a dárci" asks `overview()` on open and again when the "Rok" `select` (the `availableYears`) changes: "Načítáme dary po měsících…" while loading, a `role="alert"` "Dary po měsících se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu" on failure, then the line "Registrovaní dárci: N, z toho nových v roce Y: M." and the table (cards up to 640 px) Měsíc (`formatReportMonth`, "říjen 2026"), Dary (`received.count`, every payment that reached the account), Dárci (`givingDonors`, the distinct donors with a received donation in the month, so paired payments only; the footer counts each donor once a year), Noví dárci, Celkem (`received`), Přiřazené (`paired`) and Bez dárce (`unpaired`), amounts as `formatCrowns` + Kč, with a "Celkem Y" footer (on phones each month is a card with Dary, Dárci, Celkem and Bez dárce two by two; Noví dárci and Přiřazené are hidden there); above the table the hint "Dárci: kolik různých dárců v měsíci poslalo dar; platby bez dárce se nepočítají. Noví dárci: kolik dárců se v měsíci zaregistrovalo." (`aria-describedby` of the table) and under it the line "Noví dárci jsou nové registrace, i bez daru, takže jich může být víc než dárců. Celkem zahrnuje i platby, které ještě nemají dárce (Celkem = Přiřazené + Bez dárce)." (`.overview-columns`; Hana 30. 10. 2026 could not tell the two donor columns apart), or "Rok Y ještě nezačal." when the year has no started month. The backups card asks `backups()` on open (fails on its own with "Stav záloh se nepodařilo načíst." and "Zkusit znovu"): the green "Zálohování je v pořádku." when there is no problem (the problems themselves are in the attention card), a `dl` "Poslední záloha" ("{time} ({size})", "selhala {time}", "zatím žádná") and "Zkušební obnova" ("{time}, N tabulek", "selhala {time}", "zatím žádná"), and the link "Otevřít zálohy" to `/sprava/zalohy`. A 401 `auth/unauthenticated` ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog at `/`; a 403 `auth/forbidden` (the role taken away meanwhile) goes to `/darce/prehled`. The card "Od posledního přihlášení" asks `previousSignIn()` (`GET /api/admin/donors/me/previous-sign-in`) and, when there was one, `overviewSince(previousSignInAt)` (`GET /api/admin/donations/overview/since?from=`), one after the other (`switchMap`): "Zjišťujeme, co je od minula nového…" (`role="status"`) while loading, a `role="alert"` "Co je od minula nového, se nepodařilo zjistit." with "Zkusit znovu" on failure; without a previous sign-in "Jste tu poprvé. Od příštího přihlášení tu uvidíte nové platby a nové dárce."; with nothing new "Poslední přihlášení {čas}. Od té doby nepřišla žádná platba a nikdo nový se nezaregistroval."; otherwise "Poslední přihlášení {čas}. Od té doby přišlo N plateb za X Kč." (one/few/other, "nepřišla žádná platba" with registrations only; `formatBankTime`) and a `dl`: "Přiřazené dárcům" ("N platby za X Kč", a link to `/sprava/platby?stav=prirazene&od=<den>`), "Čekají na přiřazení" (a link to `/sprava/platby?od=<den>`) and "Noví dárci" ("N nové registrace", a link to `/sprava/darci`), "žádná" without a link for a zero, the payment rows left out when no payment came; `<den>` is the Prague day of the sign-in (`pragueDay` in `donations/donation-date.ts`), so the payments page opens on the payments booked from that day, both tabs keeping the period. The new payments are split into the paired and the waiting ones because the payments page has no tab with both; the period filters the booking day while the card counts by import time, so a payment booked before the sign-in day but imported after it is counted and not listed (rare: the import runs hourly). Texts under `admin.home.since.*`. Texts under `admin.home.*`, the failure names `admin.home.import.failure.<kind>`, the attention card under `admin.home.attention.*`.
- `report/` (`/sprava/prehledy`, "Měsíční e-mail"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what the report is, that the page sets who gets it, when and what it carries, and that the outage alerts go to the same addresses. Three cards from `report()`. "Nastavení": the line "Příští přehled za {month} odejde {dueOn} v {hour}." (`nextReport` and `settings.sendHour`) or, when `nextReport.month` is still `lastEndedMonth` and its sending time has passed in Prague by the browser's clock (only this wording depends on it: the backend's `nextReport` keeps the date of the missed sending), "Přehled za {month} měl odejít {dueOn} v {hour}, ale zatím neodešel. Web ho zkusí poslat znovu v příští celou hodinu; poslat ho můžete i hned níže." (the hourly report check sends a due, unsent previous month; Pavel 30. 10. 2026 read the past date as an unanswered question), "Naposledy změněno {time}." or "Nastavení jste zatím neměnili.", and the form: the textarea "Příjemci" (one address per line, `EmailListValidator` in `email-list.ts` with the errors `emailList`, `emailListLength` for more than `MAX_REPORT_RECIPIENTS` = 10 and `emailListDuplicate`, whose messages name the offending address through `firstInvalidAddress` and `firstRepeatedAddress`; `parseEmailList` also accepts commas and semicolons), the select "Den odeslání" (1.–28., hint "Přehled za minulý měsíc odejde v tento den v měsíci."), the select "Hodina odeslání" (0:00–23:00, hint "Pražský čas; výchozí je 7:00."), the fieldset "Co přehled obsahuje" with the four checkboxes, and "Uložit nastavení" ("Ukládáme…"), which refuses an empty selection with the form-level "Vyberte prosím aspoň jednu část přehledu.", sends `changeReportSettings`, shows "Nastavení jsme uložili. Příští přehled odejde podle něj." right under the button (`.ui-form-status`, `role="status"`) and reloads quietly (the cards stay); a refusal shows `describeProblem` under the form. "Poslat přehled hned": the intro "Přehled za vybraný měsíc odejde hned, i když už jednou odešel. Poslat jde jen měsíc, který už skončil: přehled za {running month} půjde poslat od {its end}." (the month after `lastEndedMonth` through `followingMonth`, and the first day of the month after it; this replaced the small hint under the select, which Pavel overlooked), the line "Přehled dostanou: {recipients}. To jsou příjemci uložení v nastavení výše." (the saved `settings.recipients`, so an unsaved edit of the textarea does not count), the select "Měsíc" with the 24 months back from `lastEndedMonth` (`previousMonths` in `report-month.ts`; the label says "(odesláno {date})" or "(zatím neodesláno)"), and below it "Poslat přehled", which opens the `role="group"` "Poslat přehled?" asking "Poslat přehled za {month} na {recipients}?" with "Poslat přehled" ("Posíláme…") and "Zrušit" (focused; the select is disabled meanwhile, and cancelling, success or failure return the focus to the opening button); the result is a notice in this card: success says "Přehled za {month} jsme poslali na {recipients}." and reloads, a refusal (400 `report-month-not-ended`, 502 `report-not-delivered`) shows the backend title and detail as `role="alert"`, and after a 502 the link "Otevřít nastavení e-mailů" leads to `/sprava/nastaveni#settings-MAIL`. "Odeslané přehledy": the table Měsíc / Odesláno (`formatBankTime`) or "Zatím žádný přehled neodešel.". Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Texts under `admin.report.*`. Specs in `admin-report.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-report.spec.ts` (saving the settings, a malformed address, sending a month again, a refused e-mail) and the axe case for the page with an invalid field and the open confirmation; `e2e/admin.spec.ts` covers the overview card and the year switch.
- `backups/` (`/sprava/zalohy`, "Zálohy"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying that the site backs up every day and tries a restore once a week, and what the page offers. Two cards from `backups()`. "Stav záloh": a `role="alert"` box listing one plain sentence per problem (`BACKUP_PROBLEM_HELP` in `backup-texts.ts`: what it means for the data and what to do, usually a button on this page or copying the message for the technician and sending it to the server administrator, named as "správce serveru, který web nainstaloval" so the board knows whom to send it to (Pavel 30. 10. 2026), a wording that also fits the overview) with the technical sentence (`BACKUP_PROBLEM_TEXTS`) under "Technické podrobnosti" (the admin overview shows the same plain sentences, without the technical ones); when a problem needs the server administrator (`needsTechnician` in `backup-message.ts`: the folder missing, a missing, failed or stale backup, a failed test restore) the shared copy button "Zkopírovat zprávu pro technika" under the problems copies `technicianMessage(view)`, plain text with the plain sentences, the technical ones and the last backup and test restore (time, file, size or table count, or the failure with its error), never anything about the backup key, or the green "Zálohování je v pořádku."; a `dl` "Poslední záloha" ("{time} ({size})", "Nepovedla se {time}." or "Zatím žádná."), "Poslední zkušební obnova" (the same shapes with the table count, "Zatím žádná. Podle plánu běží v neděli 4:00." from the plan) and "Poslední obnova", each failure with its error under "Technické podrobnosti" (`RunLine`, rendered through one `ng-template`); "Soubory k obnově" only while the restore folder holds files, with their state (waits for the container's next start, restored, failed with the data kept, skipped); "Na vyřízení čeká N požadavků." (`role="status"`) when request files wait. The buttons "Zálohovat teď" (`.primary`) and "Ověřit obnovu teď" post a request, read "Zálohujeme…" / "Ověřujeme obnovu…" and poll `backups()` every 3 s (`JOB_POLL_MS`) up to 20 times (`JOB_MAX_POLLS`) until a run carrying the request id appears: a `role="status"` line ("Záloha {file} je hotová ({size}).", "Zkušební obnova zálohy {file} proběhla: N tabulek.") or a `role="alert"` line with the run's error; after 20 polls "Kontejner nfnn-backup si požadavek do minuty nevzal. …"; a refused request (409) shows the problem's title and detail. Both buttons are disabled while a job runs, while `restorePending` or when `available` is false (then a line says they wait for the problem above); while at least one backup is listed, a warning callout says the backups sit on the same disk as the site, recommends downloading the newest one once a month with Stáhnout to keep elsewhere and says they are encrypted (without a backup there is nothing to download, so it is left out). After the status card the card "Klíč k zálohám" (`backup-key-card`, `app-backup-key-card`, `id="klic-k-zaloham"`, from `backups().key`): an intro (encrypted backups, restorable only with this key, every view confirmed by the second factor and recorded in the history); without a key a danger callout (no new backups, update the app in ZimaOS); unconfirmed a warning callout "Klíč si zatím nikdo neuložil. Uložit ho má někdo ze správní rady, kdo má přístup do správy, ne technik. …", confirmed "Uložení klíče jste potvrdili {time}."; "Zobrazit klíč…" opens a form in the card: for an administrator with the code app the card first asks `AuthStore.hasPasskeys()` (`GET /api/donors/me/passkeys`, a failure counts as none) when the browser supports passkeys: with a passkey it offers the passkey first (Jakub 2026-10-29: the note, "Zobrazit klíč" runs the ceremony and has focus, "Nebo bez přístupového klíče: Zadat kód z aplikace" switches to the code), otherwise "Kód z aplikace" (six digits, focused) plus "Nebo bez kódu: Potvrdit přístupovým klíčem" where passkeys work; for one with passkeys only a note and the passkey ceremony (`AuthStore.confirmWith(null)`, as on `/pripojeni`); "Zobrazit klíč" ("Ověřujeme…") posts `AdminApi.revealBackupKey` and "Zrušit" closes; a refusal stays as a `role="alert"` line with the code field focused and selected. The revealed key (focus moves to it) shows in `app-backup-key-panel` with "Mám uloženo, ověřit" and "Skrýt klíč"; the first hides the words and shows the three word fields of the positions the reveal drew (as in the setup, focus on the first), "Ukázat slova znovu" and "Potvrdit uložení" ("Potvrzujeme…", `AdminApi.confirmBackupKey` with `{ words }`; a 409 `backup-key-confirmation-expired` hides the key and says to show it again; success hides the key again, says "Děkujeme, uložení klíče jsme si poznamenali. Klíč jsme zase skryli." and replaces the view's `key`) and "Skrýt klíč"; focus returns to "Zobrazit klíč…". The key lives only in the component's signal and is never stored. "Zálohy na serveru": a table (cards up to 640 px) Vytvořeno (Prague time, the file name small under it), Druh ("Plánovaná", "Na požádání", "Před obnovou", "Jiná"), Velikost (`backup-format.ts` `formatSize`: kB rounded up below 1 MB, else MB or GB with one decimal, NBSP before the unit) and per row "Stáhnout" and "Obnovit…" (danger outline, `aria-label` "Obnovit databázi ze zálohy {file}"), which opens `restore-dialog` (`app-restore-dialog` in `<app-dialog>`: "Obnovit databázi ze zálohy?", the file and its time, four points: everything since the backup is lost, receipts issued since then disappear but their numbers are never handed out again (a new download gets a new number), the current state is saved first as a "Před obnovou" backup, the site is down for a few minutes, the restore ends every sign-in, so everyone, the administrator and the donors, signs in again and the connector in claude.ai has to be connected again; "Zrušit" focused, the danger button "Obnovit a restartovat web", "Předáváme obnovu…" while posting, a refusal as an alert in the dialog). Once accepted, the request id and file go to `sessionStorage` (`nfnn.restore-request`, in try/catch) and the card "Obnova ze zálohy" replaces the page with a `role="status"` line while `SetupApi.watchRestart(RESTORE_MAX_POLLS)` (300 × 3 s) waits for the restart, then reloads: the run with the stored request id gives "Databáze je obnovená ze zálohy {file}. Stav před obnovou je uložený jako záloha „Před obnovou“." or the alert with its error, no such run "Kontejner nfnn-backup si obnovu do pěti minut nevzal …"; when the wait runs out, "Web se ještě nevrátil …" with "Zkontrolovat znovu". Because the request survives a sign-in in `sessionStorage`, the notice also appears after the session was lost during the restart. The status card has a row "Obnova" with the last restore. "Stáhnout" (`aria-label` "Stáhnout {file}"), which fetches the dump as a blob with the token and saves it through `saveBlob`, "Stahujeme…" meanwhile; "Zatím žádná záloha." when empty. Loading and failure as on the other pages; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog, 403 goes to `/darce/prehled`. After the list the card "Plán záloh" (`backup-schedule-card`, `app-backup-schedule-card`): "Denní záloha" (`type=time`), "Zkušební obnova" with a day `select` (pondělí … neděle) and a time, "Uložit plán" ("Ukládáme…" while saving, disabled while `available` is false), a refusal as a `role="alert"` line in the card, and a warning callout when the container reports no plan ("Plán se na serveru zatím neprojevil …", with the older-image explanation under "Technické podrobnosti") or another one; a saved plan replaces the view's `schedule` and the card says under its button "Plán záloh jsme uložili: denní záloha 2:30, zkušební obnova v sobotu 5:15." (`backup-plan.ts`: `describePlan`, `formatPlanTime` without the leading zero, the locative days). Specs in `admin-backups.spec.ts`, `backup-key-card.spec.ts`, `backup-schedule-card.spec.ts`, `backup-plan.spec.ts` and `backup-format.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-backups.spec.ts` (the status and the list with a download, the key shown with a code and its saving confirmed, a requested backup polled to its notice, a restore confirmed in the dialog and reported after the restart, the unavailable folder disabling the actions) and the axe case for the page with problems, the revealed key with a refused confirmation, the restore dialog and a busy action.
- `alerts/` (`/sprava/upozorneni`, "Upozornění na telefon", slice 2.28): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what is watched and that an alert names no person or amount. Loads `push()`; 409 `operations/push-unavailable` shows a `role="alert"` box that the server cannot push, any other failure the retry box with the problem through `describeProblem`, 401 and 403 as on the other pages; the count of `openAlerts` (missing counts as none) goes to `AppBadge.show`. "Co se hlídá": a `.ui-list` of three rows, each a link to its page ("Import plateb z banky" → `/sprava`, "Zálohy" → `/sprava/zalohy`, "Odesílání e-mailů" → `/sprava/nastaveni`) with the badge "Bez poruchy" (green) or "Nefunguje" (amber) and, for an open alert, "Nefunguje od {Prague time}" (`formatBankTime`). "Toto zařízení" (its `h2` takes the focus after turning on or off, `tabindex="-1"`): the subscription with `current: true` shows its service and day, its last delivery ("Naposledy doručeno …" or "Zatím nic nedoručeno.", "Nedoručeno N× po sobě."), the fieldset "Hlásit na tomto zařízení" with one checkbox per area (each change `changePushAlerts` with the three switches, shown at once and put back on a failure; while one saves, the checkboxes are `aria-disabled` rather than disabled, so the focused one keeps the focus, and a change meanwhile is undone; "Uloženo pro toto zařízení: {area} – zapnuto/vypnuto.") and "Vypnout na tomto zařízení" (`removePushSubscription`, then `SERVICE_WORKER_PUSH.unsubscribe`). When the browser no longer holds a subscription (`browserSubscription()` answers `not-subscribed`, read after each load while a worker controls the page) it says so and offers "Zapnout znovu"; when notifications are now blocked it says how to allow them. Without a current subscription the answer's `deviceSession` decides first: when it is not `true` (missing counts as false; a sign-in whose token names no device, `sid`: one opened before slice 2.9) one sentence says that this sign-in is older than the site's list of devices, so alerts cannot be turned on in it, and that signing out and in again makes it possible, and the browser is never asked; otherwise `core/push/push-availability.ts` (`pushAvailability(window, push)`) decides: `available` offers "Zapnout upozornění na tomto zařízení", whose click handler first calls `SERVICE_WORKER_PUSH.requestPermission()` synchronously (Safari and Firefox prompt only inside the user's gesture) and only on `granted` `requestSubscription(publicKey)` and then `subscribePush` with the endpoint and the two keys (alerts left out, so all on), after which the list is read again (the server may have replaced a device of an older session with the same endpoint); a refusal of the server unsubscribes the browser again (409 `operations/push-subscription-limit` with the page's own sentence pointing to removing a device under "Další zařízení"), a refused permission or subscribe says how to allow notifications; otherwise one paragraph says why: `home-screen` (an iPhone or iPad outside the home screen app: add the site to the home screen, open it from there and sign in there), `no-push` (no Push API), `no-service-worker` (no worker controls the page yet; re-read on every `controllerchange`) or `denied` (`Notification.permission`: allow notifications for the site in the browser settings). "Další zařízení": the other subscriptions, each a fieldset named "{Google|Apple|Mozilla|Microsoft}, zapnuto {day}" with the last delivery, the failures in a row, the same three switches ("Uloženo pro {service} ({day}): …") and "Odebrat" (`aria-label` "Odebrat zařízení {service}, zapnuto {day}", "Odebíráme zařízení …" while it runs; the card's `h2` takes the focus after the removal), or "Na jiném zařízení upozornění zapnutá nemáte."; "Zkušební upozornění", shown while the administrator has any device: "Poslat zkušební upozornění na všechna moje zařízení" (`testPush()`; "Zkušební upozornění jsme předali k doručení na {n} zařízení." with what to check when it does not arrive, its own sentence when it reached none, the failed and the removed devices in Czech plurals, `pluralCategory`, the list read again when a device was removed; the 429 of the limit through `describeProblem`); each card ends with its `app-live-notice`.
- `logs/` (`/sprava/logy`, "Logy a diagnostika"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying the page is for technical help: the server administrator finds the details here when the site misbehaves, nothing needs setting. It loads `logFiles()`, `logLevels()` and `diagnostics()` together and opens `backend.log` (or the first file). "Soubory logu": a table (cards up to 640 px) Soubor (mono, "komprimovaný" badge for `.gz`), Změněno, Velikost and "Zobrazit" (`aria-label` "Zobrazit {file}", `aria-pressed`; the shown file's row has an accent bar); a red sentence when the backend does not see the folder, "Složka logů je prázdná." when empty. "Výpis {file}": the search form (`app-form-field` "Hledat", required, max 200, hint about regular expressions and the 50 newest matches) posts `searchLog`, and "Chyby z prohlížečů" beside "Hledat" fills it with `Browser error` (`BROWSER_ERRORS_PATTERN`) and searches at once, so the browser error reports and the hourly cap line are listed (contract "Browser error reports"); the matches (`role="status"`, Czech plural count, newest first) are buttons that open the window at their offset, "Načíst starší shody" pages with `before = scannedFrom` and appends; a refused pattern shows the problem in `role="alert"`. The pager "← Starší" (window ending at `from`, 20 kB, `WINDOW_BYTES`), "Novější →" (from `to`), "Na konec" and the range ("Celý log", "Konec logu", "Začátek logu" or "Starší část logu", `aria-live="polite"`) drive `logWindow`; the text is a focusable `pre` (60vh, scrolls) with `<app-copy-button what="výpis logu">` above it. "Úroveň logování": an intro (raise only while looking for a fault; it goes back by itself after the time and after a restart; audited), a form Logger (`datalist` of the watched loggers, `pattern` ROOT or a dotted Java name, required, max 200), Úroveň (`select` TRACE…OFF, DEBUG by default), Na kolik minut (1–1440, 30) and "Nastavit" ("Nastavujeme…"), which puts the level and shows "Logger {name} má úroveň {level} do {time}." (`role="status"`) or the problem; the table Logger, Nastaveno ("zděděno" when inherited), Platí, Dočasně ("do {time}") with "Vrátit" (`aria-label` "Vrátit úroveň loggeru {name}", "Vracíme…") on raised rows (accent bar), "Logger {name} má zase původní úroveň." afterwards; levels and diagnostics reload after each change. "Stav aplikace": "Stav k {time}." and a `dl` Java, Běží od, Paměť (heap), Vlákna, Úklid paměti, Zatížení, Databáze (red when unreachable), Složky (free of total per folder, red "složka není připojená"), Dočasné úrovně ("Žádné." or the list); "Obnovit stav" reloads `diagnostics()`, "Zobrazit výpis vláken" / "Skrýt výpis vláken" (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`) loads the thread dump into a `pre`. Loading and failure as on the other pages; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog, 403 goes to `/darce/prehled`. Specs in `admin-logs.spec.ts` (paging, a `.gz` file, the search with older matches and a refused pattern, the browser error preset, a raised and reset level, the form's errors, a refused level, the diagnostics and the thread dump, the missing folder, the lost session); Playwright `e2e/admin-logs.spec.ts` with the mocks in `e2e/admin-logs-api.ts` (the tail, older lines, a search opening a match; a level raised and reset; the thread dump on a phone) and the axe case for the page with matches, a form error and the thread dump.
- `people/` (`/sprava/lide-fondu`, "Lidé fondu"; the API in `features/content/people-api.ts`, `PeopleApi`: `load()`, `mine()`, `add`, `update`, `remove`, `order`, `saveNote`, `saveFootnote`, `setWebSignature`, `removeWebSignature` over `/api/admin/content/people…`; Jakub 2026-10-30): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying where the people show and that the site keeps its own block while nobody is here. The card "Lidé v pořadí na webu" is an ordered list in the shared block `.ui-person-list` (`styles/ui/_person-list.scss`, used by the administrators too): a small round portrait (`app-avatar`, the initials without one), the name, the role and badges ("Karta s portrétem", "Ve správní radě (…)", "Podepisuje potvrzení", "Propojeno s účtem na webu", a `ul`), under them the person's signatures as labelled previews (Jakub 2026-10-31, so they can be checked on a phone; each a `figure` with its `figcaption`, side by side and wrapping at 390 px): "Podpis na webu", the public web signature (`?w=480`, at most 48 px high like the site's, `alt` "Podpis na webu: <name>", `loading="lazy"`), and "Podpis na potvrzení", the receipt signature read from the browser-only `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatures/{personId}` and drawn by `app-signature-artwork` (the vector through `[attr.d]`, a scan as `img`, label "Podpis na potvrzení: <name>", at most 160 px wide). The receipt previews are fetched for the people the signatory view lists in `signatures`, once per person and upload (kept by `personId@uploadedAt`, so reading the signatory again fetches only a changed or new signature); while one loads the line says "Načítáme náhled…", and a failed one only "Náhled se nepodařilo načíst." under its caption, never an alert, and the buttons "Výš", "Níž" (`aria-label` "Výš: <name>", disabled at the ends; the order is saved at once and focus stays on the moved person) and "Upravit" (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls="person-editor"`), then "Přidat osobu" (disabled at 30). The editor card under it holds the name (required), the role, the checkboxes for the card and the board line, the board note while on the board, the fieldset "E-maily jen pro správu" with the personal and the fund e-mail (each optional, checked against the backend's shape, never shown on the site), the portrait (`FileButton` and `NewsApi.uploadImage`, preview in an `app-avatar`, "Bez portrétu"), and saving ("Přidat a ukázat na webu" / "Uložit a ukázat na webu") or closing; for a saved person also the web signature (uploaded and set at once, PNG with a transparent background preferred, replaced or removed; the hint says it is never the receipt signature and only the browser sets it) then the part "Na potvrzení o daru" (slice 1.16, reads `receiptSignatory()` of `admin-api.ts` and keeps its state: "Načítáme, kdo potvrzení podepisuje…" while it loads and, when it fails, the alert "Nepodařilo se zjistit, kdo podepisuje potvrzení." with "Zjistit znovu", which moves the focus to the part's heading "Na potvrzení o daru" (`tabindex="-1"`) while it asks again, never a claim that nobody signs; until it is known the part offers no choice and "Odebrat osobu z webu" stays disabled; when it arrives after the signatory's editor opened, their receipt role is filled in): for the person who signs the line that they sign and that only one person signs; otherwise the checkbox "Podepisuje potvrzení" with a hint naming who signs now or that nobody does; then the field "Funkce na potvrzení" (required, at most 120, Jakub 2026-10-31: the receipt's own role, the role on the site stays) and "Uložit pro potvrzení" (`chooseReceiptSignatory`, `PUT /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory`; "Potvrzení vystavená od teď podepisuje … s funkcí …"), and the receipt signature `<app-receipt-signature [personId] [signs] (busyChange)>` (`features/admin/receipt-signature/`, moved from the settings in 1.16; it drops its pending requests when another person is shown, reports a running upload or removal so the page blocks switching people and "Zavřít bez uložení" until it answers, moves the focus to its heading on "Zkusit znovu", and for a person who does not sign says "Podpis je uložený. Potvrzení ho ponesou, až je bude tato osoba podepisovat.", asks "Tato osoba potvrzení teď nepodepisuje. Odebrat její podpis?" and answers "Podpis je odebraný."; per person `GET`/`POST`/`DELETE /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatures/{personId}`; the hint says it is printed on the receipts this person signs and never public, best an SVG of filled shapes up to 200 kB, else a JPEG/PNG/WebP scan up to 10 MB; "Podpis není nahraný. …" or the scan or vector in a dashed frame with `forced-color-adjust: none` and "Nahráno …"; "Nahrát podpis na potvrzení" / "Nahrát jiný podpis na potvrzení", a refused SVG with the backend's title and detail, "Odebrat podpis z potvrzení" with the group "Odebrat podpis z potvrzení?"; texts under `admin.signature.*`, spec `receipt-signature.spec.ts`); a list row of the signing person carries the badge "Podepisuje potvrzení"; and the removal (its intro says the portrait and the web signature leave the site and the database, from backups within 30 days, that the receipt signature goes on no further receipt, and that issued receipts keep their copies of the signature and the portrait) with an inline confirmation ("Odebrat a smazat", "Zrušit"), which for the person who signs is replaced by a warning that another signatory must be chosen first. The card "Slovo zakladatelů" has a textarea of at most 600 characters, and the card "Řádek pod správní radou" one text field of at most 200 characters ("Uložit řádek"); an empty text removes either. Every outcome is a line in `app-live-notice` (list, editor, signature, note, line); a 401 sends to the sign-in, a 403 to `/darce/prehled`. Spec `admin-people.spec.ts`, `people-api.spec.ts`; e2e `e2e/admin-people.spec.ts` (with choosing the signatory and the receipt signature: a scan, a vector, a refused SVG, a scan and a vector replacing each other, the vector in a dark forced-colours mode), an axe case in `accessibility.spec.ts`, a line in `theme.spec.ts`.
- `focal-point-picker/` (slice 2.23b): `<app-focal-point-picker inputId [imageId] [shapes] [hint] [(point)]>` shows the photo (`?w=960`) as one button "Fotka, klepnutím nastavíte bod výřezu"; a tap places the point where it lands (percent of the image's box, kept within 0–100; a click from the keyboard, `detail` 0, is ignored), the arrow keys move it by 5 from the centre or the point, a marker shows it and "Zrušit bod" removes it. A polite live line says "Bod výřezu: X % zleva, Y % shora." or "Bez bodu výřezu: web fotku ořízne jako dosud.", and a figure per shape in `shapes` previews the crop live (`?w=480`, `appFocalPoint`). The news and project editors show it in place of their former preview with the shapes "Karta v Aktualitách"/"Karta v Projektech" and "Nahoře v článku", the About page without previews and with the hint that the site still shows that photo whole; on saving, each page sends a moved point to `PUT /api/admin/content/media/{id}` first (`PhotosApi`, `features/content/photos-api.ts`), then saves the item only when something else changed too and the point went through; a placed point becomes the saved one at once, so a failed save of the item afterwards sends only the item again. The news and project editors count a moved point as unsaved (the leave guard and `beforeunload`); the About page, which has no leave guard, sends it with its Uložit button.
- `about/` (`/sprava/o-nas`, "Fotka v sekci O nás"; the API in `features/content/about-api.ts`, `AboutApi`: `load()` → `GET /api/admin/content/about`, `save(request)` → `PUT`, `AboutResponse`, `AboutPhotoResponse`, `AboutRequest`; Jakub 2026-10-30: the photo shows a child, so it is uploaded here instead of living in the repository): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying where the photo shows, that the stock photo stays until one is saved and that it can be removed at any time. The card "Fotka" shows the saved or the freshly uploaded photo as a preview (`?w=480`) or "Fotka zatím není nahraná, web ukazuje ilustrační fotku.", the `app-file-button` "Nahrát fotku" / "Vyměnit fotku" (JPEG, PNG or WebP up to 10 MB checked before the upload, which goes at once through `NewsApi.uploadImage`; "Nahráváme fotku…", then "Fotka je nahraná. Na web se dostane, až ji uložíte."), and with a photo the fields "Popis pro čtečky (nepovinné)" and "Popisek pod fotkou (nepovinné)" (200 characters each) and "Uložit a ukázat na webu" ("Ukládáme…"; the texts trimmed, blank as null), then "Uloženo. Fotka je na webu v sekci O nás." or the backend's refusal. The danger card "Odebrání fotky" (only with a saved photo) says the photo leaves the site and the database at once and the backups within 30 days, and "Odebrat fotku z webu" asks inline "Opravdu fotku odebrat z webu a smazat?" with "Odebrat a smazat" ("Odebíráme…") / "Zrušit" (focused on open); the removal `PUT`s no photo and no texts, says "Fotku jsme z webu odebrali a smazali. Sekce O nás ukazuje zase ilustrační fotku." and focuses the upload button. 401 and 403 as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-about.spec.ts`, e2e in `e2e/admin-about.spec.ts`.
- `news/` (`/sprava/aktuality`, "Aktuality"; the API in `features/content/news-api.ts`, `NewsApi`, with `slugFromText` and `mediaUrl(id, width)`): `admin-news` lists every article from `GET /api/admin/content/news` as a table (cards up to 640 px) Titulek (a link to the editor), Štítek ("bez štítku"), Stav ("Koncept" amber, "Na webu od {date}" green when `live`, "Zveřejní se {date}" accent while the date is still ahead), Upraveno (`formatBankTime`); "Nová aktualita" opens the editor; "Zatím tu žádná aktualita není. …" when empty. After a deletion the list says "Aktualitu „{title}“ jsme smazali." (the title comes in the navigation's `state.deleted`, read through `Router.currentNavigation()`). `admin-news-editor` (`/sprava/aktuality/nova` and `/sprava/aktuality/:id`, `h1` "Nová aktualita" / "Úprava aktuality" focused on open; the link "← Aktuality"): the card "Obsah" with the form Titulek (required, 120), Adresa na webu (nepovinné) (made from the title with `slugFromText` until the administrator types into it; the hint names `nfnn.cz/aktuality/<slug>` and, for a published article whose address changed, adds "Pozor: dosavadní odkaz nfnn.cz/aktuality/{old} po uložení přestane fungovat."; pattern and 80 characters; a 409 `content/slug-taken` marks this field "Tuto adresu už má jiná aktualita nebo projekt. Zvolte prosím jinou." and focuses it), Štítek (nepovinné) (40, hint: the word above the date), Krátký souhrn (required, 300, hint: shown on the card in the list), Text (the Markdown toolbar above the textarea, required, 20 000; the hint says to separate paragraphs with an empty line, format with the buttons and check the look with the switch Náhled at the top) and Podepsáno: a `<select>` "Bez podpisu" and the people of the foundation from `PeopleApi.load()` (the article's own signer stays among them when the list cannot be read, so saving keeps it); a new article preselects the person linked to the administrator (`PeopleApi.mine()`), sent as `signerId`. Fotka (nepovinné): `<app-file-button>` "Nahrát fotku" / "Vyměnit fotku" (`accept` JPEG, PNG, WebP) that uploads at once through `POST /api/admin/content/media` ("Nahráváme fotku…", then "Fotka je nahraná. K aktualitě se připojí, až ji uložíte." in a `role="status"` region), refuses another type or more than 10 MB before sending, shows the backend's words on a refusal (413 `upload-too-large` included) and the preview `<img>` from `/api/content/media/{id}?w=480` with "Odebrat fotku" and, only with a photo, the field "Popis fotky (nepovinné)" (200, hint: what is on the photo for people who cannot see it; sent as `coverImageAlt`, null when blank or without a photo) with the line "Fotka zatím nemá popis pro nevidomé." while it is blank. Under the `h1` the `ui-segmented` group "Zobrazení editoru" switches "Upravit" / "Náhled" (`aria-pressed`, Jakub 2026-10-24): "Náhled" hides the fields (`[hidden]`, so the form keeps its values, touched and dirty state) and shows `features/admin/content-preview` (`app-content-preview [content]`, `{ kind, item }`): the line "Takhle to uvidí návštěvníci webu, včetně neuložených změn. …", the heading "Karta na úvodní stránce a v seznamu aktualit" over the public `app-news-card` without its link on the home section's background, and "Stránka aktuality" over the public `app-content-article` in preview mode; the item is a snapshot of the form taken when the preview opens (the address made from the title when blank, the uploaded photo, the chosen date), so nothing is saved or sent. The last field of the form is "Datum zveřejnění" (`type="date"`, today for a draft, the published date otherwise; its hint says who sees the article: "Aktualitu zatím vidíte jen vy. Tlačítko Uložit a zveřejnit ji pustí na web s tímto datem, …", "Aktualita je na webu, změny se ukážou hned. …" or "Na web se aktualita dostane {date}, do té doby ji vidíte jen vy. …"). The form ends in the sticky action bar (`.ui-action-bar` from `styles/ui/_action-bar.scss`, `position: sticky` at the bottom of the viewport while the form scrolls; `shared/ui/action-bar` (`appActionBar`) writes its height into `--action-bar-height` on `html`, whose `scroll-padding-bottom` then keeps a focused field above the bar on phones). The bar shows the state from the API's `live` (never the browser's clock): "Koncept" (amber), "Na webu" (green) or "Zveřejní se {date}" (`ui-badge--accent`), then "Máte neuložené změny." or "Naposledy uloženo {at}."; its buttons: for a new article or a draft "Uložit koncept" (the first submit button, so Enter in a field saves a draft) and the primary "Uložit a zveřejnit" (creates or saves, then publishes with the date; a missing date marks the field "Vyberte prosím datum zveřejnění."); for a published one the primary "Uložit a ukázat na webu" ("Uložit změny" while it waits for its date), which saves and publishes again when the date changed, "Stáhnout z webu" ("Zrušit zveřejnění" while scheduled) and, only when `live`, the link "Otevřít na webu" (`/aktuality/{slug}`, new window). A running request names itself ("Ukládáme…", "Zveřejňujeme…", "Stahujeme…") and disables the bar. Results show in the bar's `app-live-notice`: "Aktualitu jsme uložili jako koncept. Na web ji dostanete tlačítkem Uložit a zveřejnit." (the URL becomes `/sprava/aktuality/{id}` without a reload and the tab title turns to the edit title), "Změny jsme uložili.", "Změny jsme uložili a na webu už jsou.", "Změny jsme uložili. Aktualita se na webu ukáže {date}.", "Aktualita je na webu s datem {date}.", "Aktualita se na webu ukáže {date}.", "Aktualitu jsme z webu stáhli. Zůstává tu jako koncept.", and refusals through `describeProblem`. An invalid form submitted from the preview switches back to "Upravit" and focuses the first invalid field. The card "Smazání" (saved articles only): "Smazat aktualitu" opens the `role="group"` "Smazat aktualitu?" asking "Opravdu smazat aktualitu „{title}“?" with "Smazat natrvalo" ("Mažeme…") and "Zrušit" (focused when it opens; cancelling or a failure, shown in this card, returns the focus to "Smazat aktualitu"); the deletion returns to the list with its notice. Leaving the editor with unsaved changes (edited fields, or a photo uploaded and not yet saved) is held by `features/admin/unsaved-changes/unsaved-changes-guard.ts` (`unsavedChangesGuard`, a `canDeactivate` on the four editor routes in `admin.routes.ts`, calling the page's `canLeave()` from `GuardsUnsavedChanges`), which opens `unsaved-changes-dialog` ("Máte neuložené změny", "Když stránku teď opustíte, rozepsané změny i nově nahraná fotka se ztratí.", "Zůstat a uložit" focused, which closes the dialog and submits the form, and "Odejít bez uložení"; Esc, × and the backdrop stay); closing or reloading the tab meanwhile triggers the browser's own `beforeunload` question. Texts under `admin.unsavedChanges.*`. Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages; a 404 `content/news-not-found` returns to the list. Texts under `admin.news.*` and `admin.newsEditor.*`. Specs `admin-news.spec.ts`, `admin-news-editor.spec.ts`, `unsaved-changes-guard.spec.ts`, `features/content/news-api.spec.ts` (fixtures in `features/content/testing/news-fixtures.ts`); Playwright `e2e/admin-news.spec.ts` (the list and the way to the editor, a new article with a photo checked in the preview and published, a missing title, a taken address at its field, withdrawing and deleting with the list's notice, leaving with unsaved changes, the action bar on a phone, a scheduled article) and the axe case for the list, the editor with its errors, its preview and the deletion confirmation.

the same unsaved-changes guard) and add the year and the category: the list has the columns Rok and Kategorie (Děti, Dospělí, Zvířata, Ostatní from `projectCategoryLabel` in `features/content/project-category.ts`, ids `content.project.category.*`) after the title and its intro points to the card Řazení na webu under the list (`project-order-card`, its own `GET`/`PUT /api/admin/content/project-order`; messages "Vyplňte prosím rok projektu.", "Rok nemůže být před založením fondu, tedy před 2005.", "Zadejte prosím rok nejvýše 2100."), the tag hint names a project area ("Kompenzační pomůcky"), the perex hint the project card, the address hint `nfnn.cz/projekty/<slug>`, and the date hint says the projects on the same place of the order come from the one published last;
