Texts under `admin.projects.*` and `admin.projectEditor.*`. Specs `admin-projects.spec.ts`, `admin-project-editor.spec.ts`, `features/content/projects-api.spec.ts` (fixtures in `features/content/testing/project-fixtures.ts`); Playwright `e2e/admin-projects.spec.ts` (the same five journeys with the year in the table and the requests, and choosing the order by category, with the description of the choice and the list asked again; `project-order-card.spec.ts` for the card) and the axe case with a year before 2005 among the errors.
- `settings/` (`/sprava/nastaveni`, "Nastavení"): `h1` focused on open, an intro (what the page holds, the technical values at the bottom, that secrets are stored encrypted and never shown, that saving restarts the site). The page is ordered by how often a part is needed (Jakub 2026-10-24), not by the backend's groups: the cards "Potvrzení o daru" (`RECEIPT_FIRST_YEAR`), "Kdo potvrzení podepisuje" (below), "Kontaktní formulář" (`CONTACT_RECIPIENT`), "Odesílatel e-mailů" (`MAIL_FROM`) and "Přihlášení" (`SESSION_REMEMBER_DAYS`, plan v3 2.10, `nastaveni-prihlaseni`), each with a one-line intro, then the card "Technické nastavení (pro správce serveru)" with the warning callout "Chybná hodnota tady může zastavit odesílání e-mailů nebo stahování plateb z banky. Než změny e-mailů uložíte, pošlete si zkušební e-mail." and a `<details id="technicke-nastaveni">` (summary "Adresa webu, server pro odesílání e-mailů a napojení na banku", folded unless a secret is unreadable, a refused value is technical or the address points into it) holding the parts "Adresa webu" (`PUBLIC_URL`), "Server pro odesílání e-mailů" (`MAIL_HOST` … `MAIL_STARTTLS`) and "Banka" (`FIO_*`, `PAIRING_EXCLUDED_ACCOUNTS`) as `h3`. The sections are `EVERYDAY` and `TECHNICAL` in `admin-settings.ts`; every field keeps the labels of `setting-labels.ts`, the fields render from one `ng-template`, and the changes are sent in the backend's order. Headings take `tabindex="-1"` with Czech ids `nastaveni-<part>` (`nastaveni-potvrzeni`, `-kontaktni-formular`, `-odesilatel`, `-technicke`, `-adresa-webu`, `-odesilani-emailu`, `-banka`); `FRAGMENT_ALIASES` keeps the older addresses, so `/sprava/nastaveni#settings-MAIL` (the link from the report page) opens the technical settings and focuses "Server pro odesílání e-mailů". Fields: `MAIL_AUTH` and `MAIL_STARTTLS` as checkboxes, `FIO_HISTORY_FROM` as a date, a secret as a `role="group"` labelled by its state line "<label>: Nastaveno / Nenastaveno" (amber "Uložená hodnota nejde přečíst, zadejte ji prosím znovu." when `unreadable`) with the field named after the secret ("Nové heslo nebo klíč SMTP", "Nový token Fio API", else "Nová hodnota: {label}") in `<app-password-reveal>` and, when a row is stored, the checkbox "Odebrat uloženou hodnotu", which once ticked shows what the removal stops (`REMOVAL_EFFECTS`: no e-mail at all without the SMTP password, no bank import without the Fio token); every other setting a text field with its value. The mail server part ends with the hint "Pošle e-mail na vaši adresu {own e-mail} s údaji z formuláře, i když ještě nejsou uložené, …" and "Poslat zkušební e-mail na moji adresu" ("Posíláme zkušební e-mail…"): the form's server, port, user, sign-in, STARTTLS and sender (a blank password means the stored one, which the backend uses only for the running server), the result from `testEmailNotice` beside it. The card "Kdo potvrzení podepisuje" (`settings/receipt-signatory/`, `<app-receipt-signatory>`, slice 1.16, inside the form after "Potvrzení o daru" but with its own requests and no control of the form) reads `receiptSignatory()` and the people (`PeopleApi.load()`, for the portrait) and, when the signatory has a receipt signature, `receiptSignature(personId)`: "Načítáme, kdo potvrzení podepisuje…", a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", then the preview of the 3B signature block on the grey card of the token `--color-surface-muted` without a border as a `figure` named by its caption "Náhled podpisu na potvrzení" (the round portrait `?w=240`, or no portrait at all, as the receipt drops it, "Za Nová naděje - nadační fond:", the signature drawn by `<app-signature-artwork>` with the label "Podpis na potvrzení", at most 220 px wide, over a rule spanning the text column in the token `--color-border-rule` (`#b8bec6` as in 3B, about 1.7:1 on the card; decorative, not text), the name and the receipt role; `forced-color-adjust: none`, so a dark forced-colours mode keeps the paper and the ink) and a warning callout naming what is missing (portrait, signature or both) or, with `portraitPrintable` false, that the portrait cannot be prepared for print and receipts go out without it ("Portrét této osoby nejde připravit pro tisk…"), together with the missing signature in one sentence when that is missing too; with nobody chosen a static danger callout (no live role, since it shows on every visit) "Potvrzení o daru teď nikdo nepodepisuje, a tak je web dárcům nevystaví. …"; always the link "Otevřít lidi fondu" to `/sprava/lide-fondu`, where the signatory, the receipt role and the signature are changed. Texts under `admin.signatory.*`, spec `receipt-signatory.spec.ts`. After it the card "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům" (`settings/receipt-signing-key/`, `<app-receipt-signing-key>`, its `h2` `#klic-k-overovacim-qr` with `tabindex="-1"`, the fragment the overview's red row opens): an intro in the present tense since receipts are signed (1.6: "Server podepisuje ověřovací QR kódy na potvrzeních o daru svým klíčem.", worded so it still reads true while that key is withdrawn; the site publishes its public part so that receipts can be verified, if it may have leaked, withdraw it and the server makes a new one), "Načítáme klíč…", then a `dl` of "Klíč serveru" (the key id), "Zaregistrovaný" (`formatBankTime`) and "Stav" ("Zneplatněný", "Zveřejněný" or "Nezveřejněný"), the line "Zveřejněné jsou i dřívější klíče: <id> (zaregistrovaný <time>)." for the published keys of earlier servers (", zneplatněný" inside the brackets for a withdrawn one) (when the server's own row is missing, the `dl` shows its id and "Nezveřejněný" only), and in one `role="alert"` region a red callout per warning: the overview's sentence for every key whose seal fails, "Klíč serveru <id> je zneplatněný a server zatím nemá nový, …" while the server's own key is withdrawn and no restart is under way, and one sentence per audit mismatch (`settings/receipt-signing-key/key-warnings.ts`: "Klíč <id> byl ve správě zneplatněný, ale jeho záznam v databázi je znovu aktivní …" for `reverted`, "Klíč <id> je v historii správy, ale jeho záznam v databázi chybí …" for `missing`). Below, a small danger-outline button per key that can be withdrawn (published and `active`, or `revoked` only by the trail while its row is `reverted`): "Zneplatnit klíč serveru…" first, then "Zneplatnit klíč <id>…" for earlier keys, "Zneplatnit klíč <id> znovu…" for a reverted one. It asks `AuthStore.hasPasskeys()` first (the button stays focusable, `aria-disabled` meanwhile, so the dialog returns focus to it) and opens `<app-key-withdrawal-dialog>` (`key-withdrawal-dialog.ts`, on `<app-dialog>`, labelled "Zneplatnit klíč <id>?" with the id in `ui-mono`): a lead (withdraw only when it may have leaked; it cannot be undone), points (the site keeps publishing it as withdrawn, so its QR codes are not to be trusted, earlier receipts included and not re-signed; for the server's key the site restarts at once and makes a new key, about a minute without receipts; for an earlier key nothing restarts; what to answer donors who write about a receipt under that key (the verification page no longer verifies it and the web cannot issue a new one yet, so the administrator confirms its data by its number; slice 1.21); it is recorded in the history and e-mailed to every administrator), then the fresh second factor exactly as the backup key card asks it (the code field with `autofocus`, "Nebo bez kódu: Potvrdit přístupovým klíčem" only when the administrator has a passkey, or the passkey first with "Zadat kód z aplikace"), the refusal in a `role="alert"` line, "Zneplatnit klíč" (`ui-button--danger`, "Zneplatňujeme klíč…" while sending) and "Zrušit" (`autofocus` when no code field is shown). The answer replaces the card's keys; the card focuses its `h2` and an `<app-live-notice>` says "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný." or, with `restarting`, "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný. Web se restartuje a vytvoří si nový klíč, obvykle do minuty." while `watchKeyRenewal` (`settings/receipt-signing-key/key-renewal.ts`) asks for the keys every 3 s (`RESTART_POLL_MS`), at most 100 times (`KEY_RENEWAL_MAX_POLLS`, five minutes, room for a slow mail run before the backend restarts, review 1.13 r2). It decides only once the server went down and answered again, or the answer names another `currentKeyId`; an answer that still names the withdrawn key from a server that never went down (the notices are still being sent) claims nothing and keeps waiting. Only an unreachable web counts as down (status 0, 502, 503 or 504, what the frontend nginx answers while the backend restarts, review 1.13 r3); any other failure (a 500, a 429, a 401 the refresh could not mend, a 403) proves no restart and does not end the wait, so a single blip before the restart can no longer turn the withdrawn key that follows into the "no new key" alert. Then "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný a web po restartu podepisuje novým klíčem." when the new key is published (`currentKeyPublished`) and not withdrawn; the alert "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný a web po restartu má nový klíč <new-id>, který ale zatím není zveřejněný." when it is not published; and when the web came back with the same key the alert "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný, ale web po restartu nový klíč nemá, takže se potvrzení o daru nestáhnou; …" (the server signs nothing while its key is withdrawn), which replaces the card's own warning about it. When the polls run out, the restarting line goes and only an alert stays, with "Zkusit znovu", which watches again: "Klíč je zneplatněný, ale web se po restartu zatím nespustil. …" when the web went down and has not answered since, and "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný, ale web se zatím nerestartoval a nový klíč nemá, takže se potvrzení o daru nestáhnou. Počkejte pár minut a zkuste to znovu; když se nic nezmění, obraťte se prosím na technika serveru." (`admin.signingKey.notRestarted`) when it never went down in the whole wait (the request for a new seed could not be written, or the notices never finished), so the card never claims a restart that did not happen. a failed load says "Klíč se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu". Texts under `admin.signingKey.*`, spec `receipt-signing-key.spec.ts`. Without a settings key a `role="alert"` box says in plain words that secrets cannot be saved and the server administrator helps with the key (the missing `secrets/settings-key` under "Technické podrobnosti"), and their fields are disabled; with a key, unreadable secrets are named in a `role="alert"` box at the top ("Tyto tajné údaje jsou uložené jiným klíčem, než má tento server, třeba po obnově ze zálohy na novém serveru: {labels}. Dokud je znovu nezadáte a neuložíte, nebudou fungovat."), which is how a restore of an older backup on a new server asks for the SMTP password and the Fio token again (Jakub 2026-10-23; since 2026-10-29 a backup carries them under the backup key). "Uložit nastavení" computes the changes (trimmed; a secret only when typed or removed); nothing changed says "Nic jste nezměnili." right under the button (`.ui-form-status`); otherwise the `role="group"` "Uložit nastavení?" beside the button lists "Mění se: {labels}." (a removed secret as "{label} (odebrat)"), a danger callout per removed secret with its effect, a warning when the mail server settings or the sender changed without a sent test e-mail for exactly those values ("Zkušební e-mail jste s novými údaji neposlali. …"), and asks "Uložením se web restartuje a asi minutu nebude odpovídat. Uložit?" with "Uložit a restartovat" ("Ukládáme…") and "Zrušit" (focused; cancelling returns the focus to "Uložit nastavení"). A 400 lists the refused values by label under the form; success shows the restart line with a spinner while `watchRestart()` runs, reloads the settings and says "Nastavení je uložené a web po restartu běží s ním.", or after 100 polls an alert saying the site has not come back yet, to wait and then ask the server administrator (the container log under "Technické podrobnosti"), with "Zkusit znovu". Loading, a failed load, 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Texts under `admin.settings.*`. Specs `admin-settings.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-settings.spec.ts` (a value and a secret changed, only the changes sent, the restart waited for; the order of the parts and a test e-mail with a new sender before saving; the report page's link opening the mail server part; the signatory: the preview of the signature block, the warning when nobody signs, the block in a dark forced-colours mode) and the axe case for the page with its confirmation and a refused test e-mail.

- `changed-meanwhile/` (plan v3 2.18, contract "Stale writes"): `ChangedMeanwhileDialog`, an `<app-dialog>` sheet "Mezitím se to změnilo" shown when a versioned write answers 409 `…/changed-meanwhile` (`isChangedMeanwhile`, `versionInRefusal`, `savedAtInRefusal` and `ifMatch` in `core/version/row-version.ts`; every versioned call of `admin-api.ts`, `news-api.ts` and `projects-api.ts` takes the `version` of the record it was read with and sends it as `If-Match`). It has three forms. On a page of actions (payments, older-symbol answers, the donor card's bank accounts), and in an editor whose refused action had no unsaved edits, it says the change was not made and offers "Načíst aktuální stav", which reads the page again (closing it does the same); its lead names what changed (`subject`: a payment, a donor's account link, an older-symbol answer, an article or project). In a form whose save was refused (the monthly report settings, the news and project editors; `keepsDraft` with `canOverwrite`) it says the edits stayed in the form and offers "Uložit svou verzi místo novější", which sends the form again with the version from the refusal (a refused publish keeps its intent and publishes after saving), and "Zahodit své úpravy a načíst novější verzi", which reads the record again; no button has the focus at opening, so Enter only closes the sheet, and closing it keeps the form as typed. When a publish, withdrawal or deletion is refused while the editor holds unsaved edits (`keepsDraft` without `canOverwrite`), it says the change was not made and the edits stay, tells how to go on (save them first or discard them), and offers "Nechat úpravy ve formuláři" and the discard button; after a refused deletion focus returns to "Smazat aktualitu" or "Smazat projekt". Where the refusal's record carries it, the sheet says when the newer version was saved. Its buttons wrap and stack below the phone breakpoint (the device steps open it in every pose, `admin-changed-meanwhile`), and its lead is tied to the dialog by `describedBy` (an input of `<app-dialog>` bound to `aria-describedby`). After a reload each page announces it in its status line once the data has arrived and moves focus there (the editors and Přehledy to their heading); the report form is pristine again (`DirtyForms`), and a repeated refusal while saving over is announced through `DialogAnnouncer`.

## Theme, fonts, assets

- The theme is a folder chosen at build time: `src/themes/nova-nadeje/_theme.scss` declares the `@font-face` rules and every token on `:root` (inventory in `frontend.md`), `styles.scss` loads it as `@use 'theme'` through `stylePreprocessorOptions.includePaths` (`src/styles` and the theme folder), and the configuration `control-theme` swaps in `src/themes/control/` (a deliberately different look with system fonts, built and checked by `e2e/theme.spec.ts` in CI only; `scripts/check-themes.mjs`, part of `npm run lint:styles`, keeps the token sets equal). The files of a theme's look live in its `assets/` folder and are served at the site root (each build configuration maps its theme's `assets/` with `output: "/"`, plan v3 2.22): `favicon.svg`, `favicon.ico`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `app-icon.svg` and `images/logo.svg` and `images/logo-on-dark.svg`, at the addresses they had in `public/`; the control theme carries copies of the same files for now. `check-themes.mjs` fails when a theme lacks a file of `nova-nadeje` or has one more, when a theme file is also in `public/` (the build would serve one of them unseen), and when a build serves no `assets/` of its own theme or another theme's. The receipt PDF still draws the logo from `receipt-logo.ts` (`receipt-logo.spec.ts` compares it with the `nova-nadeje` logo), so a theme with another logo also changes that file. The theme also carries the tokens of the app layout (2.22, used from 2.23): the floating tab bar of decision 2.1 (Jakub 2026-10-31 08:29 and 13:43: `--color-tab-bar` navy glass at 66 % with `--blur-bar` 22 px and `--shadow-tab-bar` `0 8px 24px` at 25 % as the variant C mockup draws them (its `saturate(160%)` and 1.5 px rim are left to 2.23, which draws the bar) and the rim `--color-tab-bar-rim` white at 35 %, the active pill `--color-tab-pill` navy at 88 % with `--color-tab-active` `#93c5fd`, labels `--color-on-tab-bar`, focus ring `--color-tab-focus-ring` white, `--tab-bar-height` 62 px as a minimum that grows with the text, `--tab-bar-gap` 20 px to the sides and also the installed app's bottom offset (`max(var(--tab-bar-gap), env(safe-area-inset-bottom) - 13px)`, iOS expert 2026-10-31, so 2.23 adds no token for it), `--tab-bar-gap-browser` 16 px above Safari's row, `--tab-pill-inset` 2 px (5 px until the pill was drawn without a rim, Jakub 2026-11-06, so it fills the bar as in a native iOS tab bar), `--color-tab-pill-rim` transparent (the control theme keeps its bar's rim on the pill), `--shadow-tab-bar`; solid under `prefers-reduced-transparency`: the bar the glass over white, `#616672` (white labels 5.75:1), the pill navy `#0f172a` (3.1:1 against the bar), the control theme its glass over white `#5c6662` with a pill darkened to `#0b110e`, because its plain bar colour gave 2.99:1; even the decided glass leaves its pill under 3:1 against the bar, so 2.23 marks the active tab by a shape or weight too, not by its colour alone), the sheet (`--sheet-max-width`, `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, `--duration-sheet`, `--color-sheet-grabber`, `--sheet-grabber-width`/`-height`), the list rows (`--list-row-min-height`, `--list-group-title-top`) and the dialog values that were raw in `dialog.scss` (`--dialog-max-width`, `-wide`, `--dialog-close-inset`, `--dialog-close-size`, `--blur-backdrop`). There are no top bar tokens: Jakub chose variant E on 2026-11-05 07:43 (no fixed bar at the top; the logo only in the corner of the landing photo and the sign-in button or avatar at the top right, both scrolling away), and what the app shell still needs there (the back button one level deeper) waits for its mockups in 2.23. `check-themes.mjs` requires the tab bar colours as literal `#rrggbb` (the glass colours also as `color-mix(in srgb, #rrggbb N%, transparent)`) and fails when, over a white page (the worst case for light labels), `--color-on-tab-bar` on the bar or `--color-tab-active` on the pill over the bar falls below 4.5:1, or the focus ring on the bar below 3:1, in the top-level `:root` and in every media query that sets one of them. Fonts: Playfair Display and Inter as self-hosted variable WOFF2 (latin and latin-ext from Fontsource, OFL licences beside them in `src/themes/nova-nadeje/fonts/`); the build hashes them into `/media/`, which nginx caches as immutable. Type sizes, weights, line heights, letter spacing, the spacing scale (`--space-unit` × n) and transition durations are tokens there too; `src/styles/_breakpoints.scss` holds the named widths and the `bp.down`/`bp.up` mixins (on the include path, so components write `@use 'breakpoints' as bp;`), and the named heights with `bp.height-down` (`short` 500 px, a phone on its side, and `hero-lead` 680 px, an iPhone SE upright, where the landing hero gives way); `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`BREAKPOINTS`, `downQuery(name)`, `HEIGHTS`, `heightDownQuery(name)`) holds the same widths and heights for code, kept equal by `scripts/breakpoints-parity.test.mjs` (run by `npm run lint`), and `core/layout/soft-keyboard.ts` (`SoftKeyboard`, root, started by the first component that asks: the tab bar, a dialog or a form field) counts the on-screen keyboard open while a text field has the focus and `visualViewport` is at least 150 px shorter than the window (`isOpen()`, `visibleArea()`), and then marks the root `keyboard-open` with `--keyboard-covered-height` (iOS Safari ignores `interactive-widget`, measured on iOS 27.2); `core/layout/compact-layout.ts` (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, root) follows `matchMedia(downQuery('nav'))`, the width of `bp.down(nav)`, false where the browser has no `matchMedia`, and keeps its answer while the window is 1 px wide or less (Chromium reports a 1 × 1 viewport for a moment while it captures a page beyond its viewport, and under load the screen spec's full-page shots caught the phone layout it set for that frame), so a page renders the compact list or the table with `@if`, never both hidden by CSS (2.22; used from 2.25). `core/layout/short-viewport.ts` (`ShortViewport.isShort()`, root) follows `matchMedia(heightDownQuery('short'))` the same way, for what a phone on its side cannot fit (the landing hero's actions). `src/styles/ui/` holds the shared building blocks (global `ui-` classes for page head, card, notice and load state, badge, button, table, form errors and segmented choices, the title, lead and points of a confirmation dialog (`_dialog`: the restore, unsaved changes and key withdrawal dialogs), the list of people with a round portrait, name, a line under it, badges and actions (`_person-list`: the people of the foundation and the administrators), the list rows of the app layout (`_list`, 2.22, unused until 2.25, which also settles the rest of the block: a group is `section > h3.ui-list__group-title + ul.ui-list` (a title inside an `li` does not stick), a second action sits beside its row as `__item` with `__action`, rows take grid areas so a row without a lead or value keeps no empty gap, the value moves under the title at large text, and rows get `scroll-margin-top` so focus is not hidden under a sticky title), the standalone panel pages, and the public site's section width, eyebrow, page intro band and calls to action, list in `frontend.md`), used by every page of the administration, the standalone pages (password reset, e-mail verification, connected application, first setup), the sign-in dialog, the cards of `/darce` and the public pages (home sections, news and project detail, privacy policy, not found, footer); their SCSS is linted with the components. `npm run lint:styles` fails on a raw colour, `font-family`, font size, weight, line height, letter spacing, `border-radius`, shadow, `px` spacing (except 1 px), transition duration or `width` media query outside the theme.
- `public/images/`: hero in 1024/1536 px with `srcset` (the source photo is 1536 px wide, so wider screens scale it up), `sizes="max(100vw, 150vh, 900px)"` and `fetchpriority="high"`; about and donate in 720/1440 px; project and news cards at 600 px; every photo below the hero has `loading="lazy"` written before `src`/`srcset` (Angular sets attributes in template order) plus `width`/`height`, so photos hidden on phones are never downloaded there. Convert a new photo with Pillow or `cwebp` at quality about 78; no file is kept that no template names.
- Icons: `favicon.svg` is the white star of the logo (its traced outline, PR #228) in a flat circle of the logo blue `#005dab` (viewBox 1024 wide, Jakub 2026-10-30); the face (`g.face`) stays at every size, also at 16 px (Jakub 2026-10-30; `e2e/home-assets.spec.ts`). `favicon.ico` holds 16, 32 and 48 px renders of it. The app icon is Jakub's white tile with the blue star outline of the logo (2026-10-30, the PWA icon of `nfnn-podklady/logo/ikona-pwa/` in project files): `app-icon.svg` is the tile with rounded corners (`rx` 230 of 1024) for the ZimaOS app list (ZimaOS shows SVG, Jakub 2026-10-30, so there is no PNG of it); `apple-touch-icon.png` is 180 px on opaque RGB, the white square without rounded corners because iOS rounds them itself (iOS takes no SVG). The cloud ImageMagick has no SVG renderer: render with Playwright's Chromium (`omitBackground`) and pack with `convert 16.png 32.png 48.png favicon.ico`.
- Web app manifest (plan v3 2.4): each theme carries `assets/manifest.webmanifest`, linked from `index.html` (`<link rel="manifest">`), with `id` and `start_url` `/aplikace`, `scope` `/`, `display` `standalone`, `lang` `cs`, the name "Nová naděje – nadační fond" and the short name "NFNN" under the icon, `theme_color` the theme's `--color-accent` and `background_color` its `--color-surface`, and the icons `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png` (`any`) and `icon-maskable-512.png` (`maskable`, the star inside the safe circle), Jakub's PWA icon of `nfnn-podklady/logo/ikona-pwa/` (the control theme carries copies, since every theme carries the same file names). `scripts/check-themes.mjs` checks each theme's manifest: those fields, both colours equal to the tokens of the top-level `:root`, and every icon a PNG in `assets/` whose real size is the one its `sizes` names, with a 192 and a 512 px `any` icon and a 512 px `maskable` one.

## Tests

Specs beside the code; `src/test-setup.ts` gives jsdom `showModal()`/`close()`, `src/test-providers.ts` the `cs` locale, `FakeLiveUpdates` in place of `LiveUpdates` and no tab locks or session channel (`TAB_LOCKS` null, `SESSION_CHANNEL` a factory of null), so no spec opens a real `BroadcastChannel`; specs of the tabs override them with `FakeTabLocks` and `FakeSessionChannel` from `core/auth/testing/fake-session-tabs.ts`, and `e2e/session-tabs.spec.ts` (Chromium only, because WebKit's request interception shows no `Cookie` header) runs two tabs over one rotating refresh cookie and a refresh whose answer never arrives. Shared cases (`.claude/rules/testing.md`, Logic on both sides): the Vitest global setup `src/testing/shared-cases-setup.ts` (`vitest.config.mts`) reads `../shared-cases` in Node and `src/testing/shared-cases.ts` hands specs frozen, schema-checked cases (`sharedCases(file)`); `donation-amount.spec.ts` runs the Czech receipt amounts through `formatMinorUnits`. `core/i18n/plural-category.ts`: `pluralCategory(count)` → `one` | `few` | `other` for text built in TypeScript. Every e2e spec imports `test` from `e2e/fixtures.ts`, whose automatic fixture answers an unmocked `/api` request with a 404 problem. `e2e/devices/` opens the main pages, dialogs and menus in the poses of `poses.ts` (iPhones upright, on their side and with large text, iPads, a Pixel), checks each layout from its boxes and keeps a screen of each in `test-results/device-report/` (`.claude/rules/testing.md`, Phones and tablets); what it finds today is listed in `known-findings.ts`. Playwright in `e2e/` by journey (`chunk-load-recovery.spec.ts`, through `LostChunks` in `lost-chunks.ts`, answers 404 for every chunk the first page did not load, until a given document request or for good: the deploy path where the automatic load shows the page, the page of its own when that load fails too, an address with a fragment, a throwing `sessionStorage` (no automatic load, the bar), `context.setOffline` (no automatic load), chunks whose requests are cut off while `navigator.onLine` stays true (`cutOffEveryChunkNotLoadedYet`: the probe fails, so no automatic load and the bar) and a dirty news editor left for a lost chunk (one question in the page, no `beforeunload` prompt, the list loads from the server); `accessibility.spec.ts` runs axe over the bar and the page of its own, `accessibility-keyboard.spec.ts` checks at 320, 390 and 1280 px that the bar hides neither the focused control nor the end of the page, and `theme.spec.ts` covers both states; `site-metadata.spec.ts` reads `robots.txt` and the sitemap and checks the canonical link, `og:url` and the preview image on a page): `donor-session.ts` holds the sample donor and `administrator` (the same donor with the `ADMIN` role), `signIn(page, user?)`, `session(status, user?)`, `openSignInDialog`, `donationItem(overrides)` and `bankAccountItem(overrides)` (the API's full item shapes) and `mockDonorApi` (payment details (from `shared-cases/payment/details.json`), summary and list, bank check, empty bank account list; with `receipts` `receipt-issuing.ts` answers `POST /api/donations/me/receipts` with an issued receipt made from a case of `shared-cases/receipts/v1/frozen.json`, and the synthetic signature and portrait by their digests, so the real worker draws the PDF), which every donor spec registers first so nothing escapes to the dev proxy; `qr-matrices.ts` reads the payment matrices (level H) the browser must draw from the shared cases `shared-cases/qr/payment.json` and the payment details the mocks serve from `payment/details.json`, by case name (found from the file's own folder, `__dirname`, since Playwright loads the specs as CommonJS, where `import.meta` is a syntax error), and `drawnModulesMatch(qr, matrix)` compares the drawn dots with one; `second-factor.spec.ts` decodes the drawn authenticator QR with jsQR back to `otpauthUri` (the backend sends no matrix since 1.4); the automatic fixture of `e2e/fixtures.ts` serves the details and fails any journey that asks for another `/api/donations/payment-*` path, and `donate-qr.spec.ts` checks the same dots for 500 and 1 000 Kč, one request in all, the redraw after typing and the saved PNG; `password-reset.spec.ts` covers the dialog's reset tab and the page; `email-verification.spec.ts` the confirmation page, and `donor-account.spec.ts` the registration's "check your e-mail" panel and the resend button; `donor-dashboard.spec.ts` also the password and e-mail change forms; `connect-app.spec.ts` the consent page (sign-in on the page, the consent for claude.ai with the fresh code and the return with the authorization code, a used code refused and a new one accepted, a plain donor declining, an incomplete request, `mockConnectApi` answers the client description and swallows `https://claude.ai/**`) and the connections in the account card (`claudeConnection`, "Odpojit"); `older-symbol.spec.ts` answers the older-symbol question both ways; `admin-administrators.spec.ts` the administrators page, `admin-donors.spec.ts` the donor search and card, `admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts` the older-symbol answers, `admin-actions.spec.ts` the history, `admin-news.spec.ts` the news, `admin-projects.spec.ts` the projects, `content-pages.spec.ts` the home page with published content, both detail pages and an unknown slug (`mockDonorApi` answers `/api/content/home` with empty lists, so every other journey sees the placeholders); `admin.spec.ts` covers the administration (the link from the overview, the import state and its outage, the retry, a remembered session, a donor without the role sent to the overview, a 403 after the role was taken away, a visitor sent to the sign-in dialog); `passkeys.spec.ts`, `second-factor.spec.ts`, `add-bank-account.spec.ts`, `contact-form.spec.ts` (also copying the IČO and the IBAN from "Údaje pro účetní") and `privacy-policy.spec.ts` cover those journeys, `device-copy.spec.ts` the device copy in Chromium and WebKit (`mockDonorSync` of `donor-session.ts` serves the manifest and chunks; the password wrap made by the Argon2id worker under the production headers, the raw database scanned for the donor's gifts, e-mail and password, the copy kept over a reload and a sign-out, "Načíst data znovu" and the deletion with the database gone), `receipt-pdf.spec.ts` the receipt drawn in the browser (through `receipt-donations.ts`: the progress line while the data is held and the saved PDF read back with `pdf-reader.ts`, a worker chunk gone after a deploy with the one reload that resumes the download, still gone after it, an emulated iPhone with `canShare` and the second tap, the focus kept on the pressed button through the drawing and the save, and the 120-gift year whose median warm drawing in the worker stays within 1 s at CPU 1×, with the cold and warm times and their ×4 values as annotations (`system-overview.md`, receipt PDF)), `verify-receipt.spec.ts` the verification page (through `receipt-verification.ts`, which reads the proofs from `shared-cases/verification/payload.json`, serves or keeps a key list, cuts the network after load with `context.setOffline` and takes WebCrypto's Ed25519 away: the signed result and the only requests that leave, the status answers that are not green, a yearly receipt, a changed amount, a tampered key list, a revoked key, a cut-off link, both signature paths, the kept keys offline, no keys offline and the retry, an unknown key offline, the server unreachable while online), and the other specs are named with their page above; downloads use `Promise.all([page.waitForEvent('download'), click])` and `download.suggestedFilename()`; `accessibility.spec.ts` runs axe on every page and dialog.

## Not implemented yet

English translations (there is no translation file and no `extract-i18n` target yet, and no locale is configured, so `LOCALE_ID` is `en-US`; every string already carries an `@@id`).
